Chapter 1101: Scheming Against The True Immortal In Secret

"Right, I also retained the memory of destroying her body before, and when creating her new body this time, I added it in..."

"You should have seen her expression when she first woke up."

Master Qiaogong grew more excited as he spoke. With the gradual completion of the source power essence buffer zone's construction, the surroundings became progressively brighter. This allowed him to clearly see the gazes directed at him from those around. Thus, he became even more smug.

"I should have thought of it earlier. Since you can copy and back up your own consciousness, preserving consciousness backups of other Heavenly Mechanism Realm cultivators shouldn't be an issue either." Li Fan suddenly appeared, his indifferent tone causing the Master Qiaogong to stiffen instantly.

"The cultivators of the Heavenly Mechanism Realm are absolute geniuses in artifact refinement. The Federation of All Worlds is in dire need of talent right now; how can you hoard it?" Li Fan's gaze turned hostile.

Master Qiaogong could only manage an awkward smile.

"How many others' consciousness backups do you have over there? Hand them all over, and I'll let it slide. Otherwise..."

Master Qiaogong felt waves of killing intent emanating from Li Fan, causing his heart to race with fear. He even sensed his neck gripped tightly by a pair of invisible giant hands, nearly suffocating him. Moreover, this constricting sensation was intensifying.

He felt like a bug trapped in a web, utterly unable to break free and only able to await death in vain. Master Qiaogong knew this was the power of the True Immortal of Salvation and Mortal Compassion Compassionate. After reciting the oath to join the federation, he had already fallen into the True Immortal's net. Unless he surpassed it in strength, simply through this connection, Li Fan, as the superior emmisary, could erase him with a single thought.

Fortunately, Li Fan had no real intention of killing him there. Just as the Master Qiaogong's consciousness began to blur, the constricting sensation vanished in an instant.

Regaining his new lease on life, the Master Qiaogong gasped for air, wiped the cold sweat from his forehead, and hurriedly explained: "Just the core few from the Elder Council, that's all."

"I fled in such haste back then; I could only take this much."

"The darkness power is profoundly mysterious; when the time comes, release them and let us study it together," Li Fan said indifferently.

Master Qiaogong naturally had no choice but to agree. Having been frightened by Li Fan once, the Master Qiaogong behaved much more obediently afterward.

Three days later, the golden buffer zone was fully constructed. Gazing at the thick darkness in the distance, now completely barred outside, everyone could not help but sigh once more at the divine might of the Compassionate True Immortal. Though the darkness power was formidable, it could not contend with a True Immortal.

Even with the buffer measures in place, this darkness power still carried a risk of going out of control. Li Fan had no intention of bringing it back to the Spiritwood Realm for study. It was merely in this Dark Realm that another research site had been newly established.

The four elders of the Heavenly Mechanism Realm had all been released by Qiaogong. Only then did Li Fan learn that the stunningly beautiful female immortal puppet from before was actually Shangguan Tuo, the former chief elder of the Heavenly Mechanism Realm whom Qiao Gong had mentioned.

Shangguan Tuo had once sought to fully control the [Ancestor], so she took a desperate gamble by fusing her divine soul into the fragmented core of the [Ancestor]. Naturally, she had prepared a consciousness backup beforehand. However, her reckless action nearly caused the collapse of the entire Heavenly Mechanism Realm. It could be called a grave sin.

Thus, the members of the elder council at the time rejected the proposal to revive Shangguan Tuo. They merely kept her consciousness backup deeply hidden. In the end, before the destruction of the Heavenly Mechanism Realm, Qiaogong had brought it out.

What Li Fan had not expected was that Shangguan Tuo was actually Qiaogong's mentor.

Qiaogong looked at the crowd's gazes, his expression showing no trace of abnormality. The other three elders of the Heavenly Mechanism Realm also remained silent regarding Qiaogong's act, which could be called betraying one's master and destroying one's ancestors.

After Li Fan asked Shangguan Tuo if she sought freedom, he received a negative response. This could not help but pique Li Fan's curiosity. His gaze flicked toward Qiaogong, who immediately shivered and hurriedly explained.

"This truly has little to do with me."

"They are merely consciousness backups, after all. To avoid the situation of two 'me's killing each other, this backup was suppressed in subjective initiative and cannot be fully equated to a true personality. Only the chief elder herself knows the method to unlock the restrictions."

"This is also a protective measure that our Heavenly Mechanism Realm implemented to prevent anyone from mass-adopting consciousness backups and endangering world security."

"As for myself, with the Heavenly Mechanism Realm gone, there are naturally no such concerns."

A hint of undisguised pride crept into Qiaogong's tone.

"So, they are second-class citizens of the Heavenly Mechanism Realm. Thus, they must obey you, the superior one?" Fang Zaiji stroked his chin and asked.

Qiaogong nodded. But upon seeing Li Fan's expression, he immediately patted his chest and guaranteed: "Please rest assured, Superior Emmisary. Although they consider their status one level below mine, their research talents are no different from the original. With the few of us working together, researching the uses of this darkny power should not be difficult."

"Then, I leave it to you."

Li Fan had no interest in meddling in the Heavenly Mechanism Realm's internal affairs. As long as they could serve his purposes, that was enough. After giving his instructions, Li Fan returned alone to the Dark Realm.

Alone, immersed in the darkness. Li Fan felt no fear in his heart; he simply quietly sensed his surroundings.

"As I thought. In the darkness, even the influence of the Faceless True Immortal is greatly weakened."

"Although it cannot fully free me, for me, it is practically equivalent to a sanctuary."

"In the depths of the You Realm, I can activate [Truth] at any time and return."

With this thought, the long-standing threat weighing heavily on Li Fan's heart finally lightened considerably.

"But blindly fleeing is useless."

"In this life, with so much preparation, I have great confidence in safely withdrawing amid the clash between the Faceless True Immortal and the Immortal Ruins Immortal."

"How exactly should I choose?"

For once in this life, Li Fan calmed down and pondered alone. The darkness, like an umbrella shielding everything, made Li Fan's heart relax in a way it never had before.

"The power of the Faceless True Immortal lingers eternally in the Xuanhuang Realm; it is not something I can avoid simply by not provoking it."

"Once I reach a certain realm, it will inevitably draw His attention. This is an unchangeable fact."

"Unless I have already found a safe way to escape the Xuanhuang Realm."

"But beyond the High Walls, True Immortals still exist."

"There is no escape..."

A sharp glint flashed in Li Fan's eyes.

"When facing a True Immortal, sometimes one's own weakness is an advantage."

"It allows careful observation while the other ignores you."

Li Fan looked again at the profound darkness around him.

"Like this patch of blackness. If something exists in the darkness, staring at me, yet its strength is far inferior to mine and poses no threat at all..."

"Then how could I even detect it?"

Chapter 1102: All Essences Gathered In One Body

The research on darkness power by the people of the Heavenly Mechanism Realm was not as smooth as imagined.

It made sense, after all; this was a power level comparable to the source power essence. No matter how genius they were, completing research from nothing would inevitably require a vast amount of time.

Fortunately, Master Qiaogong and the others were all research fanatics; since starting the study, they had been utterly focused and completely immersed in it. All Li Fan needed to do was wait patiently and then reap the benefits.

During this period, Li Fan had already quietly begun plotting against the two Immortals.

In the Spiritwood Realm, within an underground secret chamber. Over a hundred puppets of bizarre designs stood silently motionless.

Li Fan, with a serious expression, personally inscribed formations onto them.

"Bait puppets: every two form a pair, totaling seventy-two pairs. In each pair, one flies into the Immortal Ruins, while the other is placed in various corners of the Xuanhuang Realm's earth veins. When the seventy-two bait puppets head toward the Immortal Ruins, the grand formation on them will activate, drawing the attention of the Immortal Ruins Immortal."

"This formation, derived from the Final Dissociation Disc, is infinitely close to Immortal-grade grand formation level. It is named [Bewildering Haze, Disordered Blossoms Gradually Enchanting]. Suddenly sensing a threat during seclusion would surely provoke an instinctive reaction, tracing back to the Xuanhuang Realm."

"As for the bait puppets left in the Xuanhuang Realm, I will use the Faceless True Immortal's source power essense to set up a warning net around them. Once the Immortal Ruins Immortal's power triggers it, it will draw the Faceless True Immortal's gaze."

"If nothing unexpected occurs... Once these two unimaginable forces collide, the Xuanhuang Realm will likely vanish into ash in an instant. Therefore, my feigned ploy against the Heavenly Dao to extract the Xuanhuang's impending transformation must be prepared in advance."

In his mind, the Dao Deviation Stone emitted flashing blue light. The sublimated Dao Deviation Stone's derivation ability had been greatly enhanced. This act of driving away the wolf only to invite the tiger involved countless facets. Even with myriad threads and frequent variables, the Dao Deviation Stone bore no significant burden.

Of course, this was merely because, in the derivation, the Faceless True Immortal and the Immortal Ruins Immortal only possessed the abilities Li Fan had preset in advance.

Li Fan was well aware of his limitations; with his current strength, attempting to derive a True Immortal would be nothing short of a fool's dream. What he could do was seek a slim chance of victory based on the realities he had already experienced.

"The Immortal Ruins Immortal shakes the True Immortal's web."

"I then seize the moment to activate [Truth] and break free in one stroke."

"Of course, this is the ideal scenario. As the saying goes, plan for defeat before victory; I must also consider if my plan fails, and the gap in strength between the Immortal Ruins Immortal and the Faceless True Immortal is too vast, insufficient to shake the True Immortal's web's bindings..."

"Then I must immediately return to the depths of the Dark Realm, activate [Truth], and escape the crisis."

"Now, I have grasped a few things that can at least slightly influence a True Immortal; delaying for a moment is still feasible."

Li Fan examined the state outside his divine soul.

The silver glow protection gifted by the Heavenly Doctor, the golden essence of source power, and the seven-colored divine light wrapped around the gray mold spots... Various powers maintained a delicate balance.

Probably only someone like Li Fan could remain fully composed, even feeling a subtle sense of security, in such a state.

"Using force against force, maneuvering through alliances and rifts. At its root, it is still because my own strength is too weak. Yet the threshold to Immortality, how could it be so easily crossed?"

"Genius figures like the Xuantian King, the Immortal Ancestor, and the Heavenly Doctor are all barred outside, unable to advance an inch."

"How much more so for me? If not for possessing [Truth], I would likely have long abandoned this seemingly impractical fantasy," Li Fan said with a slight self-mocking smile.

"But since I have the fortune to encounter the wondrous object [Truth], no one can stop my path to ascension..." A chill flashed in his eyes.

Li Fan knew that whether [Chaos], [Hunger], or [Nothingness], what he had seen were not the original versions. But clumsy imitations by later generations.

Thus, Li Fan had never given up on his in-depth research into these three True Immortal characters script. Even a copy of a copy, the more closely it imitates, the stronger its power becomes.

Even Immortals can comprehend the Dao through one of its characters. It can be said that any one of these three, for Li Fan, would take a lifetime of effort to fully comprehend.

Li Fan's comprehension is limited. But he can borrow from external sources.

Besides the unparalleled talent of his clone Li Qing, in the [Mountain River Pavilion], the Xuanhuang Realm prodigies who had recently been successively coaxed and turned over also became Li Fan's resources.

The Xuantian Sect technique obtained from Senior Sister Zhao, The Heaven and Earth Converging Manual, can equalize talent and comprehension with any target.

After achieving minor success with it, clone Li Qing began selecting suitable targets. Outwardly proclaimed as sending them out to execute secret missions of the True Immortal of Salvation and Mortal Compassion, but in reality, they had already been secretly controlled, their talents extracted.

Of course, due to the convergence and equalization, these prodigies also received half of Li Qing's talent enhancement.

Li Qing's talent was no weaker than theirs, so the Mountain River Pavilion prodigies, including Shang Tianci, noticed no abnormalities. They only marveled at the wondrous nature of the [Foundation Establishment Through Oneself] method.

With the talent enhancement from over ten prodigies, clone Li Qing's comprehension had reached a shocking, unprecedented level. He even sensed that the main body Li Fan had excised part of his memories.

But after all, he was merely a clone; there was no distinction of you and me between him and the main body. He steadfastly carried out the task of comprehending the True Immortal script characters.

The three True Immortal script characters continually floated in his mind, as if alive. Before Li Qing's eyes, it was as if he saw a blurry figure, the act of transforming the Dao into characters.

"Inscribing the Dao..."

"A being even more tyrannical than the Faceless True Immortal. Perhaps that is my ultimate goal."

...

While Li Fan secretly prepared for the final grand plan of this world.

The Heavenly Sovereign of Reversing Life and Death, who had previously appeared out of nowhere, finally demonstrated his influence on the Xuanhuang Realm.

Because people discovered that, ever since this Heavenly Sovereign descended, no new offspring had been born among the Xuanhuang Realm cultivators.

Or rather, the entire human race was the same.

At first, people could not be certain it was this Heavenly Sovereign's influence. But when a previously fallen cultivator returned in a way no one had ever imagined, everyone instantly connected everything.

The Lancang River, the largest river in the southwestern frontier of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

One day, a cultivator named Changji suddenly emerged naked from the river water. This bizarre scene naturally drew onlookers from the surroundings.

But Changji paid no heed; instead, he looked at his body with ecstasy and disbelief, muttering to himself: "Am I alive again?"

"Reversing life and death, reversing life and death; the Heavenly Sovereign did not deceive me!"

Previous

Next

Chapter 1103: Xuanhuang Revival River

Changji ignored the strange looks from the crowd and respectfully performed a grand salute toward the heavens. Then he transformed into a streak of escaping light and flew away.

The words he spoke before leaving immediately caught the attention of observant cultivators. After some investigation, people finally discovered that this Changji should have died completely a year ago. During the exploration of a wild sect ruin, he had unfortunately triggered a protective formation and ended up with his body completely destroyed on the spot. Over a dozen companions who were with him at the time could testify; it could not be faked!

Yet now, this Changji had actually come back to life! The location of his revival happened to be the nearest great river to the site of his fall! This somewhat terrifying discovery spread rapidly throughout the entire Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance in an instant.

Soon, Changji's friends from before his death came knocking one after another, wanting to confirm the truth of his resurrection. Changji himself did not deny it and openly admitted that he had indeed revived.

"Thanks to the divine power of the Heavenly Sovereign!" he even said devoutly in front of everyone.

In the past, when cultivators in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance mentioned the Heavenly Sovereign, they inevitably meant the Immortal Ancestor. But at this moment, coming from Changji's mouth, everyone knew he referred to that Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign who had quietly reversed life and death.

Seeing that his relatives and friends were still somewhat skeptical, Changji directly invited everyone into his Tianxuan Mirror space. It was well known that the Tianxuan Mirror space was bound to the cultivator and could only be opened by the cultivator themselves. With the Tianxuan Mirror as proof, everyone finally completely believed in Changji's true identity.

Death and revival, and it was by no means an isolated case. This shocking news quickly became known to all. In the following days, the appearance of more and more revived cultivators served as further verification of its authenticity.

After summarization, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance found that without exception, revived cultivators reappeared from the nearest great river to their death site. In the territories of the Immortal Alliance, there were a total of fifty-four Revival Rivers. As for the Five Elders Association side, it seemed there were also corresponding fifty-four. These one hundred and eight rivers in total formed the Revival Rivers of the Xuanhuang Realm.

The time required for cultivators to revive seemed related to their strength before death. The higher the cultivation realm, the longer the revival time needed. The first batch of revived cultivators were without exception only at Foundation Establishment or below. As for how much time higher-realm cultivators needed to revive, that would require gradual verification in the coming days.

After revival, they always appeared naked. Items carried on the body before death, even those stored in the divine soul, did not appear with them. But to pick up a life was already a great fortune. No one paid much attention to these external possessions.

Revived cultivators seemed to match their pre-death age. However, after detailed measurements, it was found that the reborn body appeared one year younger than before the fall. It was not only cultivators; as long as they were living beings in the Xuanhuang Realm, upon death they would revive from the river waters. Thus, people could often see the astonishing sight of creatures continuously emerging from the waters onto the banks of the Revival Rivers.

There seemed to be some miraculous power in the river waters protecting the newly revived beings, preventing them from drowning immediately upon revival. But this power disappeared quickly after leaving the Revival Rivers. Even if one reentered the waters, its trace could no longer be found.

...

The various wonders of death and revival became the hottest topic among Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance cultivators. Some rejoiced that from now on, there would be no more worries about falling in battle. While other cultivators began to worry that if no new lives were born, the future of the Xuanhuang Realm might not look optimistic.

Fortunately, a discovery soon dispelled this concern among the people. In certain prefectures of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, new lives were still being born since the Heavenly Sovereign reversed the principles of life and death. It was just that the numbers were extremely few; across the entire Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, it did not exceed fifty per year.

But at least the nurturing of life had not completely stopped. And even if life would not disappear entirely from now on, an increase of fifty cultivators per year was not a big issue for the Xuanhuang Realm.

Soon, the cultivators' attention shifted to other key questions. For example, after dying once, could one still revive? How much time would a second revival take? Was there truly no limit to the number of times? Did the one-year age reduction per death have an upper limit?

...

That extremely mysterious Heavenly Sovereign had never stepped forward to explain from beginning to end. Everything had to be explored by the cultivators themselves. This would soon become a guideline that must be followed, so the cultivators naturally attached great importance to it.

Soon, under the orders of certain existences, some cultivators who had just been resurrected not long ago were once again forced to sacrifice their lives in pursuit of the mysteries of resurrection. Fortunately, knowing they could die and be reborn, with the promise of generous rewards, these cultivators treated as test subjects had few complaints.

They had already died once anyway. After death, they had no conscious awareness at all. One blink of the eyes, and upon opening them, they were good as new. They could even obtain rewards that they might not earn in a lifetime of living; why not seize the opportunity?

The secrets of resurrection would likely take some time to fully unravel.

After hearing the news spread, Li Fan personally went to the bank of one of the resurrection rivers to investigate for a long while. In the end, he still could not discern any clues.

"Compared to the Rebirth Mansion I experienced before, this era's reversal of life and death is clearly much more perfected."

"It can even nurture life out of thin air from the river..."

"Truly inconceivable."

The principle of reversing life and death had thoroughly fused with the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, making it impossible to discern separately. Even Li Fan, proficient in formations, could not glimpse any traces of the [Great Reversal of Life and Death Formation] he had once seen.

Worried that this inexplicably appeared Heavenly Sovereign might affect his grand plan. Li Fan quietly contacted Dharma-Transmitter Zhou, trying to glean some inside information from her. To his disappointment, Dharma-Transmitter Zhou gave the same response as before: she knew nothing about it.

"As far as I know, your Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance has an organization called the Heavenly Oversight Division, specifically monitoring the principles of the Xuanhuang heaven and earth. This Heavenly Sovereign has succeeded in reversing the principles for so long, yet you insist you are completely unaware... Fellow Daoist Zhou, this does not add up," Li Fan said in an unfriendly tone, questioning her.

Dharma-Transmitter Zhou smiled: "Your Federation of All Worlds truly has swift intelligence."

"Even the confidential existence of the Immortal Alliance's Heavenly Oversight Division cannot be hidden from you. But I am not brushing you off; I truly know nothing about this."

The smile on Zhou's face gradually faded, and her expression turned serious. "In general, for a Dao Integration cultivator to succeed in reversing principles, it is a gradual process."

"During this process, the traces of the heaven and earth principles being twisted and altered are quite obvious; this is why the Heavenly Oversight Division can detect signs of a Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign about to be born."

"But this Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign is different."

"The speed of his appearance... Was a bit too fast."

Previous

Next

Chapter 1104: Heaven And Man Follow Different Paths

"From the first signs of the reversal of heaven and earth's principles to the successful reversal and attainment of longevity, the entire process took no more than ten years."

"Under normal circumstances, a Dao Integration cultivator would not even complete the first step of reversing principles, yet this one has already achieved ascension."

"I almost suspect that this Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign is a survivor from the outside world, already a great cultivator very close to the Longevity Immortal realm. That would explain how everything went so smoothly." Dharma-Transmitter Zhou's tone carried a hint of horror.

"An outsider?" Li Fan fell into inexplicable contemplation upon hearing his words.

"But fortunately, this Heavenly Sovereign seems to have no intention of intervening in the Xuanhuang Realm's conflicts. Neither we nor the Five Elders Association side has seen Him make any proactive contact. As for the effects brought by reversing life and death, we are still assessing them. However, it is foreseeable that both sides will become increasingly cautious about launching wars in the future." Dharma-Transmitter Zhou sighed with some emotion.

Li Fan nodded in agreement. Do not be fooled into thinking that reversing life and death, with infinite resurrection, means endless manpower. The key issue lies in the time required for resurrection. Meanwhile, the Xuanhuang Realm's own rate of nurturing new life has been weakened to the extreme.

Once major casualties occur, it would take a very long recovery window to slowly rebuild. Moreover, as the number of resurrections increases, the time required for each revival is estimated to grow longer. In the short term, there would be no major issues. But if this continues indefinitely...

Li Fan suddenly spoke: "Fellow Daoist Zhou need not worry excessively. If I remember correctly, does your Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance not have many cultivators suppressed and eternally slumbering? In a critical moment, releasing them could serve as an emergency measure."

Li Fan was referring to the cultivators suppressed by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance within the Eternal Immortal Wall, including those whose bodies had been occupied by the million gods and demons he previously summoned using the [Three Disasters and Hundred Tribulations Divine Clarity Method]. Though they had largely lost their self-awareness and were reduced to empty shells, they could still barely serve as cannon fodder.

Dharma-Transmitter Zhou gazed at Li Fan for a moment but did not respond. He simply ended the conversation with a polite smile.

"I always have a premonition about this Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign's identity..."

"Perhaps in the next life, we can test and verify it together. In this life, it would only add unnecessary variables."

Li Fan's eyes flickered as he returned to his preparations.

...

Time passed swiftly, and in the blink of an eye, it was Anchor year 24th.

"Because I previously did False Misconversion of the heaven, provoking the Heavenly Dao consciousness's fury, this life's Crimson Flame Incinerating Sea calamity has not only advanced by three years, but the intensity of the Crimson Flame has also increased further."

He narrowed his eyes, gazing at that crimson figure that seemed to dance with the wind. It was still a vague humanoid form, but unlike the detached figure cradling a flame he had seen before. In this life, the Crimson Flame sat cross-legged atop auspicious clouds conjured from flames, its eyes filled with killing intent as it stared down at the beings of Congyun. Coiled around its body was a serpentine creature like a roaming dragon, also fixating on the Congyun Sea below like a predator eyeing its prey.

Suddenly, it seemed to sense Li Fan's gaze and abruptly turned toward his position. But with the formations enveloping Li Fan's body, it was naturally impossible for them to detect him.

"An entirely new form I've never seen before; it seems that my gradual arrangements over these years have stirred unease and vigilance in the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao," Li Fan thought, a smile in his eyes. "But unfortunately, as long as I do not act, no one in this world can predict what I am truly scheming."

"All your resistance is in vain."

Li Fan did not halt the progression of the Crimson Flame Incinerating the Sea; he merely observed silently from the side.

In this life, he had no intention of drawing out the Ink Death. As the strongest among the Spirits of Heaven and Earth, once the Ink Death emerged, it would inevitably attract the attention of all the Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns in the world. Even though Li Fan no longer feared them now.

But the world-ending preparations he had made were not to be seen by the light of day. To avoid disrupting the plan of driving the tiger to swallow the wolf, maintaining a low profile for the time being was the wise course.

Li Fan had come to the Congyun Sea today precisely to observe the specific changes that would occur in the Xuanhuang Realm during its self-purification process. Having thoroughly studied the Heavenly Doctor's Record of Mending the Heavens, Li Fan had gained a completely new understanding of the Crimson Flame Incineration Sea event.

The Congyun Sea originated from the Heavenly Law Realm and did not belong to the Xuanhuang Realm's core. During the mutual fusion of the two realms, various "rejection" reactions were bound to occur. The most representative were the frequent bizarre incidents erupting across the Congyun Sea. The Crimson Flame's seemingly world-destroying actions were, in truth, to better facilitate the fusion of the Congyun Sea with the Xuanhuang Realm.

"The same event looks entirely different from the perspective of Human versus the perspective of the Heavenly Dao."

"There is no right or wrong."

As the mutated Crimson Flame slowly accumulated power, a layer of sealing force began to emerge along the entire boundary of the Congyun Sea, attempting to prevent living beings from escaping.

And in the final moment before this heavenly prison fully took effect, Li Fan spotted a group of people hastily sailing across the sea toward him. After finally reaching the shore, they hurriedly abandoned their ship and fled.

The leader was someone Li Fan recognized: Zhang Haobo!

In this life, Li Fan had no dealings with him. But relying on his own faint premonitions and his personal fortune, Zhang Haobo had still led his close friends and family to escape before the great calamity arrived.

"Haobo, we were living just fine on Liuli Island. Why did we have to leave our home and come to this unfamiliar continent?"

"Yes, boss. Although I definitely trust you and will follow you forever, I'm still really confused inside."

...

Faced with the doubts from those around him, Zhang Haobo's expression showed little fluctuation. He simply unfolded the map, confirmed their position, and calmly directed everyone to continue on their way.

As they walked, he said: "Haven't you noticed that the island seems to have fewer people lately? Ever since that last time when it was said that immortals from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance came for a visit. It's not just our island; other islands, including the immortals themselves, are quietly withdrawing from the Congyun Sea."

"Clearly, some major upheaval is about to happen," Zhang Haobo analyzed calmly. "Rather than stay in the Congyun Sea and wait for death, it's better to come here and take our chances."

The others, upon hearing this, still wore expressions of disbelief.

Some even couldn't help but retort: "But isn't it said now that under the great power of the Heavenly Sovereigns, everyone can be resurrected after death? If we can be revived, then what's there to fear?"

Zhang Haobo snorted coldly and offered no explanation.

"Those who wish to follow me, keep following. Those who don't, the ship is still at the seaside; you can return on your own at any time," he said indifferently.

The crowd fell silent upon hearing this, not daring to say another word.

Next

Chapter 1105: Xuanhuang Cycle Of Life And Death

Though doubts lingered, they held deep trust in Zhang Haobo, who had repeatedly led them through life-or-death crises amid world-shaking perils.

Li Fan watched them gradually depart in secret, falling into deep contemplation.

"Even among ordinary mortals, there are those like Zhang Haobo who sense impending calamity before it arrives."

"Much less the countless cultivators in the Xuanhuang Realm."

"It seems I may have been overly optimistic. Though all arrangements are sufficiently concealed, it cannot be guaranteed that someone favored by heaven will not discover anomalies through various coincidences and opportunities."

"If one wishes to cover up a matter, the best method is to use another matter to divert people's attention."

Li Fan's gaze flickered as an idea instantly formed in his mind.

For now, however, he would quietly watch the spectacle of Crimson Flame burning the sea to completion.

Not long after Zhang Haobo and the others left.

Crimson Flame, who had been sitting cross-legged in meditation, finally made his move.

The flame dragon coiled around his body revealed an excited gaze; in an instant, it transformed into countless streams of crimson flames, surging wildly toward every corner of the Congyun Sea.

The seawater heated up at an alarmingly terrifying speed, and fear spread through the hearts of the living beings who had yet to flee the sea.

Faced with desperation, some cultivators rose to resist.

But with the highest being only at the Soul Transformation realm, how could they oppose this enhanced version of Crimson Flame?

Li Fan's attention was not on the rapidly vanishing lives.

Instead, he silently observed the changes in heaven and earth itself.

"Rebirth amid destruction."

"Revival amid ruin."

The Crimson Flame sea-burning lasted a full three months.

The ocean dried up, the land scorched for a thousand miles, the vast sea transformed into a deep valley.

Yet Li Fan, who had witnessed every scene from beginning to end, faintly sensed the hidden vitality within.

"This is the Xuanhuang Cycle of Life and Death."

"Having comprehended this path, I can now advance to the Soul Transformation realm at any time. Moreover, just like the Great Five Elements Cave-Heaven in the Nascent Soul realm, once I advance, I will absolutely be the strongest among Soul Transformation cultivators."

"Only pity..."

"My ambitions extend beyond this."

Li Fan took a deep breath, forcibly suppressing the restless spiritual power within his body.

"The local cycles of life and death in the Xuanhuang Realm, how can they compare to the instinctive response of the entire world amid a world-ending crisis?"

Li Fan recalled the various insights he had gained over the years from studying the Mending Heaven Record.

The world is like a person.

When a person faces life-or-death desperation, they often erupt with power beyond their limits.

The world is the same.

In the instant a world-ending crisis descends, the Xuanhuang Realm's Heavenly Dao will undergo all sorts of profoundly mysterious self-evolutions in that moment, seeking a slim chance of survival.

The various changes born at that time will far surpass those during periods of normal, stable operation.

"If successful, my strength after advancing to Soul Transformation will exceed even my estimates."

"Therefore..."

"No one can stop me!"

Li Fan's eyes filled with indifference as he turned and left.

...

Spiritwood Realm.

Ou Daozi carefully examined the corpse before him.

"Incredible, simply incredible."

"This is truly forged by you, Lord Emmisary? If you had not told me personally, I would almost truly believe this to be an immortal's shed remnant."

"After all, the sensation it gives me is almost identical to when Shang Tian was by my side."

A corpse emitting faint golden light lay flat in mid-air.

Under the reflection of the golden glow, its facial features could not be discerned.

Yet the sacred aura it emanated filled Ou Daozi with both reverence and greed.

After hearing Ou Daozi's conclusion, Li Fan nodded in satisfaction.

This forged immortal remains was his trump card for stirring up chaos in the Xuanhuang Realm.

The corpse had been purchased with heavy gold: the impeccably preserved remains of a Dao Integration cultivator from thousands of years ago.

It was then meticulously refined with golden source power essence, infusing it with an aura surpassing the mortal realm.

Moreover, to make the disguise even more convincing.

Li Fan had extracted abilities from some exotic treasures fished from the pond earlier and implanted them into the corpse.

For instance, anyone approaching the remains would have their inner emotions infinitely amplified, nearly losing their reason.

Furthermore, upon being attacked, the remains would actively tear through space and flee.

When someone touched the remains, the hidden pure mutated spiritual energy within would surge madly into their body, instantly boosting their cultivation.

...

All of this was to make the performance as authentic as possible.

"Even a Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign would surely take interest in the legendary True Immortal remains, no?"

"Then let's make a big spectacle of it."

On this so-called True Immortal remains, Li Fan had added not only these tangible benefits but also some hidden tricks.

Guaranteed to give the final claimant a massive surprise.

With the Faceless True Immortal's power concealing it, those skilled in divination could not trace the corpse's origins.

After completing the final preparations, two pitch-black puppets entered and hoisted the "True Immortal remains."

They set off to carry out Li Fan's mission.

From appearance to departure, they moved in utter silence.

Even if Li Fan were not their controller, it would be hard to detect their movements.

These two puppets were the fruits of Heavenly Mechanism Realm's Master Qiaogong's labors over these years.

As expected of Heavenly Mechanism Realm's most outstanding talents: through relentless research, though they still could not grasp the essence of darkness power.

They had no trouble achieving practical applications to a certain degree.

The black puppets perfectly embodied the traits of darkness power.

They could vanish and appear from the "crevices" of the world, just like the darkness.

Divine sense could neither lock onto nor detect them.

They also exerted strong suppression over spiritual power.

Ordinary attacks could scarcely damage them.

Their sole drawback was limited destructive capacity: they could only handle covert infiltration tasks.

Moreover, the darkness' rejection of the Immortal Ruins meant these puppets could not operate long-term outside.

Every so often, they had to return to the Dark Realm to replenish darkness power.

In any case, Li Fan was quite satisfied with these black puppets.

Entrusting them with missions would leave virtually no flaws.

After sending Ou Daozi away, Li Fan entered a sealed space alone.

The light screen flickered with countless young faces.

Accompanying each image was a long string of details below, describing their personalities.

"There are millions of mortals in the small realms, but finding those with a pure child's heart like Mr. Bai, those of utmost sincerity, is no easy task."

"Especially since the Rebirth Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign's power has already influenced many small realms."

"In recent years, the number of newborns has clearly declined."

"No choice. We must settle for the next best thing."

"If innate qualities fall short, then shape them through acquired education."

Next

Chapter 1106: Madness For The Man-Made True Immortal

Collecting the information on these children was naturally to find candidates to succeed as the Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable.

Now that Li Fan knew all the answers to the trials, as long as he found individuals whose temperament and personality roughly matched, the chances of successfully succeeding in the end were quite high.

There was no need to demand completion in this lifetime.

First, select a few suitable candidates, then pick them out in the next lifetime and provide special training.

While Li Fan was using the Illusionary Dream Technique to conduct various tests on these children.

The grand play he had arranged to muddy the waters of the Xuanhuang Realm slowly began to unfold.

First came a rumor that spread like wildfire.

The once-sensational Ou Shangtian, who had drawn the pursuit of nearly the entire Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's cultivators, possessed two heaven-defying abilities, [Kun Qian Bone Hand] and [Book of Ten Thousand Deaths], because he had come into contact with a True Immortal's remnant body as a child.

The [Kun Qian Bone Hand] could reverse heaven and earth, causing an item to regress to a state from a period of time ago.

The [Book of Ten Thousand Deaths] could predict one's countless ways of death. Used properly, it could turn calamity into fortune and ward off evil.

For the name Ouyang Tian, the Alliance's cultivators had almost forgotten it.

But as soon as those two terrifying abilities were mentioned, it instantly stirred memories among the Alliance's cultivators.

Back then, Ouyang Tian, with cultivation below Nascent Soul, had persisted for nearly half a year under the chase of countless cultivators. With his actions, he proved the power of the [Kun-Qian Bone Hand] and [Book of Ten Thousand Deaths].

And these two powerful abilities were merely byproducts of the True Immortal's remnant body?

Then, if one could obtain this so-called True Immortal's remnant body intact, what kind of fortune would it bring?

The instant they heard this rumor, nearly all cultivators had this thought flash through their minds.

But talk of True Immortals was ethereal and vague.

The crowd treated it merely as an anecdotal legend and did not take it seriously.

Yet strangely, as time passed, the rumor did not die down. Instead, it grew increasingly intense.

Any discerning person could see that someone was fanning the flames behind the scenes.

Just as they prepared to investigate the mastermind.

An event occurred that caught everyone off guard.

That evening, a graceful white figure suddenly appeared in the Xuanhuang sky.

With his emergence, the entire Xuanhuang Realm trembled faintly in an instant.

And all cultivators who looked up at this figure felt an indescribable sensation surge in their hearts.

The figure lasted only a few breaths before vanishing abruptly.

But combined with the previously rampant rumor, the words "True Immortal's remnant body!" instinctively leaped into the minds of the cultivators.

That phenomenon covering the entire sky, and the faint tremor of the Xuanhuang Realm accompanying the white figure's appearance: these could not be faked!

"Even if it is not a True Immortal, it is absolutely a remarkable figure!"

As people buzzed with speculation about the true identity of the white figure.

In Tianling Prefecture, an endless black swamp suddenly surged from within a deep mountain, seeking to devour everything around it.

The moment cultivators saw this black, viscous substance, they knew it was a creation of the Spirit of Heaven and Earth [Black Swamp].

Faced with this existential crisis, the cultivators were not frightened but delighted; they did not flee but charged forward.

Most launched fearless assaults toward the black swamp.

On the contrary, if they happened to slay this Spirit of Heaven and Earth, it would truly be a step to the heavens!

Though the Spirit of Heaven and Earth with Dao Integration combat power was strong, many ants could bite an elephant to death!

Cultivators holding this mindset were truly not few.

Under their desperate struggle, the black swamp's expansion was actually halted.

Just as the cultivators rejoiced, thinking the black swamp had weakened. A figure radiating brilliant golden light suddenly flew out from the black swamp.

Before the crowd could react, it tore through the long sky and vanished without a trace.

And after that golden figure departed, the rampaging black swamp inexplicably retreated entirely into the depths underground.

"That is..."

"True Immortal's remnant body?"

In the stunned crowd, someone suddenly shouted in a trembling voice.

Boom!

After a moment of silence, the crowd erupted in an instant.

Because from that fleeting glimpse just now, that figure indeed bore a striking resemblance to the one that had appeared on the celestial canopy not long ago!

Moreover, merely its aura alone could suppress a Dao Integration Black Swamp...

After being stunned for a moment, the cultivators present all reacted and chased toward the spot where the golden light had vanished.

Only a few cultivators, after hesitating briefly, still launched an assault on the Black Swamp that had retreated deep into the mountain and gone dormant once more.

However, somewhat regrettably, the Black Swamp that had returned to its lair became even more ferocious and brutal than before.

It seemed that its strength had even increased substantially.

These cultivators without exception all perished here.

But even a total annihilation could not halt the cultivators' greed.

After all, everyone could resurrect!

Thus, cultivators who rushed over upon hearing the news to meet their deaths arrived in an endless stream.

...

The emergence of the Black Swamp and the True Immortal's corpse remnant body quickly spread throughout the entire Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

The golden figure that could incite the rampage of the Spirit of Heaven and Earth gradually drew the attention of more and more cultivators.

And next, wherever the True Immortal's corpse remnant went, without exception, a Spirit Heaven and Earth would inexplicably appear, seemingly intent on suppressing this corpse remnant.

No one had ever known that the Xuanhuang Realm actually concealed so many Spirit of Heaven and Earth.

For a time, the cultivators were utterly stunned by this grand spectacle.

Almost every few days, news would come of a new Spirit of Heaven and Earth emerging.

And the cultivators of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance who had long been stuck at the Soul Transformation realm all fell into a frenzy.

They could no longer sit still and set out to hunt the Spirits of Heaven and Earth they desired.

While more cultivators still focused their gaze on that True Immortal's corpse remnant.

The cultivators' desire for the True Immortal's corpse remnant reached its peak after a certain event occurred.

A mortal named Zhang Haobo, after luckily touching the True Immortal's corpse remnant that suddenly descended before him, instantly leaped from an ordinary mortal to Golden Core perfection!

And the wondrous item that allowed him to achieve Foundation Establishment was none other than a rib-like oddity artifact!

Unfortunately, the True Immortal's corpse remnant only lingered by his side for a moment before vanishing and fleeing once more.

Otherwise, for this Zhang Haobo to advance to the Soul Transformation stage in an instant would probably not be impossible!

After the crowd investigated Zhang Haobo's background and identity, they also ruled out the possibility that he was staging a fake act.

After all, the nearly hundred mortals by his side who had personally witnessed the miracle showed no issues in their memories!

"The True Immortal's corpse remnant must be seeking a fated person."

"That Ou Shangtian was like this, and so was this Zhang Haobo! But their fortune was insufficient, and in the end, they were not chosen by the True Immortal's corpse remnant."

"However, if one could ultimately obtain it..."

Chapter 1107: One Scheme To Hevoc The Heavens

True Immortal. The allure for cultivators is simply too fatally irresistible.

Ever since the Immortal path was severed, becoming an immortal became an unattainable dream for all cultivators.

But now, suddenly, a True Immortal's remnant body has appeared in the world.

Moreover, all signs indicate that this True Immortal's shed remnant body is very likely genuine!

At this point, no matter how much doubt lingers in their hearts, the choice every cultivator makes is to seize this remnant body first and deal with the rest later.

Even if it is not a True Immortal's remnant body, judging by the various anomalies it has triggered, the benefits to be gained would still be considerable!

As a result, cultivators from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance have all left their Heavenly Cities and are searching everywhere for traces of this True Immortal's remnant body.

Even many Five Elders Association cultivators who were shopping in Nine Mountains Prefecture have rushed over from thousands of miles away just to join in the excitement.

With the establishment and development of the Nine Mountains Free Trade Prefecture, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and the Five Elders Association have maintained peace for a long time.

Relations between the two sides have further eased; as long as they apply, cultivators from either side can visit the other for tourism and sightseeing.

It seems they intend to prove through this method that their side is more suitable for cultivators to survive.

However, there has yet to be any news of anyone switching allegiances.

The exchanges between the two sides remain at the most superficial level.

Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance cultivators are still largely wary of these Five Elders Association members.

While searching in groups for the True Immortal's remnant body, they try to keep their distance as a precaution.

"You think this True Immortal's remnant body might be some ploy from the Five Elders Association? Deliberately trying to throw us into chaos. We've all been too focused on business lately, and our vigilance against the Five Elders Association has been waning year by year. Sigh, this is no good at all."

"Indeed, who knows when they might suddenly strike again! The Five Elders Association is no benevolent group!"

"What are you all thinking? Those mysteriously appearing Spirit of Heaven and Earth are also the Five Elders Association's doing? This is on our Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's turf. We ourselves were unaware of these Spirits of Heaven and Earth; if the Five Elders Association knew about them so clearly, we'd be better off surrendering early!"

...

The occasional appearance of Five Elders Association cultivators did not hinder the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's pursuit of the True Immortal's shed mortal body.

With the emergence of a second lucky one, the desire in their hearts grew even stronger.

The second cultivator fortunate enough to make close contact with the True Immortal's remnant body was named Xue Mu.

He was a Foundation Establishment formation master.

In truth, he did not truly touch the True Immortal's remnant body; he was merely blocked by the golden light encircling it.

But in the instant the golden light entered his body, Xue Mu's mind was instantly flooded with countless bizarre and unimaginable array formations.

The sheer volume of information nearly burst his sea of consciousness.

At this life-or-death juncture, Xue Mu instinctively let go. The True Immortal's remnant body then vanished once more.

But Xue Mu, who had missed such a tremendous opportunity, showed no regret. Instead, after being stunned for a long time, his face lit up with joy, and he threw his head back in wild laughter.

Later, everyone learned that in that seemingly brief instant, he had obtained at least a hundred formations from the True Immortal's remnant body.

Moreover, and more importantly, the level of these formations generally surpassed those of the Xuanhuang Realm by several tiers.

Even the most powerful formation in the current Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance could not compare to any one in his mind!

Although his strength had not increased, for the formation master Xue Mu, this harvest was far more important than advancing several major realms.

Soon, Xue Mu was taken away by someone dispatched from the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's Strategy Formation Hall.

Not long after, internal news confirmed the advanced nature of the formations he had obtained in his sea of consciousness.

What existence besides a True Immortal could possess such ability?

This undoubtedly confirmed the rumor of the True Immortal's remnant body.

After the news spread, some people in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance finally could not sit still.

In the Spiritwood Realm, Li Fan connected the communication with Dharma-Transmitter Zhou.

“The matter of the True Immortal's remnant body has caused quite a stir recently. What is Fellow Daoist's view on it?”

Li Fan quickly replied, his tone resolute: “It is all fabrication, pure rumor! Fellow Daoist Zhou, you must not believe it!”

“You are so certain?” Now it was Dharma-Transmitter Zhou's turn to be suspicious.

Li Fan sneered: “The immortal path has been severed for so many years; none of our colleagues from the various realms has ever seen a True Immortal's corpse.”

“The Xuanhuang Realm...”

“Is the same. How could it be such a coincidence that it appeared without warning? And it spread to everyone in an instant; I suspect someone is stirring things up in secret!” Li Fan said with conviction.

“But the accompanying Spirits of Heaven and Earth are indeed genuine. Even in the Immortal Alliance, two people have already successfully advanced to Dao Integration because of them. I truly cannot think of any force in the Xuanhuang Realm that could so casually produce so many Dao Integration resources and use them as bait?” Dharma-Transmitter Zhou did not heed Li Fan's words but countered.

Li Fan seemed stumped as well.

After a long silence, he sighed: “The more this is the case, the greater the plot behind it must be! Your Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance reaps full rewards, while we live in constant fear.”

Dharma-Transmitter Zhou suddenly asked upon hearing this: “It truly is not your handiwork?”

“What kind of talk is that! The Federation of All Worlds has lain dormant for many years; how could we suddenly stir up such a storm?” Li Fan's tone carried some displeasure.

“In my view, it might instead be an enemy of your Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. Ruling the Xuanhuang Realm for thousands of years, have you no mortal foes?”

“Hm?!” Li Fan's words seemed to remind her of something.

Dharma-Transmitter Zhou was somewhat taken aback, and after a brief silence, she hastily cut off the communication.

Only then did Li Fan's face reveal an enigmatic expression.

There was no need for words; all the turmoil in this affair was naturally Li Fan's doing.

Using the darkness power of the pitch-black puppet, the True Immortal's remnant body shuttled back and forth across regions, appearing and vanishing like a ghost, evading the pursuit of countless cultivators.

And by secretly diverting the Heavenly Dao False Misconversion in the shadows, directing one Spirit of Heaven and Earth after another to manifest voluntarily, Li Fan made the performance even more lifelike.

As for the two beneficiaries of this incident, Zhang Haobo and Xue Mu, though they had no connection to him in this life, they were all acquaintances from past reincarnation experiences.

Li Fan did not mind; he was happy to do a good deed and help them out.

However, as he widely spread false heavenly mandates across various places, the Xuanhuang Realm's tolerance and vigilance were nearing their limit.

Next, he would probably have to lie low for a while.

“The formation is set; they have no choice but to enter the trap. In such a chaotic situation, it should conceal my true intentions,” Li Fan said with a low chuckle.

“But Dharma-Transmitter Zhou's reaction at the end was somewhat strange. What did she recall?”

“Could it be that the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance truly has an enemy willing to use several Dao Integration resources as bait?”

As Li Fan thought this, he could not help but furrow his brow.

Chapter 1108: Alien Star Falls Into Xuanhuang

Although the Xuanhuang Realm had grown vastly larger than other normal cultivation realms after thousands of years of attraction and devouring. Li Fan truly could not imagine where such a force, capable of making the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance dread it immensely, could possibly hide if it still existed.

Back then, when the Tianxuan Mirror ascended and transformed, it had thoroughly scoured the entire Xuanhuang Realm. Not only had it basically mapped out information on nearly all affiliated Cave-Heavens and small realms, but even the world fissures deep within the white mist had been explored almost completely.

"Unless it is another special hidden region like the Dark Realm."

"One can only say it lives up to being the last surviving gathering place of Darkest Star Sea cultivators; you never know when a powerhouse might suddenly emerge."

"But since they did not survive the final cataclysm in the Xuanhuang Realm, no matter how strong they are, their strength is limited."

After this analysis, Li Fan felt slightly reassured.

"Speaking of which, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's sudden rise from the Cultivation Wasteland, upon closer reflection now, does seem rather suspicious."

After the great calamity of the Xuanhuang Realm, cultivators fell into an era of mutual suspicion and slaughter, a dark age. In the end, civilization shattered, and no organizations remained in the world. Only lone wanderers, vigilant like wild beasts and filled with killing intent, survived: the so-called Cultivation Wasteland.

All records that can be consulted today regarding what happened later in the Xuanhuang Realm are basically consistent. The long-vanished Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor returned once more and rebuilt civilization upon the ruined land.

So the question arises. Whether from the [Era] illusion experienced in the Museum of Divine Treasure, or from the historical fragments unearthed by [Seeking the Hidden] in the blood-colored long river of the Spiritwood Realm, the descriptions of the cultivators living in that era can all be summed up with the four words "fierce and brutal."

Those who could survive the great free-for-all naturally possessed exceptional mindsets, methods, and strength, which goes without saying. For such a group of people, faced with the sudden appearance of the Immortal Ancestor proclaiming unification of the Xuanhuang Realm, what would their reaction be?

And where are those people now? Neither the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance nor the Five Elders Association seems to include any trace of them.

"I remember that the Ten Thousand Law Chamber of Commerce in Nine Mountains Prefecture was inherited from that era. Unfortunately, it has now been razed to the ground; otherwise, I might have gone to inquire about some related information."

Li Fan noted this down, leaving it for resolution in his next life. His attention then returned to the "True Immortal Remnant Body."

After receiving Li Fan's reminder, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance indeed became much more vigilant. For those Spirits of Heaven and Earth that appeared, they directly opted for collective capture. As for their final allocation, it had already been discussed in advance, all to swiftly swallow these "baits" without harm and convert them into their own combat strength.

With the official intervention of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, the various disturbances caused by the appearance of the Spirit of Heaven and Earth were quickly quelled.

As for the True Immortal remnant body, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's attitude was one of extreme caution. They pursued it relentlessly, yet forbade cultivators from slaughtering each other over it. In the end, who obtained it would depend entirely on personal fortune.

At the same time, to swiftly quell this storm, those Dharma-Transmitters who typically ignored worldly affairs made the unprecedented move of dispatching multiple members. It seems that after the Mo Rubin incident, their style of conduct had accordingly changed.

Once the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance joined this game, Li Fan clearly felt that manipulating the black puppet to walk the dog had become much more laborious. The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's control over its own territorial lands remains quite strong.

The puppet carrying the True Immortal remnant body would be discovered soon after appearing, forcing another relocation. As the frequency of relocations increased, the black puppet's drawback of being unable to sustain prolonged combat became glaringly apparent.

Fortunately, Li Fan had ordered Qiaogong and the others to urgently craft several more puppets for alternating use, barely maintaining the True Immortal remains' elusive and ghostly nature.

However, Li Fan faintly sensed that the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had become aware of the True Immortal remnant body' method of transfer via darkness power. The time required for tracking was growing shorter and shorter.

“It seems this act cannot last much longer.”

“But no matter; my goal has already been achieved. Now, I await the final curtain, to give the Xuanhuang Realm's denizens a surprise.”

After surveying the specifics of his various arrangements, Li Fan revealed a kindly smile.

“Young friend, why must you go to such lengths?”

Three shadowy figures suddenly emerged, encircling Li Fan from left and right. Their faces were etched with sorrow, their complexions sallow and withered. They stared fixedly at Li Fan without speaking, merely sighing deeply.

These were the paper figures from the Book of Du'e, now fully awakened.

Ever since Li Fan began preparations for world-ending destruction, even though all arrangements were executed through clones and puppets, these paper figures from the Du'e Sect had somehow sensed the impending crisis. From a single one at the start, two more had awakened in succession.

Moreover, they could faintly detect the world-destroying intent in Li Fan's mind. Whenever Li Fan simulated or deduced future scenarios, these fellows would persistently surround him with entreaties.

Li Fan, of course, ignored them entirely, responding with a cold face.

Once the three Du'e Sect paper figures realized their persuasions were futile, they simply gave up. Instead, they wore mournful expressions and clung to Li Fan.

At first, Li Fan thought this was mere impotent rage. But later, he discovered that being surrounded by these sorrowful-faced paper figures actually infected him with their emotions. No matter what he had been thinking before, whether surging with passion or harboring the most extreme notions. It all gradually faded into utter negativity and utter emptiness.

If not for the protection of the [Xuanhuang Immortal Heart Mantra], Li Fan might truly have been swayed and altered by them.

The Du'e Sect paper figures could only sense the general situation. As for the specific measures Li Fan had taken during this period, they lacked the ability to perceive them remotely. Li Fan also refrained from revealing that he was utterly unaffected. Instead, he cooperated fully in performing this charade with them.

The gloomy expression on his face slowly vanished, replaced by a profound emptiness where nothing seemed worthwhile. His mind gradually emptied of all thoughts, no longer dwelling on preparations related to world destruction.

The figures of the three Du'e Sect paper figures also began to change. Rather than returning directly to the Book of Du'e, they transformed into the solemn images of stern old scholars. They sat upright and proper, solemnly recounting to Li Fan the various calamities the Xuanhuang Realm had endured in the past.

Their names were quite evocative as well. Each was named after one of those historical calamities.

For instance, one of them was called [Starfall]. This referred to the event, before the Immortal Path was severed, when an alien star had plummeted from the cosmos into the Xuanhuang Realm. The energy permeating that alien star had once briefly disrupted the spiritual power's efficacy for all cultivators in the Xuanhuang Realm. Regardless of realm, everyone's strength had plummeted dramatically.
