Chapter 1109: Neither Body Nor Mind Cross The Calamity

A Golden Core cultivator stumbles while walking and accidentally breaks his leg.

A Nascent Soul cultivator flies in midair, but his spiritual power falters, causing him to plummet to his death on the spot.

A Soul Transformation cultivator dies suddenly from a heart attack during an overly intense battle with another...

These are the bizarre cases that occurred back then.

Later, this not-too-major calamity eventually dissipated on its own as the strange energy orbiting that alien star slowly faded away, vanishing without a trace.

In this current era, it has long been forgotten by everyone and does not exist in any historical records.

Only the Du'e Sect, through their special cultivation methods, recorded it.

"A force capable of affecting the entire Xuanhuang cultivation world is undoubtedly at the immortal level. It's just a pity that the alien star was merely a chance visitor to the Xuanhuang Realm. It has never appeared again since."

Li Fan could not help but feel a bit regretful.

Otherwise, just imagining those familiar cultivators having to live like ordinary mortals would have been quite amusing.

The paper figures from the Du'e Sect were not idly chatting with Li Fan about these past calamities in the Xuanhuang Realm.

Clearly, they had another purpose.

As scenes of cruel calamities continuously appeared in Li Fan's mind, they gradually receded, and the battered Xuanhuang Realm finally regained new life.

A sense of "surviving the calamity" welled up in his heart.

He could not help but feel a touch of hard-won gratitude and cherishment for the current peaceful and stable state of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Of course, the effects of this brainwashing all quickly faded away with the circulation of the Xuanhuang Immortal Heart Mantra.

But Li Fan still acted the part with utmost sincerity, his face showing the perfect expression.

Finally, seeing that their methods had taken effect, the three paper figures obediently returned to the Book of Du'e.

"The Du'e Sect's various tricks mostly involve toying with people's hearts. One can see just how vile this sect is."

"It's just unfortunate for them that they encountered me. Hmph..."

Ever since he had freed the Book of Du'e from the Dharma-Transmitter's restrictions, they had vaguely latched onto Li Fan.

Like persistent ghosts, following him everywhere.

Although with Li Fan's current mastery of formations, he could easily reseal them by force with an array.

But Li Fan did not do so.

Because being pestered by these Du'e Sect paper figures was not without its benefits.

Li Fan narrowed his eyes and gazed into the distance at the Xuanhuang Realm.

He could vaguely see countless tiny specks of paper figures scattered across various parts of Xuanhuang.

When his intent focused on one of them, that paper figure inexplicably opened its eyes.

Through the paper figure's eyes, Li Fan could see the scene it was surveilling.

However, it was like looking through frosted glass: not very clear. The sounds were also intermittent.

The connection did not last long before it was forcibly severed.

"It seems my authority is still insufficient," Li Fan thought with a cold smile.

The benefit of accepting the Du'e Sect paper figures' brainwashing was that he could gradually gain control over the [World-Observing Divine Eye].

The process of seemingly acknowledging the Du'e Sect's ideology was also the process of joining and becoming a member of the Du'e Sect.

But clearly, Li Fan only intended to reap the benefits without actually joining this so-called Du'e Sect.

"Only if one's strength is sufficiently great can any calamity be endured without harm."

"Hiding like a turtle in its shell, what kind of skill is that?"

"Blindly fleeing, what use is it? When the Immortal Ruins Immortal wiped out the world with a single glance before, they could not even struggle and simply vanished."

Li Fan had long known how the Du'e Sect would behave when the true calamity arrived; he held their ideals in utter disdain and contempt.

Yet he did not underestimate them.

While Li Fan was dealing with the Du'e Sect, the grand drama of that True Immortal remnant corpse was also reaching its climax.

The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, which had been tracking the remnant corpse for so long, had finally deciphered the patterns and methods of its appearances.

This time, they dispatched a full ten Dao Integration cultivators to encircle and intercept it, determined to capture this so-called True Immortal remnant body that had stirred up such turmoil.

The operation even included assistance from three Dharma-Transmitters.

Faced with such a massive force's encirclement, the black puppet was clearly overwhelmed.

In the end, under Li Fan's command, its body dissolved, pouring all of its stored final traces of darkness power into the True Immortal remnant corpse.

Thus, the True Immortal remnant corpse, replenished with power, vanished once more under everyone's watchful eyes, right in the midst of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's encirclement!

However, this time, the distance it fled was not far.

The pursuing group of Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance cultivators quickly spotted its trail.

Unlike the previous elusive and ghostly evasions, it was now fleeing using the normal flight method of a cultivator.

"It seems he has finally tired of this game of hide-and-seek!" one Dao Integration cultivator said, his tone laced with faint anger.

Anyone who had been led around by a mere remnant body for so long would inevitably feel some frustration.

"Chase it! Be careful; do not get careless!"

"Eh, look at the direction he is flying..."

"No good; he is heading into the Essence Devouring White Mist!"

"No matter. I carry the Samantabhadra True Boat with me. Continue the pursuit!" Wei, one of the supervising Dharma-Transmitters, said coldly.

He released a flying boat, and the group of Dao Integration cultivators boarded it before closely pursuing the True Immortal remnant.

Some among them clearly were not entering the white mist for the first time.

Their eyes showed no fluctuation; instead, they calmly locked onto the position of the True Immortal remnant body ahead.

"It feels a bit off. Before, he only moved on the outskirts; why suddenly lead us into the white mist?"

"Could it be a trap?"

The group exchanged glances with one another.

However, with their current combined strength, even if they encountered a Longevity Immortal realm cultivator, they could retreat unscathed.

So they had few qualms.

Without much hesitation, they quickly reached a consensus: continue the chase and see what scheme this True Immortal remnant body was up to.

In the white mist, time lost all meaning.

The True Immortal remnant body's enticing aura was also extremely pronounced, as if afraid the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance behind would lose its trail; while flying, it would even deliberately slow down.

Maintaining a fixed distance between the two sides.

Moreover, the True Immortal remnant body seemed extremely familiar with the white mist, occasionally changing direction.

It clearly had a definite destination.

This eerie scene made all the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance cultivators inside the Samantabhadra True Boat look grim.

After pondering for a moment, the Dharma-Transmitter Wei ultimately decided to keep following and investigate.

After all, as a Dharma-Transmitter, he could not truly perish.

And even if the True Immortal remnant body had some plot, figuring it out at the cost of a single life would still be quite worthwhile.

As for the Dao Integration cultivators beside him...

The Dharma-Transmitter Wei's heart remained undisturbed.

The True Immortal remnant body and the Samantabhadra flying boat.

One ahead, the other behind, speeding through the white mist.

After an unknown length of time, the scene before them suddenly shifted.

When they clearly saw what lay ahead, the cultivators on the boat, including the several Dharma-Transmitters, all showed horror; their minds trembled violently.

An endless ocean floated with countless corpses.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1110: Death And Life Reveal Heavenly Sovereign

These corpses were all sealed in pale blue transparent ice crystals. Through the ice crystals, one could vaguely see the final expressions on these corpses' faces from their lives. Anger, fear, doubt... Lifelike, as if they were still alive.

Dense clusters of ice crystal coffins filled the entire field of vision, blanketing the whole black ocean.

Not to mention those few Dao Integration cultivators; even the three leading Dharma-Transmitters felt a chill run down their spines and were speechless with horror upon witnessing the scene before them. Shocked by this spectacle, by the time they regained their senses, the True Immortal's remains that had lured them here had long since vanished without a trace!

"This... what in the world is this ghostly place?!"

After a deathly silence, among the group, the Dao Integration cultivator named Zhu Gongchuan could not help but speak up. Fearing to disturb the cultivators slumbering here, Zhu Gongchuan's voice was very soft. Moreover, one could faintly hear the fear deep in his heart.

It was not that they had never seen dead people. But so many corpses, all gathered in one place, and still preserving their vivid expressions from life... The shock brought by this scene was far beyond what words could describe.

No one answered Zhu Gongchuan's question. The group flew silently over the Sea of Death, and even after a full half day, they still had not found its end.

"By preliminary estimate, the number of corpses here exceeds two billion... I'm afraid that even the combined number of cultivators and mortals currently living in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's Outer Prefectures is far less than this. Exactly..."

"These people are not from the Xuanhuang Realm. Or rather, the vast majority are not from the Xuanhuang Realm," Dharma-Transmitter Wei said slowly at this point. "Judging from their clothing styles, these people should all come from different cultivation realms. Yet for some unknown reason, they were all sealed in ice here."

"So many corpses, hidden within our Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's territory. And for so long, we have been completely unaware of it." The other Dharma-Transmitter Chu's expression also turned extremely ugly.

"What purpose did that True Immortal's remains have in luring us here? Could it be that it wants to turn us into this state as well?"

"No. We have been inside for so long, yet we have not been attacked at all."

The group discussed in hushed tones, yet they had no clue about the True Immortal's remnant' ultimate goal.

"Let's search a bit more."

So they flew over the Sea of Death for a long time longer. Finally, Dharma-Transmitter Wei discovered a clue: "These countless frozen corpses seem to form a massive formation."

"A formation?" The group exchanged glances upon hearing this, then looked carefully around once more.

"Such a large-scale formation must possess world-destroying power. Could it be that this is a secret arrangement by the Five Elders Association to annihilate our Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance over these years?"

Not everyone present was proficient in formations, and after pondering fruitlessly, they could not help but let their imaginations run wild.

"This formation is extremely complex; seeing only a corner without the full picture, we cannot infer its true purpose. The urgent matter now is to first report this discovery to the Alliance headquarters. The longer we stay here, the more uncomfortable I feel in my heart," Dharma-Transmitter Chu said gravely.

"Good thing this formation shows no intent toward us outsiders. It is neither a trapping formation nor a killing formation; as long as we find the formation's eye, we should be able to leave," Dharma-Transmitter Wei also made his judgment.

With the two Dharma-Transmitters reaching conclusions, the group finally breathed a secret sigh of relief in their hearts.

After lingering in the formation for several days, the group finally located the formation's core. Passing through the core, they had assumed they would return to the white mist, but instead, they found themselves above an unfamiliar ocean. Unlike the previous dead, frozen black sea, here the salty, damp sea breeze carried a faint intoxication, and the calls of seabirds echoed endlessly in their ears.

From time to time, schools of fish could be seen leaping from the surface or darting through the depths. Teeming with life, the scene was refreshing and uplifting.

After a brief moment of astonishment, the group quickly scanned their surroundings and found no immediate danger. The Dao Integration cultivators thus felt a slight sense of relief. But the three leading Dharma-Transmitters grew unprecedentedly grave.

Dharma-Transmitter Wei furrowed his brows, gazing into the distance at the sky, murmuring to himself: "Different worlds, two sides of the same coin."

"One is life, the other is death."

Dharma-Transmitter Chu interrupted him: "No, death is the source of life, and life is the gathering of death. This is..."

The two exchanged a glance, their faces filled with horror. "Reversing life and death?" In perfect unison, they refrained from voicing those four words. Instead, they communicated via transmission.

"So that's it. Does that mean this place is that Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign's abode?"

"No wonder His speed in reversing the principles of heaven and earth to prove His Heavenly Sovereign status was so astonishing. He had long hidden here in this white mist, using the formation as a foundation to study the principles of life and death to utter perfection!"

"Then what connection does that True Immortal's remains have with the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign?"

Having unraveled one mystery, new doubts immediately arose in their minds.

"From the current situation, they do not seem to be allies."

The two fell into deep thought. After pondering for a long time, they reached no firm conclusion.

"Finding the formation's core in this world of life should allow us to exit from here." With no resolution from their deliberations, and a thorough search of the area yielding no information on the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign's true identity, the three Dharma-Transmitters decided to withdraw for now and plan at length.

But just as they prepared to leave, Dharma-Transmitter Wei's body paused slightly, as if struck by a realization.

"Reversing life and death, reversing life and death... The grand formation is complete, yet those deceased cultivators have not awakened. What has truly revived is the [world]?"

"If that's the case, then the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign's true purpose is..."

Dharma-Transmitter Wei paled as if struck by lightning.

"Go!" he commanded decisively.

However, it seemed his thoughts had been detected. A figure seemingly composed of white mist appeared silently, blocking the group's path. Faced with this sudden enemy, who might very well be the elusive Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign, the group tensed to the extreme. Battle was imminent.

Dharma-Transmitter Wei stared at the figure for a moment. Seconds later, incredulity flashed in his eyes.

"It is..."

Before he could finish speaking, the white mist figure abruptly opened its eyes. Endless white mist surged from its body, overwhelming the sky and earth. In an instant, it engulfed the entire Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance group.

Ten Dao Integration cultivators and three Dharma-Transmitters. Such a formidable force had no power to resist and vanished into the white mist in the blink of an eye.

Chapter 1111: Silently Setting The Prison Net

In the Xuanhuang Realm, within the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, all of the floating heavenly cities suddenly resounded with the same urgent bell chime at that very moment.

The cultivators of the Immortal Alliance froze, halting whatever they were doing, stunned in place. After a brief mental lapse, they quickly recalled what this sound represented.

It was the highest-level alarm of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. Such a signal would only be activated when the alliance faced a crisis of life and death.

After a brief silence, the entire alliance erupted into chaos. People panicked, confused, and directionless.

Meanwhile, Li Fan, located in the Spiritwood Realm, naturally heard the bells the moment they rang. At first, he thought his own world-destroying arrangements had been discovered.

However, he quickly realized that the sudden ringing was most likely connected to the events occurring inside the Life and Death Grand Formation. When the image recorded by the formation left behind by a True Immortal’s remnant body appeared before him, it confirmed his suspicion.

The recording ultimately stopped at the moment when the Dharma-Transmitters Immortals and their group disappeared into the white mist. Immediately after, the recording formation itself was destroyed by that surging, endless white fog.

Li Fan repeatedly watched the recording, a trace of solemnity flashing in his eyes.

“The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance’s reaction is this extreme. Could it be…”

“Those three Dharma-Transmitter are dead?”

A surge of doubt and unease rose in his heart. He knew that each Dharma-Transmitter was protected by the Immortal Ancestor.

As long as that protective power remained, they could be said to be undying and indestructible within the Xuanhuang Realm. Although such protection restricted them from advancing to the Longevity Immortal Realm, they were, for all practical purposes, nearly eternal.

Yet now, before the newly ascended Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign, they had been annihilated without resistance.

“To instantly slay three Dharma-Transmitter?”

“If these three really have perished just like that, then the power of the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign…”

Li Fan’s eyes narrowed slightly. A trace of doubt soon followed.

“If He is truly that powerful, then why kill the three Dharma-Transmitters?”

“They had only entered by mistake. They neither damaged anything nor showed any disrespect.”

Li Fan slowly replayed the recording again, frame by frame. Eventually, he noticed something peculiar.

“It seems that, at first, the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign had no intention of appearing.”

“But one of the Dharma-Transmitters, Wei, must have discovered something—only then did Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign appear.”

“So, was it to silence him?”

Li Fan’s expression turned strange.

To kill for the sake of silencing someone—it was an act unbecoming of one who held the title of Heavenly Sovereign. Yet He had done exactly that.

There could only be one explanation. If what Dharma-Transmitter Wei discovered were to be revealed, it would cause the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign unpredictable harm.

To prevent that variable from occurring, He chose to annihilate them.

“Before Dharma-Transmitter Wei’s death, he must have been thinking about Great Reversal of Life and Death Formation.”

Following that line of thought, Li Fan began to deduce. With his current mastery of formation arts, he quickly grasped the core concept.

“As I suspected before, reversing life and death was never meant to reverse the life and death of cultivators.”

“The resurrection of cultivators was merely a byproduct caused by the change in the world’s natural laws.”

“The true target of this Heavenly Sovereign’s reversal is the [world] itself.”

A flash of brilliance burst forth in Li Fan’s eyes.

“To conceal this purpose, He exterminated the entire Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance expedition.”

“This implies that the world He seeks to resurrect is not the Xuanhuang Realm itself, but one of the many cultivation worlds that were devoured by Xuanhuang over the years.”

“How interesting. Is this… nostalgia for His homeland?”

After going through the entire situation once more, Li Fan slightly nodded. “No wonder this person has kept a low profile for so many years, never showing himself to the public. This can indeed be called borrowing a corpse to bring back a soul. Once successful, his homeland world might truly be revived.”

“But as for the fate of the Xuanhuang Realm itself, that would be hard to say.”

“If this matter were to be exposed, it would surely provoke fierce resistance from the native cultivators of the Xuanhuang Realm.”

A faint smile curved Li Fan’s lips.

“However, all these calculations are destined to fail.”

The smile vanished instantly. Li Fan’s attention returned to the True Immortal Remnant Body. By now, it had already returned to the Xuanhuang Realm.

As the direct culprit behind the fall of three Dharma-Transmitter, it was immediately hunted down by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance upon its reappearance.

This time, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance was truly taking things seriously. After all, during the Mo Rubin possession incident, not a single Dharma-Transmitter had perished.

But now, three of them had fallen at once. The shock this brought to the Council of Dharma-Transmitter was beyond imagination.

More than ten Dharma-Transmitter appeared together, all locking onto the True Immortal Remains at the center. Among the crowd, Li Fan even noticed the curator of the Museum of Divine Treasure, the beast-race Emperor Beast Sanmo.

Compared to his appearance in the Fallen Immortal Realm, he now looked far older. However, he seemed utterly unconcerned about the Alliance’s pursuit. Expressionless, eyes half-closed.

Clearly, his attendance was merely a formality. Besides them, Li Fan finally saw the Mirror-Holder Lineage.

They were all dressed in dark green Daoist robes, with mirror-like imprints engraved on their foreheads.

The three true powers that controlled the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had all gathered here, which showed how tremendous the impact of the three Dharma-Transmitter’ fall truly was. Under the pressure of their combined power, Li Fan noticed his control over the True Immortal Remnant weakening.

He was just about to detonate the remains, to open a channel between the Xuanhuang Realm and the Dark Realm, and unleash a tide of the Dark Sea, when something unexpected happened.

The core forces of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, which had been fully prepared to strike, suddenly seemed to have received some kind of message. At first, there was confusion and disbelief.

Then, without hesitation, they all moved in perfect unison. Their figures blurred, as they completely ignored the culprit and prepared to withdraw from the scene.

Li Fan then saw Emperor Sanmo, who had kept his eyes closed until now, suddenly open them wide. It was as if something had arrived and drawn his attention.

Far away in the Spiritwood Realm, Li Fan felt a surge of foreboding in his heart. His many years of experience made him decide instantly.

He severed his connection with the True Immortal Remains and detonated them at once.

At the exact moment when the remains shattered into countless fragments, a figure shrouded in white mist silently appeared beside him. It appeared without warning and with astonishing speed.

But ultimately, it was still too late.

Endless darkness surged out from the cracks of the shattered True Immortal Remains. It collided head-on with the white mist emanating from the newcomer’s body.

The dark tide that Li Fan had anticipated did not appear. The white mist acted like countless waterproof sandbags, or like an unyielding seawall that stood firm against the waves.

It firmly blocked the catastrophic darkness from spilling out.

…

In the Spiritwood Realm, a trace of coldness flashed across Li Fan’s face.

“What does this mean?”

“The two sides… have joined forces?”

“To find me, they are willing to disregard even the fall of the Dharma-transmitter?”

Chapter 1112: Heavenly Sovereign’S Extermination Strategy

After a brief moment of doubt, what arose next was a profound sense of crisis.

"The only thing in this world that could make those people from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance react so swiftly and uniformly is... The Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor!"

Li Fan's thoughts raced.

"The fall of a Dharma-Transmitter would alert the Immortal Ancestor; that much is understandable. But why would He choose to collaborate with the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign?"

Li Fan's brows furrowed deeply.

With the Immortal Ancestor's strength, upon encountering the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign, He would undoubtedly discern his origins at a glance.

The Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign sought to borrow a corpse to return its soul and revive the devoured world; and from prior intelligence, the Immortal Ancestor was exceedingly concerned with the Xuanhuang Realm... Their positions were almost entirely opposed.

Yet according to the current situation, they seemed to have reached some kind of agreement in such a short time.

"Is it because He was toyed with and grew angry? Or is there some deeper reason?"

The joint entry of two Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns was something Li Fan had not anticipated.

He acted decisively, severing his connection to those puppets currently executing tasks in the Xuanhuang Realm. The final command he issued was for the puppets to self-destruct.

At the same time, he used a formation to seal the entrance from the Spiritwood Realm to the Xuanhuang Realm. As for the passage to the Dark Realm, that was also temporarily closed.

He planned to lie low for the time being.

Although Li Fan could forcibly activate the world-destroying arrangement at this moment, first and foremost, under the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor's gaze, those bait puppets flying toward the Immortal Ruins and drawing the immortals' attention within might not even successfully exit the Xuanhuang Realm.

Though the bait puppets had the cover of darkness power and formations, the Immortal Ancestor was not to be underestimated. Li Fan had no confidence in completing the task right under His eyes.

Faced with this unexpected turn, Li Fan remained calm despite his surprise.

"I still have plenty of time. I refuse to believe these two can keep watching forever. There has never been a reason to guard against thieves for a thousand days."

"Moreover, in Their eyes, I am at most just a petty schemer trying to incite internal strife and profit from the chaos.𝚘𝕧𝕖𝗹.𝗰𝗼𝕞

"As long as I seize the opportunity..."

With these thoughts, Li Fan gradually calmed down.

He suddenly recalled the final scene he had glimpsed through the True Immortal's remnant body just moments ago.

"The Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign, with a body of white mist. To think it could directly contend with the great tide formed by darkness power... He possesses strength that truly threatens the Xuanhuang Realm. Perhaps this is one reason the Immortal Ancestor chose to cooperate with him."

A new thought struck Li Fan.

"The white mist barriers began appearing in the Xuanhuang Realm after the great calamity descended. On the surface, they span heaven and earth, dividing the prefectures. In truth, they serve as the adhesive fusing the Xuanhuang Realm with the devoured world."

"And the white mist is faintly linked to that Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign. In this light, it seems the Immortal Ancestor and the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign likely knew each other before. That would explain how they reached an agreement so quickly."

Regarding the true identity of the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign, Li Fan also had several suspects. The Heavenly Doctor was one of them.

Coming from another cultivation realm, viewing worlds as human bodies, reversing life and death. No matter how one looked at it, he fit the conditions perfectly.

Moreover, in this lifetime, the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign's attainment of the Dao was slightly faster than in previous lives, likely because Li Fan had inherited the [Mending Heaven Record] and resolved the hidden worries of the Cloudwater Heavenly Palace's members on his behalf.

Connecting this to the Heavenly Doctor's previous performances with Tia Jue, Tian Yu, and others, it was almost certain that the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign had some connection to the Heavenly Doctor. Even if not the Heavenly Doctor himself, the relationship was undoubtedly profound.

The Heavenly Doctor appeared and vanished like a dragon. Like the Immortal Ancestor, he traversed the void alone, wandering the Darkest Star Sea to find surviving fragments of cultivation realms to bolster Xuanhuang.

Since their last parting, he had not contacted Li Fan again. Li Fan, naturally, would not take the initiative to reach out to the Heavenly Doctor. Furthermore, he had used the Faceless True Immortal's power to obscure the other's perception.

“With my current level of strength, no matter what I wish to accomplish, it is almost inevitable that I will encounter figures on the level of the Immortal Ancestor and the Heavenly Doctor. This is due to Xuanhuang Realm being reduced to but a corner of its former self.”

“Although I still know very little about them, fortunately... They know even less about me.”

Li Fan's gaze was profound: “There is indeed a gap in strength, but since I dare to scheme against even True Immortals, how could I fear them. It is merely a matter of enduring for now.”

...

Although the Spiritwood Realm was completely sealed, Li Fan remained fully aware of events in the Xuanhuang Realm.

First, through the origin of the Tianxuan Mirror, he spied on the cultivators' various idle chats. Second, naturally, through gathering intelligence from the Dharma-Transmitter Zhou.

“Fellow Daoist Zhou, has something major happened in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance? That highest-level warning bell, and before that, I couldn't even reach you...”

The tone of the Dharma-Transmitter Zhou carried a sense of helplessness: “A fellow Dharma-Transmitter has fallen.”

Li Fan feigned extreme shock, then hurriedly pressed for details. The Dharma-Transmitter Zhou gave a simple account of the matter's cause.

“I had long known that this so-called True Immortal remnant body was a trap. But I never imagined that the power behind it could harm a Dharma-Transmitter?” Li Fan's face was full of incredulity.

“Have you caught the true culprit behind the scenes? Any leads?” Li Fan casually followed up.

The Dharma-Transmitter Zhou shook her head, offering no detailed reply.

Li Fan did not press further, instead saying solemnly: “I will also report this matter to the Federation of All Worlds. If there is any discovery, I will inform you immediately.”

“Then I thank you in advance,” the Dharma-Transmitter Zhou replied perfunctorily, seemingly harboring no hope from Li Fan's side.

After cutting off communication, Li Fan began pondering potential scapegoats.

Yet after racking his brains, in the vast Xuanhuang Realm, those with the strength to accomplish what Li Fan had done were few and far between.

“The remaining few that fit the conditions, such as the Five Elders Association or the Taiyan Sect, framing them would not be easy. If I overplay my hand, it would only expose myself.”

Li Fan sighed inwardly, drawing a lesson: “I still do not have enough alternate identities. If I had another role to play at this time, I could frame myself and clear my own suspicion. I must pay more attention in the future.”

Even Li Fan, who prided himself on his cunning, facing the current situation, could think of no better method than patiently waiting for the storm to pass.

The main reason was that in this lifetime, he had imprudently fallen into the True Immortal's web and could not immediately return to [Truth]. Thus, whatever he did felt constrained and hesitant.

But fleeing to the depths of the Dark Realm for a hasty activation of [Truth] was also not Li Fan's wish.

...

“The situation is stronger than the people. No matter how one schemes and maneuvers, there are times when room to maneuver is lost.”

Chapter 1113: Sole Body Hidden In The Nether Prison

But regarding whether the Immortal Ancestor and Rebirth could track him down, Li Fan was still very confident; he would not be found.

Not to mention the countless concealment formations on that True Immortal Puppet, which were so intricate that they had even faintly surpassed the Great Reversal of Life and Death Formation.

In this world, that frozen Sea of Death had never been visited by anyone before.

Yet the True Immortal's remains could precisely lock onto its location.

Just this point alone was enough to make Rebirth suspicious and paranoid.

"By common sense, he should lock his target on those possible old acquaintances from these years."

"Even if he wants to investigate me, he has no way to start."

"The only flaw is that the True Immortal Puppet's transfer method used darkness power."

"And the Dark Realm, aside from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, only I from the Federation of All Worlds have ever visited..."

Thinking of this, Li Fan could not help but feel a chill in his heart.

At the level of the Immortal Ancestor, actions no longer required evidence to "deduce."

As long as there was the slightest possibility, it was enough for him to make a decision.

"Since I have just reviewed it, in the entire Xuanhuang Realm, there are hardly any forces that could accomplish this. So, from their perspective, as long as they eliminate all of them, they can nip the danger in the bud..."

"Although it seems somewhat unreasonable, it is the way of a hegemon."

As his thoughts turned, Li Fan's heart suddenly pounded fiercely.

He almost immediately made a decision.

In the Spiritwood Realm, all defensive formations were activated at full power.

All cultivators of the Federation of All Worlds prepared for battle at any moment.

He summoned the main personnel from the Heavenly Mechanism Realm, Medicine King Sect, Empire small World, and others, explaining the severity of the situation.

Qiaogong, Liu Ruchen, and the rest all showed slight changes in expression.

"This is too unreasonable, is it not? Merely because we have the strength to slay a Dharma-Transmitter, and they cannot find the culprit, they will wipe us all out?"

"Is the Superior Emmisary being too pessimistic? The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, I think, would not act so recklessly, right?"

The crowd discussed animatedly, but upon seeing Li Fan's gradually darkening expression, they all instantly chose to shut their mouths.

"Through seeking the protection of the True Immortal of Salvation and Mortal Compassion, I have almost confirmed this matter."

"No need for doubt; you all prepare in advance. In this battle, there is no room for compromise."

Li Fan said coldly.

Upon hearing this, everyone's faces turned deathly pale.

"The only chance for survival is if our resistance is strong enough."

"They are coming to avenge the three Dharma-Transmitters, but if we can bring them not just the fall of three Dharma-Transmitters..."

"They'll actually be hesitant." Li Fan mobilized them further.

"Although the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance is powerful, it cannot mobilize the entire alliance to attack us."

"Moreover, we have the advantage of terrain. The Spiritwood Realm is impregnable, covered with countless formations, and backed by the Heavenly Mechanism Realm's barriers."

"Finally, we can also draw on the power of the Dark Realm, striking unexpectedly with the great darkness tide to assault the enemy..."

Li Fan said confidently: "As long as we can withstand the initial fiercest waves of attacks, we should be able to survive this crisis."

Although Li Fan said this, no optimism appeared on the faces of the crowd.

They all left with heavy hearts to carry out pre-battle mobilizations.

The Spiritwood Realm was already fully sealed; even if they had thoughts of fleeing, they could not escape.

Only death in battle remained.

As for Li Fan...

In the underground secret chamber.

A Holy Embryo identical to Li Fan in appearance was rapidly forming.

After forcibly elevating the Holy Embryo to the Nascent Soul realm using the divine power of the True Immortal of Compassion.

The Holy Embryo left the secret chamber and donned the Tianyang Battle Puppet.

As for Li Fan himself...

He brought nothing with him and left the Spiritwood Realm quietly, without anyone noticing.

Knowing that the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance would come to attack the Spiritwood Realm, how could Li Fan leave his True Body behind to fight them to the death?

For Li Fan, things like the Federation of All Worlds, the Spiritwood Realm, the Heavenly Mechanism, Medicine King...

All were mere external objects, things that could be discarded at any time.

Only his own safety mattered most.

As long as he preserved his life and weathered this storm.

Once the Immortal Ancestor and Rebirth lowered their guard in the future, he would activate the world-destroying arrangement.

After everything truly returned, all that he had once lost would eventually come back into his hands.

With Li Fan's current concealment abilities, after leaving the Spiritwood Realm and traveling swiftly, no one discovered his trail from start to finish.

As for his hiding place in the coming days, Li Fan had already planned it out.

"The so-called darkness under the lamp: the most dangerous place is the safest."

Before long, he arrived at his destination.

It was the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's Nether Prison, where he had previously rescued the Dharma-Transmitter Wei from within.

"Heh heh, does this count as walking into the trap myself?"

Li Fan chuckled mockingly to himself.

"However, this so-called net of the Nether Prison cannot hold me."

After carefully observing for a moment, Li Fan cracked the formation enveloping the Nether Prison's entrance.

Transforming into a strand of dim shadow, he slipped inside unnoticed.

The scene before him suddenly grew dim.

It resembled the Dark Realm somewhat.

No heaven or earth; all that met the eye was endless darkness.

But compared to the utter blackness of the Dark Realm, faint glimmers of light existed here.

At least, divine sense could barely discern the direction forward, avoiding complete disorientation.

In the pitch-black endless space stood towering prison pillars like cages.

Each pillar seemed to hold a suppressed prisoner.

The surfaces of the pillars constantly shifted with images of various terrifying punishments.

As for the wardens of this Nether Prison, they were grotesque monsters composed of nameless black mist-like substances floating in the space.

There were thousands upon thousands of them, several times more numerous than the pillars.

They occasionally let out roars and darted into the pillars holding prisoners.

Before long, faint cries of agony echoed endlessly from within the cages.

After tormenting for a long while, these black mist monsters finally withdrew from the pillars, seemingly satisfied.

Similar scenes played out endlessly in the Nether Prison, as if without end.

...

Li Fan gazed at this hellish spectacle, his expression unchanging.

After examining the structure of one prison pillar and confirming it was formed by some array, his eyes flickered.

In an instant, he broke it open.

Then, he chose an empty one and voluntarily entered it.

"Not bad."

Once inside the black pillar, Li Fan looked around and found the cage interior brighter than the outside.

Punitive formation attacks would occasionally strike down as a form of torment.

Naturally, they could not harm Li Fan.

"They could turn the Xuanhuang Realm upside down and still never think to look for me here."

"And once they forget about me, that will be the time for me to emerge."

Li Fan settled into the Nether Prison as comfortably as if returning home.

To him, the Spiritwood Realm and the Nether Prison held no essential difference

Chapter 1114: The Spiritwood War Begins

In the Nether Prison, time seemed to lose all meaning.

Every day, one faced only unchanging dimness. And the endless wails of agony that echoed in one's ears without cease.

The wisdom of those black mist jailers appeared rather low. For Li Fan, this sudden additional prisoner, they merely issued cackling laughs, then mechanically activated the formations in the prisoner pillar, attempting to torment him.

Naturally, all were easily resolved by Li Fan.

Yet he cooperated by simulating his own screams of agony, fooling the black mist jailers into leaving satisfied.

Besides these frequently appearing low-level jailers in the Nether Prison, one could occasionally spot patrolling cultivators from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

There were three in total, taking turns to come. For the task of inspecting the Nether Prison, they often merely went through the motions. After all, the Nether Prison had been peaceful for thousands of years, with never any disturbances.

However, after a period of observation, Li Fan also discerned that the area where he currently hid was merely the outermost layer of the Nether Prison. For the prisoners held here, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance truly paid little attention. Li Fan had even personally witnessed one of his neighbors, unable to endure the formation's torment, shatter his soul and scatter. For a long time afterward, no one came to check.

It was only when the black mist jailers conducted their routine patrol that they discovered it. Then they activated the purification formation, cleansed the black pillar, and awaited the arrival of the next prisoner.

This state of the Nether Prison left Li Fan quite satisfied.

The chance of discovery was very small, and normally no one came to disturb him.

Enjoying the tranquility, Li Fan prepared for the final world-destroying plan on one side, while observing the situation in the Spiritwood Realm through the Holy Embryo on the other.

After Li Fan issued the order for all to prepare for battle and fled, for quite a long time, the outside world remained calm and peaceful. There was no sign at all of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance about to launch a war.

This led everyone to murmur inwardly, doubting Li Fan's judgment.

However, awed by the might of the True Immortal of Salvation and Mortal Compassion, they dared not show it.

Yet Li Fan knew this was merely the calm before the storm.

Facts proved Li Fan's prediction correct.

On this day, the sealing formation at the entrance to the Spiritwood Realm suddenly showed faint traces of fluctuation, all in utter silence.

The Holy Embryo Li Fan detected the anomaly first.

Focusing his mind to observe for a moment, Li Fan snorted coldly: "They do have some skill. It seems the formations I gifted to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance have already been thoroughly studied by them. Compared to before, their formation level has improved by at least a century. It's just a pity..."

"They still lag behind me by more than a generation!"

"At their turtle-like speed, without three months, they won't crack the formation. And that's even without me repairing it. But thinking the other way, if it were the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, they might not have that patience. If breaking the formation fails, they should resort to a direct assault..."

Li Fan did not believe that a mere formation could withstand the assault of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's army.

"Why not create an opening and lure a batch of them in first, then trap them like turtles in a jar!" As his thoughts raced, Li Fan quickly devised a plan.

Although, for safety's sake, his true body had already fled, Li Fan had no intention of completely abandoning resistance against the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's pressure.

After all, accumulating the foundation of the Spiritwood Realm in this lifetime had been no easy feat; it had consumed vast amounts of effort and energy.

Now that the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had come knocking, he certainly had to put up a fight.

"This is the perfect chance to test the mettle of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance."

"See if they truly deserve the title of one of the Xuanhuang Realm's two dominant organizations."

The Holy Embryo Li Fan notified everyone in the Spiritwood Realm to prepare for battle. At the same time, he cooperated with the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's formation-breaking methods, gradually easing the formation's defenses.

It appeared as though the formation had truly been breached.

The opening grew larger and larger. Once a faint crack appeared at the entrance to the Spiritwood Realm's passage.

The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's first wave of attackers instantly slipped through these fissures into the Spiritwood Realm.

In the moment of confrontation, both sides were momentarily taken aback.

The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's side was surprised that the Spiritwood Realm's inhabitants seemed to have long detected their covert maneuvers and were already arrayed in strict formation.

Meanwhile, Li Fan's side was astonished that the so-called vanguard of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance was not some elite suicide squad.

But rather, over a hundred arrows gleaming with chilling light.

"Sky-Patrolling Arrows?!" Li Fan was somewhat surprised.

The Sky-Patrolling Arrows were a major weapon developed by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance in this era: incredibly fast, capable of traversing an entire prefecture in mere breaths. Their power was also astonishing, nearly equivalent to a strike from an ordinary Dao Integration Immortal Sage.

Yet that was not the most terrifying aspect. The true strength of the Sky-Patrolling Arrows lay in their ability to link together into a special formation, forming a massive net that could even twist and influence the laws of heaven and earth.

During the previous great war between the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and the Five Elders Association, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had used the Sky-Patrolling Arrows to disrupt a vast expanse of heavenly principles, preventing the Five Elders Association's Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns from intervening in the battle.

"It seems the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance intends to execute a decapitation strike. One hundred sixty-eight Sky-Patrolling Arrows; for an ordinary small realm, this move would likely eradicate all living forces outright. However..."

Li Fan sneered, reacting with remarkable speed.

First, he used the grand formation enveloping the Spiritwood Realm to interfere with these Sky-Patrolling Arrows, preventing them from completing their array. Then, he dispatched the Tianyang Immortal Puppet, using overwhelming might to collect and suppress them one by one before they could scatter and wreak havoc.

The Sky-Patrolling Arrows were fast, but the Tianyang Immortal Puppet was faster!

The Tianyang Immortal Puppet transformed into a streak of azure light, darting back and forth across the sky.

In the blink of an eye, it had subdued them all.

"Take a few to study; see if we can turn this thing to our advantage." The Holy Embryo Li Fan said to the Master Qiaogong.

Master Qiaogong eagerly accepted the Sky-Patrolling Arrows and hurried off without delay to disassemble and analyze them.

On the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's side, the sudden loss of contact with over a hundred Patrolling Heaven Arrows caught them off guard.

Stunned for a long while, they delayed launching the next wave of attacks.

Seeing this, Li Fan's eyes gleamed. He feigned urgency in repairing the sealing formation at the entrance.

As the "hard-won" crack in the formation began to slowly heal. The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance indeed could not hold back and soon launched another assault.

This time, what passed through the entrance fissure and appeared before the Spiritwood Realm's inhabitants was a team of cultivators clad entirely in golden battle armor.

Shrouded in golden light, their faces could not be discerned. Even their precise positions seemed impossible to lock onto. Upon entering the Spiritwood Realm, the golden-armored cultivators swiftly dispersed.

Their speed was extraordinarily swift, even surpassing the Sky-Patrolling Arrows by a fraction.

Most troublesome of all, they left trails of afterimages along their flight paths.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1115: Laughing Off The Immortal Alliance’S Defeat

Whether observed with the eyes or divine sense, these golden phantoms seemed indistinguishable from true cultivators. It was impossible to tell true from false.

In mere moments, the entire sky over the Spiritwood Realm was filled with the figures of those golden-armored cultivators. Their mission seemed not just a simple assault, but to provide specific intelligence on the Spiritwood Realm for the main force behind them.

"How about these golden armors?" Li Fan asked Fang Zaiji indifferently.

Fang Zaiji narrowed his eyes and observed carefully for a moment, then said with full arrogance: "They're just a slightly advanced smokescreen. I thought the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had truly developed battle armors capable of infinite self-division! A few probes, and the smokescreen will be broken, revealing the real culprits!" Fang Zaiji declared confidently.

"It seems with the technological support from the Heavenly Mechanism Realm, the Spiritwood Realm has surpassed the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance in artifact refinement." Satisfied with the answer, Li Fan waved his hand, signaling that Fang Zaiji need not probe.

Suddenly, between the heavens and earth of the Spiritwood Realm, a massive arc-shaped light sphere enveloped the entire world. The surface of the light sphere was covered in dense, ever-shifting metallic stripes. Under Li Fan's command, in an instant, they formed countless pitch-black cannon muzzles.

"Boom!"

The muzzles locked onto those golden-armored cultivators darting through the sky. Then, countless black light beams erupted fiercely from them!

Before this black light radiating an aura of annihilation, the golden-armored cultivators could not withstand even a fraction; in a flash, they were torn to shreds and completely vanished into the air. With just one volley, over half of the countless golden phantoms were eradicated.

This terrifying sight caused the remaining golden-armored cultivators' movements to halt for a half-beat. In the moment they were stunned, another round of cannon fire arrived in an instant.

Before the black death rays, the golden armors were as fragile as paper. All the golden phantoms between the heavens and earth of the Spiritwood Realm were utterly annihilated.

"The Heavenly Mechanism Fortress, combined with the Sky-Splitting Cannons modeled after those of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. No need to fuss over your fancy smokescreens; even if there are more, just wipe them all out."

This is what they mean by one force subdues ten skills, Li Fan thought to himself. He casually checked the energy stored in the Heavenly Mechanism Fortress.

The Heavenly Mechanism Fortress had merged as one with the Spiritwood Realm. The countless corpses on the plaza that continuously operated the Vital Essence of All Living Beings Technique, as well as the vast number of cultivators in the various small realms, provided source power to it unceasingly. After this period of accumulation, it had already reached an extremely astonishing value.

Li Fan estimated that a barrage of cannon fire at the intensity from earlier could continue nonstop for seven days and seven nights without running out.

“Bring out whatever tricks you have next,” Li Fan said coldly, his eyes fixed on the entrance fissure that kept pulsing.

After consecutively losing two vanguard units, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance seemed to have grown much more cautious. This time, without much deliberation, they finally dispatched their true elite forces.

Before the figures even appeared, a surging aura of killing intent had already arrived from afar. The fissure suddenly widened greatly; Li Fan's heart skipped a beat, and he directly activated the Heavenly Mechanism Fortress's defensive function.

In an instant, the arc-shaped light curtain shook violently, with wave-like patterns flowing ceaselessly within it. It took a long while to finally dissipate this inexplicable impact. After checking, Li Fan's eyelids twitched uncontrollably.

Because the energy reserves in the Heavenly Mechanism Fortress, which he had just thought were more than sufficient, had been depleted by a full sixth in that brief moment! And the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance troops launching the assault had not even shown themselves yet!

What is that thing!? Li Fan narrowed his eyes slightly.

The subordinates in the Spiritwood Realm also sensed something, their expressions turning somewhat tense and uneasy.

“Emmisary, be careful. The enemy has likely already arrived,” Liu Ruchen suddenly warned in a grave tone.

Before the words had finished, the light curtain of the Heavenly Mechanism Fortress shook violently once more. This time, the intensity was even fiercer than before, and the curtain dimmed noticeably as a result. Clearly, it had once again blocked an attack from this unknown enemy on behalf of the Spiritwood Realm.

Li Fan knew that without the Heavenly Mechanism Fortress, the Spiritwood Realm would likely be riddled with holes by now. But the opponent had already launched two assaults, while everyone on the Spiritwood Realm's side still had no idea what kind of existence they were dealing with.

Worthy of the colossal organization that rules half of the Xuanhuang Realm. As expected, it possesses trump cards that I failed to discover even after reincarnating through multiple lifetimes, Li Fan thought, facing this tricky foe not with fear but with delight. One could say that this discovery alone made it barely worthwhile, even if the entire Spiritwood Realm were destroyed right now.

However, the current Spiritwood Realm had gathered forces from numerous ancient surviving cultivation realms after all. It was not truly so easy to annihilate.

As everyone remained baffled and unable to grasp the situation, Daoist Hundred Flowers made a discovery after careful observation.

“This method of attack seems something I once heard a certain friend mention. Fierce beasts hidden in the void, feeding on cultivation realm continents. Ordinary people cannot discern their forms; a single claw or strike holds the power to shatter worlds... If I am not mistaken, it should be the void giant beast known as [Oblivion].”

“In truth, it is not a true living entity. Rather, it is a self-operating manifestation of certain rules in the starry sea,” Daoist Hundred Flowers quickly explained to everyone. “The world of that friend of mine once encountered an assault from this terrifying existence while drifting through the void after the great calamity descended.”

“The countermeasure is also quite simple: Oblivion is only interested in cultivation realms with living beings present. Barren lands without life do not even enter its field of perception. I just never expected that this kind of rule-bound fierce beast could be controlled by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and used against enemies?”

Daoist Hundred Flowers, who had developed a trace of contempt for the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance after their two easy failures earlier, now had eyes filled with utmost gravity. The others, upon hearing this, brightened up. They then all turned to look at Li Fan in unison.

Concealing the life aura of an entire world was not a particularly difficult task for Li Fan. After a moment of contemplation, with the Oblivion Beast continuing its assaults and the Heavenly Mechanism Fortress's energy reserves down to only a third, the light curtain emitted strands of silver-soft glow.

Under the dual overlay of the Heavenly Mechanism Fortress and the Spiritwood Realm's grand formation, they finally managed, just before the defenses were utterly breached, to fade away the life aura of the entire world.

Everyone held their breath in anticipation. After all, a monster like Oblivion, invisible in its true form yet with savage strength, was truly hard to handle.

Fortunately, Daoist Hundred Flowers's guess was correct. Once it lost its sense of the life aura, that terrifying giant beast seemed to have lost its assault target as well. It fell silent just like that.

In the third clash, the Spiritwood Realm claimed a pyrrhic victory.
