Next

Chapter 1116: Strategic Retreat

It was called a pyrrhic victory, but in truth, during these three clashes, the Spiritwood Realm had suffered no real losses. Merely some accumulation of energy had been depleted.

If the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance retreated now, or if it was deterred by the strength the Spiritwood Realm had displayed and temporarily halted its offensive, the Spiritwood Realm could quickly replenish that energy loss. However, it was clear that the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance would not be so easily frightened off.

The Oblivion Beast, having lost its attack target, wandered aimlessly in place for a moment. Then, as if catching a whiff of the enticing aura from the Xuanhuang Realm beyond the spatial passage, it abruptly shifted its focus.

The spatial rift was once again forced open wider, and that aura which had made Li Fan's heart race vanished in an instant. Before the people of the Spiritwood Realm could celebrate, the entire Spiritwood Realm began to shake violently without warning.

"This is..." As the master of the Spiritwood Realm, Li Fan in the Nether Prison sensed the anomaly first. "The anchor point of the Spiritwood Realm in the Xuanhuang Great World is shifting! They intend to draw the coals from under our feet, avoiding direct confrontation and instead directly annihilating the small realm?"

In the past, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had forcibly relocated the Five Elements Great Cave-Heaven in Tianling Prefecture to the central heartland domains. The actual scale of the Spiritwood Realm now was not even as large as the Five Elements Cave-Heaven. Even after Li Fan had reinforced it with formations, it likely could not resist the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's brute force relocation.

Moreover, even though his true body was far away from the battlefield, through the sensations from the Holy Embryo and his clone, Li Fan had already discerned the method the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance was using to migrate the world.

"Floating Starry Sky Grand Formation!" A glint flashed in Li Fan's eyes. He had long coveted this magnificent grand formation, created by the Xuantian King and used by the Xuanhuang Realm to capture other cultivation worlds.

Yet even though his Final Dissociation Disc was on the verge of sublimating to the level of an immortal artifact, it remained powerless against this starry sky grand formation and could not forcibly deduce it. It seemed that beyond the formation itself, there were other unknown factors involved.

When Li Fan had previously asked the Tianxuan Mirror about the details of this formation, it had repeatedly evaded the question, deflecting with information about other grand formations. As a result, Li Fan had never glimpsed the true nature of this formation. He had not expected to encounter it today, during the siege of the Spiritwood Realm.

In his true body's sea of consciousness, the Dissociation Disc emitted a faint excited white glow and flickered rapidly. "From a glimpse through a bamboo tube, one can see the whole leopard. But the Dissociation Disc can reverse-engineer the full formation from this partial view. Indeed, as an opponent of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, one gets to witness more of their hidden cards."

Li Fan was quite satisfied with the results of this probe. As the Spiritwood Realm's anchor position gradually shifted, the initial deductions from Li Fan's Dissociation Disc had already yielded results. "The Tianxuan Mirror did not lie; the grand formation is indeed severely damaged."

"Currently, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance is invoking the Floating Starry Sky Grand Formation's ability to span the starry sea, lock on, and pull in worlds. Judging from the speed at which the Spiritwood Realm is now being forced to move, the formation's power is less than one ten-thousandth of its intact state. But drawing in the entire Spiritwood Realm is still effortless. No, that's not right... Something is still missing."

This was a situation that had never occurred before. The Final Dissociation Disc's deduction had unexpectedly halted on its own. With the Dissociation Disc's profound knowledge of formations, no matter how it calculated, it could not construct a formation with the same function as the ancient Floating Starry Sky Grand Formation.

This once again confirmed Li Fan's previous conjecture. This Floating Starry Sky Formation is indeed not merely a simple formation. “No wonder, with the qualifications of a Immortal Ancestor, it has taken so many years without successfully restoring this formation. He still had to painstakingly scour the void for world remnants himself,” Li Fan mused thoughtfully.

Although he could not glimpse the full scope of the great formation, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance was also employing a flawed version of its power. Under Li Fan's instructions, the people of the Spiritwood Realm, who were in a state of panic and uncertainty, quickly sprang into action.

As a formation manifested on the land of the Spiritwood Realm, an invisible fluctuation enveloped the entire small world. The Spiritwood Realm, which had been dragged forward at high speed, instantly seemed to sever its connection. The shaking gradually subsided, and it finally came to a halt at an unknown location.

Li Fan immediately checked the current position of the Spiritwood Realm. It had shifted from the eastern region of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance to the southern area. It was now remarkably close to that Great Rift Vortex.

“They plan to borrow the natural power of the rift boundary to directly shatter the world? The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance truly has devised a clever scheme.”

The Spiritwood Realm's ability to neutralize the Floating Starry Sky power had far exceeded the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's expectations. Internally, it seemed divisions had arisen, and they refrained from further assault. However, Li Fan could faintly sense that immortal alliance cultivators were continuously emerging in the surrounding void, encircling the Spiritwood Realm completely.

Daoist Hundred Flowers, the Medicine King Sect, and others also perceived the current situation; their expressions turned grim. “This is what they call a fight of a cornered beast. It seems my considerations in this life were still not thorough enough. Next time, when rebuilding the Spiritwood Realm base, perhaps I can incorporate the ability for the entire small realm to teleport instantly.”

“Before today, achieving this would have been somewhat difficult. But with the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's demonstration, heh...” Li Fan sneered inwardly. All blades directed at Li Fan would be returned a thousandfold.

“Emmisary, I believe we can no longer sit idly by,” Liu Ruchen proposed gravely at this moment. “Although the Spiritwood Realm's various defenses are impeccable, they cannot withstand the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's endless consumption. Perhaps breaking out and fighting to the death might offer a slim chance of survival.”

“The Medicine King True Cauldron, after years of modifications, has expanded its internal capacity several times; it should be able to carry most people along,” Fang Zaiji added.

Liu Ruchen's suggestion garnered approval from the vast majority. They all turned to look at the Holy Embryo Li Fan. “Preserve the people and lose the land: both people and land endure; preserve the land and lose the people: both people and land perish.”

“Very well, then everyone prepare for evacuation,” Li Fan nodded in agreement with the group's plan. The crowd immediately acknowledged with a resounding response and dispersed.

One group took charge of monitoring the spatial passage entrance to counter any potential sneak attacks from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. The other group swiftly transferred the Federation of All Worlds' resources and population.

In truth, if the Spiritwood Realm's anchored position had not shifted, Li Fan had prepared a contingency plan of severing the tail to survive. That is, transferring to affiliated small realms like the empire or Great Li. Then destroying the spatial passages.

Although it could not completely eliminate the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's pursuit, it would at least delay them by a few days. Unfortunately, during the recent forced transfer, the Spiritwood Realm's position had deviated. This caused all channels to affiliated small realms to sever.

At this point, to withdraw, the only viable direction was to carve a bloody path through the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's encirclement.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1117: If There’S A Grudge, It Must Be Avenged

"We have a branch established at the Blood-Colored Long River that has not yet been discovered."

"If we are fortunate enough to escape with our lives, we can rendezvous there."

"The blood-colored long river's power has a unique nature that should effectively isolate the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's tracking." Li Fan had already devised countermeasures for after their escape. He spoke these words to core members of the Federation of All Worlds, such as Liu Ruchen and Daoist Hundred Flowers.

As for how the specific location of the Spiritwood Realm was exposed, Li Fan already had a faint inkling in his heart.

But now was not the time for reckoning.

Li Fan did not utter any cold words urging them to fight to the death rather than surrender.

With the power of the Faceless True Immortal binding them, even if they wished to surrender, they probably could not.

While the people of the Spiritwood Realm prepared for a desperate breakout, the Holy Embryo Li Fan quietly arrived before the statue of the Faceless True Immortal.

The statue was merely a vessel.

The True Immortal's power had long since revived through the growing network of Federation of All Worlds members.

"But without a doubt, compared to ordinary members, He should place slightly more importance on His own statue."

"In truth, I could choose to hurl the statue out and let the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance face the True Immortal's power head-on. However..."

"This method can only be used once and not repeated. Afterward, I still intend to rely on Him to contend with the Immortal of the Immortal Ruins."

Under the gaze of the Holy Embryo Li Fan's eyes, the Faceless Statue unexpectedly began to turn translucent.

It then vanished completely.

The Nether Prison.

Li Fan gazed expressionlessly at the statue slowly materializing out of thin air before him.

Wherever the True Immortal's net extended, the True Immortal's power could naturally flow freely.

The cultivation and knowledge he had previously bestowed upon Federation of All Worlds members had also been transmitted through this method.

This was simply his first attempt at transferring a physical object.

"If I truly chose to fully submit to the Faceless True Immortal, devoting myself without reservation and becoming a true emissary of the True Immortal, then by borrowing the vast power of the True Immortal, perhaps I could relocate the entire Spiritwood Realm with a single thought." Li Fan stared at the statue, pondering this.

The arrival of the True Immortal statue brought no change to the Nether Prison.

After stowing the statue, Li Fan, hidden within, turned his attention back to the Spiritwood Realm.

Members of the Federation of All Worlds were hastily preparing to evacuate.

The Holy Embryo Li Fan, meanwhile, unhurriedly set up formations.

Sensing the figure appearing behind him, Li Fan's movements paused slightly.

"Milord Emissary... must it truly come to this?"

The newcomer was the spirit of the giant tree in the Spiritwood Realm, Lin Ling.

"Once this formation activates, everything in the Spiritwood Realm will likely be reduced to ashes." Lin Ling looked at the silver lines faintly visible across the land and said, her face pale.

Li Fan smiled: "This is a strategic withdrawal. How could we let such fertile land in the Spiritwood Realm benefit the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance for nothing? Of course it must be destroyed. As for you..."

"I have not paid attention during this time, yet your proficiency in formations has improved considerably. You can even discern the intent behind my formation setup?" Li Fan's gaze swept over Lin Ling.

Lin Ling pursed her lips: "I lack such ability. It is merely because I share a heart with the Spiritwood. Facing a crisis of life and death, I simply had a faint premonition."

"We will return here one day, will we not? Even if we temporarily yield this place to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, what of it? I believe that under Milord Emissary's leadership, that day will not be far off."

Who could have imagined that the once ignorant and unknowing tree spirit would now utter such gentle words of persuasion.

The Holy Embryo Li Fan's movements in setting up the formation paused slightly.

After a moment of silence, Li Fan asked: "The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance was able to pinpoint the exact location of our Spiritwood Realm so quickly. There must be a traitor among us. Do you have any leads?"

Lin Ling's body trembled instantly, and she exclaimed in alarm: "It is not me!"

Li Fan glanced at her: "If it were you, could you still stand here unscathed and speak to me?"

Lin Ling immediately felt a wave of relief wash over her, exhaling a long breath as if collapsing from exhaustion.

After these years of witnessing the Spiritwood Realm rise from nothing and Li Fan's various methods, she truly harbored not the slightest thought of betrayal.

Compared to Li Fan, those people in the Xuanhuang Realm outside seemed far more naive.

Offending the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance did not necessarily mean death.

But offending Li Fan...

Lin Ling wiped the imaginary sweat from her forehead, thought for a moment, and quickly replied: “Actually, it may not be that a traitor appeared in our Federation of All Worlds. The entrance to the Spiritwood Realm is hidden, yet it cannot fool everyone in the world.”

“At the very least, I know of one who is very clear about it. Moreover...”

Lin Ling carefully glanced at Li Fan, then said softly: “It seems to have some grudge against you.”

Li Fan’s eyes immediately narrowed: “You mean, the Earth Vein Ancient Tree?”

Lin Ling nodded.

“When I was on good terms with him before, he saw all the times I frequently entered and exited the Spiritwood Realm. Although contact was cut off later, the location of our Spiritwood Realm has never changed. Moreover, wherever the earth veins reach, it is all within his gaze...

“More crucially, I could faintly sense his line of sight cast upon us before. But ever since the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance attacked us, his spying gaze has never appeared again. It is just like a thief with a guilty conscience. If it were not him who leaked the secret, given our enmity with him, he should be gloating at our demise right now.” Lin Ling analyzed logically.

Hearing this, Li Fan nodded slightly.

“You did well. The Spiritwood Realm can stay for now.” The Holy Embryo Li Fan halted the progress of setting up the formation.

“A scholar who has been away for three days should be looked upon with new eyes. It would have been even better if you could have reminded us sooner.”

Looking at Lin Ling, Li Fan encouraged her.

Lin Ling’s face flushed red with excitement.

He drove Lin Ling away to prepare for escape. Li Fan then gazed far into the distance outside the Spiritwood Realm.

“Heh, just like his ancestors, so vengeful.”

“Coincidentally, so am I.”

Li Fan still approved of Lin Ling’s deduction.

After all, the entrance to the Spiritwood Realm was extremely secretive; only a few knew of it. Once each was eliminated, the target could be quickly locked.

As for the possibility that Lin Ling was the inside traitor and her entire deduction was fabricated to clear her own suspicion...

Li Fan trusted his own judgment.

He also trusted his eye for people.

“Earth Vein Ancient Tree...”

“Relying on fusing with the earth veins to be so bold and reckless. Hmph.”

In an instant, Li Fan had already thought of how to deal with him.

“The Ruomu tree is very greedy for the descendants left on the surface by the past. If we can present it a great gift, it might improve our relationship.”

However, dealing with the Earth Vein Ancient Tree will have to wait until the next life.

Li Fan noted this grudge for now and waited until the people of the Spiritwood Realm were mostly prepared.

He summoned Liu Ruchen and the others again.

“After we break out, follow behind Tianyang.”

“Form this formation.”

Li Fan solemnly transmitted the formation to the people of the Spiritwood Realm.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1118: Heavenly Sovereign Puppet Master

Although it was merely an formation that Li Fan had just deduced, it was custom-tailored for the people of the Spiritwood Realm. With the Tianyang Immortal Puppet as its core, relying on its Longevity Immortal realm defensive capabilities, it could maximize the combat power of everyone else.

This formation also had an effect similar to the Soul-Ignition Method, capable of fully awakening the potential of the cultivators within it in a short time. Though it came at the cost of burning their divine souls. Before withdrawing the formation, the Holy Embryo Li Fan cast a final glance at the Spiritwood Realm.

"The next life will be better."

Then, without the slightest reluctance, he led everyone in charging out of the Spiritwood Realm. They had originally expected to encounter a tight encirclement by the heavy forces of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. However, to Li Fan and the others' surprise, the void outside the Spiritwood Realm was completely empty!

Yet strangely, the sensation of being constantly monitored that lingered in everyone's hearts persisted!

"Be careful!" Daoist Hundred Flowers was the first to issue a warning. Her face was ashen, and cold sweat actually beaded on her forehead. Even when facing Li Fan's coercion before, she had never appeared so disheveled and distraught.

Because what she had sensed from Li Fan back then was merely pure killing intent. But now... "Pure malice..."

Liu Ruchen from the Medicine King Sect and the others also tensed up as if facing a formidable enemy. Although the path ahead appeared utterly void, with no one guarding it, they all simultaneously halted their charge. Including the leading Li Fan.

Because their instincts told them that charging forward would mean death. It was as if they had been targeted by a natural predator; everyone's bodies stiffened, not daring to move recklessly. At that moment, Tianyang's voice transmitted into Li Fan's ear: "Ahead. Longevity Immortal realm. Strange."

It was brief and grave. To the Holy Embryo Li Fan, it struck like thunder. "Longevity Immortal realm?" "The Immortal Ancestor has arrived?"

His heart felt as if it were gripped tightly, and even the true body of Li Fan, concealed in the Nether Prison, had his aura thrown into slight disarray.

"Of course. The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had long known about the existence of Longevity Immortal realm cultivators in the Federation of All Worlds. After the initial probes, they naturally would not continue pointless sacrifices but would deploy decisive force to settle it in one stroke."

"But... To avenge a few Dharma-Transmitters, He would personally intervene? That would be too degrading, wouldn't it?"

Li Fan was clearly caught off guard by the appearance of the Immortal Ancestor. But immediately after, various doubts arose in his mind. From the Immortal Ancestor's previous attitude toward the Dharma-Transmitters, it was clear that He viewed them merely as tools. Perhaps there was some special regard for Transmitter Jiang, but the other Dharma-Transmitters were no different in His eyes from ordinary cultivators in the Xuanhuang Realm.

Would the Immortal Ancestor personally descend to conquer for the sake of just three Dharma-Transmitters? Li Fan naturally did not believe it. Moreover, Li Fan had once felt the Immortal Ancestor's vast and majestic power. His aura was sacred and boundless, immeasurable in depth. It had absolutely no connection to the "malice" now surrounding them!

"It is not the Dharma-Transmitter. Then who could it be?"

The Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns of the Xuanhuang Realm number only a few. Excluding the five Heavenly Sovereigns of the Five Elders Association, it could not be the recently ascended Rebirth. In a flash of rapid thoughts, a name buried deep in his memories, nearly forgotten, suddenly surfaced in Li Fan's mind: The Eastern Extremity Heavenly Sovereign!

Records of the Eastern Extremity Heavenly Sovereign, even after scouring all the texts of the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance, amount to only a scant few lines. His life story seems deliberately erased; the only known fact is that the Body-Purifying Spiritual Pool, used by the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance today to cleanse mortals of the Immortal-mortal Miasma, was his invention.

Beyond that, nothing else remains. A generation's Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign, and the only one in the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance besides the Immortal Ancestor. To receive such treatment is truly bizarre and unimaginable. If it was not by his own request, then the events that befell him are profoundly thought-provoking.

Moreover, all Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns of the Xuanhuang Realm are named for their defying principles and paths. Only this Eastern Extremity (Dongji) seems to be merely his name.

In moments, countless thoughts flashed through Li Fan's mind. Meanwhile, the malicious intents lurking around launched attacks on the Spiritwood Realm group. The might of a Heavenly Sovereign is truly unlike that of ordinary cultivators.

The Tianyang Immortal Puppet, whose body originally emanated pure white light, suddenly seemed tainted by an ominous aura. The light flickered unsteadily; Tianyang's expression turned grave, as if wrestling with that aura. Black spots appeared sporadically on the puppet's surface, east here, west there.

The assault of the evil aura bypassed the protection of Li Fan's formations, striking everyone in the Spiritwood Realm simultaneously. Not everyone possesses the capital to contend with a Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign. In the blink of an eye, they were eroded.

Li Fan also witnessed the fate of those fully corrupted by the evil aura. Their pupils vanished, leaving their eye sockets pitch black. In the same instant, they turned on their companions with attacks, clearly having lost all sense of self.

Almost immediately, the Spiritwood Realm team descended into chaos, showing signs of collapse. Yet the enemy hidden in the darkness still did not reveal itself.

"Is this the true power of a Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign?" "Defeat is acceptable. But to not even glimpse his true face..."

Amid the turmoil, Li Fan's expression showed no fluctuation; he merely looked toward Tianyang. A blinding crimson suddenly surged from within Tianyang. Like the great sun in the sky, it illuminated the surrounding pitch-black space.

"Tianyang's life... Is not inferior to any!"

The Tianyang Immortal Puppet's body expanded a hundred millionfold in an instant. Like a flame giant from the dawn of creation, it stood tall in the dark void. Rings of crimson flames erupted from his body, spreading ceaselessly outward to incinerate the entire space to ashes.

Under the scorching of Tianyang's fierce flames, the Spiritwood Realm allies remained unscathed. The cultivators eroded by the evil aura turned to black ash amid eerie, piercing shrieks. And the culprit that had always lurked in the darkness was finally forced to reveal its form!

The scene before him made the Holy Embryo Li Fan narrow his eyes slightly. A tall, naked man stood in midair. His black hair reached his ankles; his body was etched with black patterns that emanated evil auras.

Behind the naked man, faint threads could be seen emerging from the void, burrowing deep into his body. The naked man's eyes were pitch black, his face expressionless. Just like those cultivators whose minds had been controlled.

"A Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign, turned into a marionette..."

This bizarre sight sent an uncontrollable chill through the hearts of the Spiritwood Realm group. Li Fan, in particular, drew in a sharp breath.

"This is... The mysteriously vanished Eastern Extremity Heavenly Sovereign, reduced to this state?" "Though not dead, it is a fate worse than death... Who did this?"

"The Dharma-Transmitters of the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance surely lack the ability. The only possibility is the Immortal Ancestor himself!"

In the Nether Prison, Li Fan's true self unconsciously clenched his fist.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1119: The Battle Of Two Heavenly Sovereigns

In the instant he saw this black-haired, bare-bodied man, Li Fan had almost immediately confirmed his identity.

It was undoubtedly the mysteriously missing Eastern Extremity Heavenly Sovereign.

Yet a majestic Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign had been turned into a puppet on strings.

How could this not inspire profound fear?

"No wonder almost no records of the Eastern Extremity could be found. Even through Mo Rubin, who had seized a Dharma-Transmitter and inherited all their memories, or the Tianxuan Mirror, it was the same."

"No wonder when the Heavenly Sovereign acted, all the cultivators of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance withdrew. Such a secret is too horrifying. If they did not leave, they would likely face extermination."

Though controlled by another, the Eastern Extremity seemed to retain some instincts.

Facing the crimson flames erupting from the Tianyang, which matched the power of a Longevity Immortal realm, his long hair behind him suddenly extended. It transformed into countless dark behemoths that extinguished the flames.

Without hesitation, the Tianyang caused a ripple in space and appeared beside the Eastern Extremity in an instant.

Before the colossal form of the Heaven-Opening Giant, the Eastern Extremity appeared as insignificant as an ant.

The scarlet-red flames burning on the Tianyang's body nearly warped the surrounding space with their heat. Yet the Eastern Extremity remained utterly unaffected.

As if he existed independently of this space itself.

His lips parted slightly, as if murmuring something. In that moment, a large chunk of the Tianyang's body deflated without warning. The puppet's frame shook violently, unable to avoid it.

The Tianyang let out an enraged roar, and his body restored itself.

Then, with a bang, he shattered into countless sparks of starfire, surging toward the black-haired Eastern Extremity from all directions.

The crimson fire roared like dragons, incinerating everything in its path.

The countless strands of black hair that had split from the Eastern Extremity also ignited. The flames spread toward the main body.

At that moment, the Eastern Extremity's pitch-black eyes shifted slightly.

Those black-haired monsters, which had already turned to flying ash, unexpectedly reformed from within the flames.

Even after the Tianyang's crimson fire burned them several times, they always revived.

If even the manifested black hair could not be truly harmed, there was no need to mention the Eastern Extremity's true body.

Though the Tianyang roared in frustration, unwilling to accept it.

He could not change this reality.

"Tianyang possesses the power of the Longevity Immortal realm, but it was forcibly remade from source power essence. It lacks the true divine abilities of a Longevity Immortal realm cultivator. Against a Dao Integration cultivator, it can bully without pressure. But facing a genuine Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign..."

"It is no match at all." Li Fan shook his head slightly in secret.

"Moreover, this Eastern Extremity Heavenly Sovereign does not seem weak. The pressure he brings me is even stronger than Senior Sister Zhao's."

In the recent clash between the two Longevity Immortal realm beings, Li Fan had faintly sensed traces of the principles of heaven and earth in motion.

Especially when those black-haired monsters resurrected from the flames, it was particularly intense.

"This is the principle that Eastern Extremity defies?"

After pondering, Li Fan vaguely realized something.

"Reversing the principle of things' development and change?"

"Is this why the Immortal Ancestor dealt such a ruthless hand against Eastern Extremity?"

"If Eastern Extremity achieves complete Dao Integration, he could very likely directly reverse the rules set by the Immortal Ancestor in the Xuanhuang Realm..."

With Li Fan's repeated experiences in glimpsing the Heavenly Dao, his sensitivity to the principles of heaven and earth was almost unmatched by any Dao Integration cultivator.

Such close-range, detailed observation should yield reliable deductions.

"Creating the Body Purifying Spiritual Pool for mortals; this Eastern Extremity is probably a figure like Mr. Bai. It's just unfortunate..."

"Not only forgotten by the world, but also reduced to a life worse than death, turned into the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's final trump card puppet." Li Fan shook his head slightly.

"This is the fate of the weak. But it will never be my end."

"Immortal Ancestor..."

"Hmph."

In the battle between Tianyang and Eastern Extremity, the people of the Spiritwood Realm could not intervene at all.

Even if they wanted to help, they were powerless.

Even with everyone combining forces and amplified by Li Fan's formations, it was the same.

Before they could even approach the battlefield of the two, the shockwaves from the fight caused their spiritual power to boil and their auras to become disordered.

"Is this the Longevity Immortal realm?"

The crowd's expressions were complex.

A trace of despair even surged in the depths of their eyes.

The Holy Embryo Li Fan, however, felt no disturbance in his heart and made accurate judgments.

"It seems there's no escaping. But witnessing the deepest hidden secret of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance is absolutely worth it."

"I just don't know who in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance is manipulating Eastern Extremity from behind. Dharma-Transmitter Jiang? Or that Emperor Sanmo?"

After receiving Li Fan's reminder, Tianyang, who was gradually falling into disadvantage, turned to attack the countless invisible threads behind Eastern Extremity.

Attempting to uncover information about the manipulator behind it.

Unfortunately, the power of those invisible threads was clearly a level higher than that of Eastern Extremity, this Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign.

How could Tianyang possibly probe its depths?

In vain, Tianyang let out another unwilling roar.

...

In the Nether Prison.

Sensing the complete loss of contact from the Holy Embryo side.

Li Fan's true self, who had been somewhat leisurely, could not help but feel a heavy mood for a moment.

The total annihilation of the Spiritwood Realm showed just how vast the gap was in high-end combat power between the Xuanhuang Realm.

But what concerned Li Fan more was the hidden information behind it.

"To turn a not-weak Longevity Immortal realm cultivator into such a state..."

"My expectations for the Immortal Ancestor's strength were already limitless and as high as possible. But now it seems, the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor's strength is even more terrifying than I imagined."

"Even without ascending to immortality, it is probably infinitely close."

"I can no longer evaluate him with the Longevity Immortal realm."

"If he were in the Xuanhuang Realm..."

Li Fan furrowed his brows: "I'm afraid even in the struggle between the two True Immortals, he might still have the ability to save the Xuanhuang Realm."

"Hmm... indeed, we cannot rule out this possibility."

"It seems that in this lifetime, my world-destruction plan needs to be delayed a bit longer."

"Fortunately, in this Nether Prison, time passes quickly."

Li Fan's complex mood finally calmed down slowly.

While reviewing the various experiences of this lifetime, Li Fan prepared for the final plan.

Time flew by, and who knows how long had passed.

The arrival of a new prisoner in the Nether Prison startled Li Fan from his seclusion.

Both arms severed, only one eye left.

The newcomer looked utterly miserable, disheveled with hair, his face covered in dried bloodstains.

Yet it was an old acquaintance of Li Fan.

Kou Hong.

His inseparable brother, Dao Xuanzi, was nowhere to be seen, for some reason.

Even while being escorted, Kou Hong remained unyielding.

He cursed under his breath, venting his hatred toward the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

Li Fan listened for a good while before understanding.

It turned out that after the Federation of All Worlds headquarters in the Spiritwood Realm was uprooted, the scattered members across various places had also suffered ruthless hunts.

Next

Chapter 1120: True Immortal’S Devoted Follower

Strictly speaking, Kou Hong and Dao Xuanzi were not members of the Federation of All Worlds.

They were merely members of the federation's predecessor, the Congyun Mutual Aid Association.

After the Dharma-Transmitter annihilated the Congyun Mutual Aid Association, they lost contact with Li Fan.

It was only through their firm faith in the True Immortal of Salvation and Mortal Compassion that they vaguely sensed the existence of the federation.

Now that the federation had been destroyed, as long as they themselves did not speak of it, the immense favor they had once received from the federation would not be exposed.

Unfortunately, these two were truly men of character.

Upon hearing the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's announcement to exterminate the federation, they did not feel fear or hide their past.

Instead, they did their utmost to assassinate cultivators affiliated with the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

Although they knew that under the divine power of the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign, the cultivators would resurrect after death, they still insisted on doing so to repay Li Fan's kindness from back then.

In this life, the two had received guidance from Li Fan: Dao Xuanzi had built his foundation with the Canghai Pearl, and Kou Hong with the Hundred Thousand Great Mountains Cauldron. Their progress had been swift these years, and they had already reached the mid-Golden Core stage.

But in the face of the colossal Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, they were nothing more than ants. After killing just over a dozen insignificant alliance cultivators, they were quickly surrounded by the alliance's Guardian Hall cultivators who arrived on the scene.

Dao Xuanzi refused to become a prisoner and laughed heartily as he self-detonated, ending his life.

Kou Hong had originally intended to follow Dao Xuanzi in death but was stopped by the alliance cultivators.

After having both his arms severed and one eye destroyed, he was sent by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance to the Nether Prison, where he would be eternally suppressed.

“You can only run your mouth for now. I hope you'll still be this tough after experiencing the torment of the formations here.” Facing Kou Hong's nonstop curses, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance cultivator escorting him was not angered but sneered coldly.

Kou Hong continued to curse without pause.

And what followed exceeded even Li Fan's expectations regarding Kou Hong's performance.

Li Fan was all too familiar with the power of the formations within the high pillars of the Nether Prison. Ordinary people could not endure such endless torment for long and would instinctively wail and beg for mercy.

But this Kou Hong, even when tormented to the brink of death and his consciousness blurred,

Would still mutter faint curses from his mouth without cease.

“This is partly due to the brainwashing by the Faceless True Immortal, but it also stems from Kou Hong's own resolute will. Once he decides on something, he must see it through. Even in death, he would have no regrets.”

When Li Fan first met them, they could have simply joined the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and toiled like oxen for over a decade to earn a technique for advancing to the Golden Core stage. Yet they refused. They preferred to risk their lives exploring sect ruins.

Later, after obtaining a Nascent Soul true technique, the two, who were as close as brothers, fought to the death over a slim chance at the Dao.

And in this life, after being saved by Li Fan from the mutated Immortal-mortal Miasma, they joined the Mutual Aid Association and became the most devoted seekers of the True Immortal of Salvation and Compassionate.

...

These examples all vividly illustrated their characters.

“He is a malleable talent, one I can use. At the very least, he would be far more reliable than those so-called geniuses.” Li Fan looked at Kou Hong in the distance, who even in unconsciousness bore a resolute expression on his face, and a glint of approval flashed in his eyes.

When the Spiritwood Realm was on the verge of destruction in this life, and Li Fan led the federation members in their escape, he had observed everyone's reactions closely.

Those prodigies from the Shanhe Xuan who had been turned by Shang Tianci's scheme had sworn oaths before the statue of the Faceless True Immortal. Yet at the moment of life and death, they showed no willingness to stand or fall with the federation.

Instead, they displayed regret, a desire to flee, to surrender, and similar sentiments.

In terms of loyalty alone, they fell far short of Kou Hong.

With these thoughts, an inconspicuous beam of light pierced through the prison cage's pillar and flew toward Kou Hong, who had passed out nearby.

The ferocious expression on Kou Hong's face instantly softened considerably.

When Kou Hong awoke and sensed the new addition in his mind, a flicker of surprise passed through his eyes.

But he still feigned defiance, angrily denouncing the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's various shameless acts.

At the same time, he began studying the knowledge he had just obtained.

“This is…”

“A detailed explanation of formations?”

“And it’s the formation that makes up this Nether Prison Cage?”

“How could it appear in my mind?”

After a long while, Kou Hong’s spirit trembled violently, and he forcibly suppressed the urge to look around.

“If this detailed explanation of the formation is real, then escaping from this prison would be as easy as turning my hand.”

Having experienced many hardships, Kou Hong was far more composed than before.

Instead of blindly trusting it, he began to study and learn the contents of the formation explanation.

Although he had only roughly studied formations before, this explanation was clear, easy to understand, and straightforward. Combined with Kou Hong’s own decent aptitude, he made progress quickly.

Seven days later, Kou Hong had barely reached the beginner level.

At the same time, a trace of doubt flashed in his mind: “Could this be a trap?”

“This is clearly a place where the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance holds serious criminals. How could the formation guarding it be so simple? As a beginner, I’ve only studied for a few days, and I can already break it?”

Kou Hong silently attempted to disrupt the formation.

At this moment, his body was extremely weak, and the spiritual energy within him was tightly suppressed, unusable. Yet, by exerting all his strength to produce a faint ripple of spiritual energy, he managed to slightly affect the prison formation.

This change manifested when the Nether Prison guards activated the formation to torment Kou Hong again. The pain he felt was noticeably reduced!

“It’s actually real?” Despite the agony, Kou Hong’s heart surged with wild joy.

But he quickly calmed himself and began to ponder the origin of this formation explanation in his mind.

“I’m just a minor Golden Core cultivator. The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance wouldn’t bother staging a play for me.”

“And this formation explanation, in its depth and finesse, far surpasses the level of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance…”

A sacred name suddenly appeared in Kou Hong’s mind.

“True Immortal of the Mortal Compassion.”

At that moment, a warm current surged within Kou Hong’s body, flowing through his limbs and bones.

In an instant, it broke through the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance’s restrictions, nourishing and repairing his broken body while rapidly enhancing his cultivation.

Sensing the earth-shattering changes within him, Kou Hong’s body stiffened. He dared not show any abnormality, fearing discovery by others.

In truth, his heart was already filled with tears of gratitude.

The great and merciful True Immortal of the Mortal Compassion had not abandoned him!

“The Federation of All Worlds has fallen, and there are probably few believers of the True Immortal left in this world.”

“Yet, while I’m trapped in this prison, the True Immortal suddenly bestowed upon me a method of escape…”

As he thought, Kou Hong’s eyes suddenly brightened.

He quickly lowered his head to conceal his inner excitement: “I understand. The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance destroyed the Federation of All Worlds. This enmity is irreconcilable!”

“As they say, the enemy of my enemy is my friend. Those imprisoned here are all sworn enemies of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. If I could release them all…”

Previous

Next

Chapter 1121: Overthrowing The Immortal Alliance In Prison

"Perhaps I can even convert them into believers of the True Immortal of Salvation!"

Looking at the countless prisoners around him who were emitting agonized wails under the torment of the formation's punishments, Kou Hong felt a deep empathy and fell into contemplation. "However, this is still a strictly guarded prison after all. To achieve this, I will need to plan carefully over the long term."

Kou Hong's eyes flickered as he once again used the same furious outburst to mask himself. But this time, he deliberately showed an intermittent and feeble demeanor, as if he could no longer hold on.

In the following period, Kou Hong used his apparent complete submission to divert the jailers' attention away from himself. Meanwhile, he frantically studied the detailed explanations of formations every day and secretly accumulated strength to enhance his own power.

Now he had finally realized it. That stream of warmth from the True Immortal of Salvation's inexplicable blessing, which had appeared in his body, was actually the cultivation that all cultivators dreamed of.

In conventional understanding, only through day-and-night painstaking cultivation would one accumulate this power, which served as the driving force for advancing realms. Yet at this moment, it was pouring into his body endlessly, like water without a source.

And because of this, his strength was advancing at a terrifying speed that defied imagination. In just half a month, he had directly broken through to the Golden Core Completion stage.

This made Kou Hong even more convinced that all these inconceivable miracles happening to him were absolutely the blessings of the True Immortal of Salvation! After all, it had taken him over two hundred years as a mortal cultivator to reach the mid-Golden Core stage! But now, breakthroughs happened in the blink of an eye.

Originally, Kou Hong thought that Golden Core Completion was already the limit. But what happened next completely shattered his cognition, making him understand what true immortal divine power truly meant!

It was well known that to break through from the Golden Core stage to the Nascent Soul stage, one needed to devour a Cave-Heaven of the Xuanhuang Realm. At this moment, Kou Hong was still trapped in the Nether Prison, so naturally, he could not meet this condition.

However, three days later, something that left Kou Hong utterly shocked occurred. Because as he circulated his spiritual energy and inspected his body, he actually discovered that within his dantian, a faint illusory scene of a Cave-Heaven had emerged!

Upon sensing it even more carefully, Kou Hong confirmed that this shadow of the Cave-Heaven was not an illusion. It truly existed! A Cave-Heaven was being born within his own body!

No need to say it; this too was the blessing of the True Immortal of Salvation. Kou Hong quickly accepted this fact with ease. Relying on this nascent Cave-Heaven, he formally broke through to the Nascent Soul stage one month later!

The various anomalies triggered by the realm breakthrough were perfectly concealed by the true immortal power surging within his body. It did not alert the jailers of the Nether Prison.

Kou Hong stared at his own hands, suspecting he was in a dream. The Nascent Soul stage, a realm he had only dared to dream of in the past, had been achieved by him so effortlessly.

"True Immortal of Salvation, I, Kou Hong, do not know what virtue or ability I possess to receive such profound favor from you. I vow to fulfill your entrustment, even at the cost of my life a thousand times over."

In the shadows, Kou Hong gazed at the countless towering structures of the Nether Prison around him, his eyes gradually filling with determination.

After initially entering the Nascent Soul stage, Kou Hong's strength continued to grow steadily upward every day on its own. But his main focus had now shifted to breaking the formation and rescuing the prisoners in the Nether Prison.

"With just me alone, accomplishing this will be quite difficult. But in this prison, where can I find helpers?" Kou Hong quietly observed his "neighbors" around him, selecting suitable candidates for collaboration.

Thus, he did not know how much time had passed. On this day, Li Fan looked at the message sent by Kou Hong and could not help but smile faintly. Yet he pretended not to see it and gave no response.

Kou Hong, unwilling to give up, tried a few more times. In the end, he had no choice but to abandon the effort helplessly. "Could it be that my judgment was wrong? It should not be."

Just as Kou Hong was in dire situation, a newly arrived prisoner immediately caught his attention. This man was not a member of the Federation of All Worlds; he had been imprisoned here for violating the laws of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

Judging by the crime he committed, he probably had no chance of getting out in this lifetime.

"Now you vermin want to die to atone for your sins at the drop of a hat. It's not that easy! I will make sure you cannot even beg for death, and spend the rest of your life rotting away in this sunless dark prison, full of worry!" The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance cultivator in charge of the escort said through gritted teeth.

The prisoner remained silent. But the look in his eyes toward the other man was full of disdain and killing intent. However, under the suppression of the cruel formation, it soon turned into cries of agony.

When people are in extreme pain, their instinctive words are always closest to their truest inner thoughts. After listening for several days to the words of this new neighbor, Kou Hong confirmed that he was indeed a suitable collaborator.

On this day, after waiting for him to be tortured until he was on the verge of death, Kou Hong quietly penetrated the formation and sent a message. The other man instantly opened his eyes, trying to locate Kou Hong's position. It was obviously in vain.

After some exchange between the two, Kou Hong learned his name. Zhang Fu.

The crime he committed was not a big deal; he had merely pocketed one-third of the Moling Prefecture's Alchemy Hall's annual profits for himself. In the past, even if the matter came to light, as long as he spent some contribution points and pulled some strings, he could settle it with money and be done with it.

But this time, unluckily, one of his archenemies somehow found out about it. He directly reported it to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters. The headquarters reacted unusually swiftly this time.

Before Zhang Fu could smooth things over, the envoy from headquarters had already arrived. After a brief investigation, the evidence was irrefutable, and he was captured on the spot. They did not even go through the usual procedure of a second review; they brought him straight to the dark prison and suppressed him.

Only then did Zhang Fu realize that he had been set up. He had thought he would be doomed forever, but unexpectedly, he encountered a turning point.

"Help release the criminals in the Nether Prison? This is a capital offense. Although everyone can reincarnate now, I heard before coming in that the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance seems to have reached some agreement with that Rebirth Heavenly Venerable. Those who commit unforgivable sins in the Alliance will lose the chance to reincarnate. They will truly die! Just like those members of the Federation of All Worlds before."

Unaware of Kou Hong's body shuddering violently not far away, Zhang Fu continued on his own: "Although being imprisoned here means no chance of ever getting out, it's still better than truly perishing. Who knows, maybe one day the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance will grant a great amnesty to the world?"

At first, Zhang Fu did not agree; he even secretly thought of reporting this schemer to atone for his crimes and make merit. But the next words from the other man made him change his mind.

"Here, death is a form of relief. If you don't believe me, just look at the people around you."

Previous

Next

Chapter 1122: Deploying The Great Formation Across The Worlds

Regarding the horrors of the Nether Prison, Zhang Fu had long heard about them.

And in the round of cruel torture he had just experienced, he also deeply felt the terror of the punishments here.

After hearing Kou Hong's words and personally witnessing several cultivators being tortured until they were raving mad and no longer human, he was very clear that he absolutely could not endure the tortures here. Thus, he decisively agreed to Kou Hong.

"If things really cannot be done, at worst I can go back on my word and sell this person out." Zhang Fu thought.

However, all his little schemes turned to nothing after he followed Kou Hong in reciting a vow.

"True Immortal of Salvation?" At first, Zhang Fu did not care about this so-called True Immortal, but later, whenever the thought of betrayal arose in his mind, an inexplicable sense of impending great disaster would always emerge.

Only then did Zhang Fu finally confirm that he had really boarded a pirate ship.

Next, Kou Hong and Zhang Fu slowly began their infiltration plan in the Nether Prison.

For every newly imprisoned prisoner, at the moment when they underwent inhumane torture and their consciousness was about to collapse, Kou Hong would always make contact at the right time.

Under the illumination of the True Immortal of Salvation's divine power, these prisoners joined the collective escape organization without any hesitation, as if grasping the last straw of salvation.

And those prisoners who had been imprisoned for a long time but still maintained basic self-consciousness became Kou Hong's primary targets for united front work.

However, these cultivators had been imprisoned for too long, becoming suspicious and distrustful of everyone. Each persuasion required a lot of time and effort.

Fortunately, the newly joined members also all participated.

Therefore, the progress of infiltrating the Nether Prison was not too slow.

Three years later.

Amid the incessant wails in the dark prison, the private exchanges of the escape organization's members were concealed.

"Brother Kou, how long do we have to wait?"

"Yes, except for the deepest part of the Nether Prison, other areas have basically been taken over by the brothers. On the surface, it seems still under the control of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. In reality, as soon as Brother Kou gives the order, the Nether Prison will instantly change hands."

"Over these years, with the protection of the True Immortal of Salvation's divine power, we have all recovered our strength. Some brothers have even advanced further. One Dao Integration, sixteen Soul Transformations, over a hundred Nascent Souls... In comparison, the defensive forces in the Nether Prison are simply no match."

"Hehehe, it's not just restoring cultivation. With the help of the True Immortal of Salvation's divine power, we can even bring in various supplies from the outside world silently. The delicacies I had been craving for a long time, I've almost eaten my fill of them recently."

...

During these three years, having witnessed too many miracles displayed by the True Immortal of Salvation, they had long become faithful believers of the True Immortal.

The discussion lasted for a long time before Kou Hong spoke: "Starting the uprising is easy, but what comes after escaping is more important. Once the Nether Prison is breached, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance will definitely react immediately and launch a large-scale search and capture."

"They won't leave us much time to respond, so before escaping, we need to plan the future arrangements well."

As soon as Kou Hong said this, the crowd was all stunned.

“The future arrangements?”

“Could it be the decree of the True Immortal of Salvation?”

Although they had joined the organization for three years already, they only knew of the existence of the True Immortal of Salvation and Mortal Compassion.

The true name of the organization, as well as its genuine purpose, were completely unknown to all of them.

Thus, they all looked toward Kou Hong with full anticipation.

“Indeed, it is the trial of the True Immortal of Salvation. Only by completing the trial can one truly become a believer of the True Immortal and a member of the organization.” Kou Hong's tone was filled with a sense of sacred mission.

Afterward, he transmitted a rather grand formation, along with a map, to the crowd.

“Heaven and Earth Myriad Spirits Great Formation?” The moment they saw the name of this formation, the cultivators in the Nether Prison all felt a slight tremor in their hearts.

According to the description, this formation possessed the ability to envelop the heavens and earth, and to connect with myriad realms.

The crowd then studied that map.

The map depicted the prefectures within the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's territory, but it was marked with over a hundred small dots.

When their divine senses focused on these small light spots, the crowd discovered with horror that each one represented a small world.

At this moment, Kou Hong also said: “The True Immortal's trial is actually not difficult. It merely requires spending some time.”

“Go to each small world and set up this Heaven and Earth Myriad Spirits Great Formation . Once each instance of the formation is completed, it will automatically connect, forming a formation network. Therefore, the moment it is built, we will all know of each other's progress...”

Kou Hong earnestly instructed them.

At the same time, he noticed the doubts of certain cultivators in the organization and explained: “Do not worry. These selected small worlds are all ones inhabited solely by mortals. With your strength, you can absolutely suppress them in a short time. The True Immortal of Salvation has also bestowed the divine artifact Heaven and Earth Furnace to aid you in transforming the small worlds...”

“One could say that the greatest trial this time is your perseverance and faith.”

Upon seeing the specific requirements of the trial task, the cultivators all fell into contemplation.

However, they had long been subtly influenced by the power of the True Immortal of Salvation, and considering that once they broke out of prison, they would inevitably face pursuit from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, hiding in mortal small worlds might not be a bad thing after all.

Thus, without hesitation, they all soon accepted it and began pondering the details of the plan.

After assigning the ownership of their respective small worlds, they also discussed in exhaustive detail how to handle various sudden situations after escaping the Nether Prison.

Three months later, when the Immortal Alliance jailers escorted another fugitive here and were preparing to throw him into a high pillar cage for suppression and torment.

He was horrified to see that in the Nether Prison, thousands upon thousands of pillars shattered in succession.

Countless vicious and ruthless prisoners, their eyes gleaming with ferocity, locked onto him all at once.

Under the onslaught of overwhelming baleful qi, he was scattered in body and soul on the spot.

Those who reacted and rushed over to attempt suppression; the mist-like jailers and Immortal Alliance cultivators, were all instantly torn to shreds by them.

“Charge out, do not linger!”

Kou Hong gave a low shout, and the crowd surged out of the Nether Prison like ferocious beasts released from their cages.

All the defensive formations were as if they did not exist.

The prisoners who saw the light of day again roared to vent the excitement in their hearts. But they did not stop in place because of this; instead, after simply identifying directions, they all shot toward the locations of their target small worlds.

In almost the blink of an eye, the dense horde of vicious criminals had scattered without a trace.

Not long after, the special envoys of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance were alarmed and led a team here to investigate.

Seeing the protective formations without any signs of forcible destruction, they revealed expressions of anger.

Teams of black-роbed cultivators followed the escape trails of the fugitives and set off in pursuit.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1123: Myriad Spirits Subduing True Immortal

As for the Nether Prison, after such a large-scale escape incident, it had become a name without substance.

After completing the transfer of the remaining prisoners, even the entrance to the Nether Prison was no longer concealed. It was thus abandoned.

Time slowly passed, and no one came here again. It was as if the world had forgotten it entirely.

After an unknown period, Li Fan's figure slowly appeared.

"Suppressed at the deepest part of the Nether Prison, even I could not see the face clearly."

"The transfer required three Dharma-Transmitters to come personally..."

"Interesting."

Instead of fleeing immediately with the group of escaped cultivators, Li Fan hid directly at the entrance. His goal was to explore the object imprisoned at the deepest part of the Nether Prison, which he had been unable to approach during those three years.

It was not that the formation at the deepest level was so profound that Li Fan could not break it, but rather that the jailers in charge there were three Dao Integration Immortal Sages working together.

It seemed they existed solely to suppress that one prisoner. Even when the Nether Prison above was breached and countless vicious criminals escaped, they remained unmoved and did not emerge to stop it.

When the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance's relief team arrived and began the transfer, Li Fan finally caught a glimpse of that prisoner. It vaguely felt familiar to Li Fan.

"Before the Myriad Spirits Great Formation is completed, it is not advisable to stir up more trouble."

"I will investigate it thoroughly in the next life."

"The only thing to do now is to bide my time and watch the world turn upside down!"

Li Fan gazed toward the distant horizon, as if he could see the Myriad Spirits Great Formation lighting up across over a thousand mortal worlds.

The Heaven and Earth Myriad Spirits Great Formation displayed to the group of vicious criminals was not the complete name of the formation.

This formation was derived from the Final Dissociation Disc over the course of three years. It represented the highest level of formation that the Dissociation Disc could derive in its current state.

Taking the previous Myriad Spirits Great Formation that covered the Spiritwood Realm, the Empire, and the Beast Realm as its prototype, it was further evolved into a shocking formation of the world.

The full name of the formation was [Heaven and Earth Myriad Spirits, All Beings Refine the Spirits] .

The primary function of this formation, and the very purpose for which Li Fan had gone to such lengths to free the prisoners from the Nether Prison and make them fight for him, was embodied in those four words: [All Beings Refine Spirits] .

It shared a similar principle with the All Beings Refine Spirita Formation that Song Hesong of Ningyuan City had used against Senior Brother Zhang in the Fallen Immortal Realm.

The killing power of the formation came from the intent of heaven and earth myriad spirits and countless beings.

However, after Li Fan's revised version... It was hundreds of billions of times stronger. Because the required materials to consume had increased by hundreds of billions of times.

Back then, Song Hesong, using the power of one city's mortals, had nearly forced Senior Brother Zhang into a desperate situation, compelling him to reveal the mystery of the Purple Core's immortality.

But Li Fan, using over a thousand small worlds and trillions of lives in the Xuanhuang Realm, aimed only to block the Faceless True Immortal for a moment.

After the Spiritwood Realm was annihilated by the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance, during those three years hiding in the Nether Prison, Li Fan had been constantly reflecting and reviewing the final world-destroying plan.

After all, this was the first time clashing with True Immortal power. Even if it was borrowing force to strike force and watching immortals fight from afar, it still carried endless risks.

These two were genuine immortals who had transcended the mortal world. Not stubborn Foundation Establishment cultivators.

If they were truly so easy to scheme against, how could they be worthy of the name True Immortal?

Once it failed, it would be a state of no return from endless calamities.

Especially after experiencing the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance's siege, which revealed an unexpected trump card and directly wiped out the Spiritwood Realm, Li Fan had become unprecedentedly cautious regarding the world-destroying plan.

He calculated every possible scenario into account. Thus, he discovered a problem that he had previously overlooked due to his assumptions.

In Li Fan's original plan, he would first draw upon the power of the Immortal Ruins Immortal, and then use this transcendent force beyond the mortal realm to provoke a reaction from the Faceless True Immortal.

Indeed, living beings always instinctively react to things that threaten them.

In the simulation and prediction, the Faceless True Immortal and the Immortal Ruins Immortal should first notice each other and engage in battle. While ignoring Li Fan, the true instigator of the matter.

But this was only in the ideal scenario. What if, after ascending to immortality, their thought patterns no longer resembled those of mortals?

Or, if they sensed the presence of [Truth] on Li Fan's body. Instead of immediately targeting their evenly matched opponent, they locked onto Li Fan first?

If no preparations were made, and if he were targeted by a True Immortal at that moment, he might not even have time to flee to the Dark Realm or forcibly activate [Truth] .

The more he thought about it, the more Li Fan found it hard to eat or sleep peacefully.

In the end, he decided that at the very least, he needed to find a method to buy him that sliver of time.

And the [Heaven and Earth Myriad Spirits, All Beings Refining Spirits] Great Formation was the final answer provided by the Dissociation Disc.

The gap between cultivators and mortals, or between True Immortals and cultivators, was as vast as a heavenly chasm.

But when the quantity reached a certain level, it could produce a terrifying qualitative change.

"Even a True Immortal, plunged into the endless sea of thoughts from trillions of living beings, would surely experience a moment of hesitation."

"This fleeting daze it buys me is my only chance of survival."

The power of a True Immortal could indeed destroy Xuanhuang in an instant.

However, if things developed as Li Fan predicted, after locking onto him, the True Immortal would not directly annihilate the Xuanhuang Realm just to capture this ant scheming in the shadows.

This would give Li Fan an opening to exploit.

"Heh, over a thousand mortal worlds, countless living beings' thoughts."

"What a colossal power this is. Yet no matter how I simulate it, it can only delay them by a mere instant."

"This absolute disparity in strength..."

"It truly makes one yearn for it."

Li Fan clasped his hands behind his back and fell silent.

With the Faceless True Immortal's power constraining them, those vicious prisoners would undoubtedly complete the construction of the great formation obediently.

Li Fan did not need to worry about that point.

And once the great formation was complete, it would no longer be something they could control.

"The day the All Beings Refining Spirits Formation is completed will be the day Xuanhuang meets its end."

"It is near, within a year."

During this period, Li Fan only needed to wait patiently. At the final juncture of this world, Li Fan still had some loose ends to tie up.

He first arrived in Tianling Prefecture, at the site of the original Five Elements Great Cave-Heaven.

Ever since the Five Elements Cave-Heaven, nurtured for who knows how many years, was devoured by Li Fan, a new Five Elements Cave-Heaven had begun to form in this formless void known as [Root of Heaven and Earth] .

However, this process was particularly slow. Only now had the embryonic form of the Cave-Heaven faintly begun to manifest.

Li Fan strolled through the void, circulating the False Misconversion Heavenly Dao Divine Ability to perceive the principles of heaven and earth.

Sensing the rhythm like a heartbeat, Li Fan narrowed his eyes slightly, carefully awaiting the prompt for [Truth] .

And from this innate wondrous treasure of his, Li Fan faintly sensed a thread of dissatisfaction.

Next

Chapter 1124: The Blood-Colored Great Formation Rises

Li Fan had sensed [Truth]'s emotions to some extent in the past.

The tremor when he first encountered the Eternal Relic, and the longing when facing Mr. Bai's skull.

Now, when Li Fan tried to "feed" [Truth] with the newly born Five Elements Cave-Heaven, [Truth] once again sent back feedback.

“Not entirely satisfied. In other words, barely acceptable.”

A glint of cold light flashed in Li Fan's eyes. Countless red threads flew out from his body's Cave-Heaven, like billions of tentacles, piercing into the void of the [Root of Heaven and Earth].

Seizing the ownership of the newborn Cave-Heaven from this maternal body.

The [Root of Heaven and Earth] was only responsible for simple gestation of Cave-Heavens. Thus, when this creation destined to detach from itself was slowly stripped away by Li Fan, it did not provoke any intense resistance.

On the contrary, the newborn Cave-Heaven itself seemed to sense something.

It exerted every effort to emit strands of resisting power, attempting to halt Li Fan's devouring.

But how could a newborn infant compare to a sturdy adult brother?

It was inevitable.

Amid continuous faint rumbling sounds, the newborn Cave-Heaven was pulled into the illusory shadow of the vast Five Elements Great Cave-Heaven, which resembled a backdrop painting.

Until it was completely engulfed and vanished.

“Buzz buzz buzz...”

A series of sharp ringing sounds echoed in Li Fan's ears, and the scene before his eyes seemed to dim for an instant.

Once everything returned to normal, Li Fan looked at his [Truth] panel.

“Current available anchor count: 3”

“It increased by one. As expected, this works. After devouring Golden Core Laws failed to satisfy [Truth]'s appetite, the next step could only be a higher-grade Nascent Soul Cave-Heaven.”

Having verified his conjecture and gained another usable anchor for [Truth], Li Fan did not feel particularly joyful.

Instead, he kept savoring the emotion [Truth] had just conveyed.

It was tasteless to eat, yet a pity to discard. But under Li Fan's insistence, [Truth] ultimately devoured the newborn Cave-Heaven.

“Now it's Cave-Heavens. So what will it want next? Small worlds, cultivation realms? Or the upcoming Heaven-Earth Transformation and Heaven-Earth Spirits from the new method system?”

“Regardless, it's time to curb its appetite a bit. Being picky is not a good habit.”

In the dim void space, Li Fan stood alone in the emptiness.

After pondering for a long time, he slowly departed.

Emerging from the [Root of Heaven and Earth], Li Fan next headed to the location of the blood-colored long river.

Thanks to the lingering protection of the Fishing Pond's remnant power, this place still remained undiscovered by the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance. The surviving cultivators in this outpost were the last remnants of the Federation of All Worlds.

They had long learned of the total annihilation of the Spiritwood Realm headquarters. Amid grief and fury, not a single one wavered in their faith in the True Immortal of Salvation and Mortal Compassion.

Because they could still sense, in meditation, the Compassionate True Immortal's omnipresent, magnificent divine power.

The cultivators Li Fan had dispatched into the blood-colored long river to seek historical fragments were mostly hot-blooded young people. Their cultivation did not exceed the early Golden Core Lord stage at most.

With the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance's overwhelming might, though their thirst for revenge burned fiercely, they knew they were no match.

Thus, they could only go into hiding first, secretly accumulating strength.

Without headquarters support and stripped of their former dependencies, they pinned their hopes for vengeance on that vast Blood-Colored Long River.

Clinging to firm conviction and ignoring the toll on their lifespans, they threw themselves into it time and again.

Attempting to probe the mysteries within.

In three years, of the original hundred-plus people, only thirty-three survived.

Moreover, these survivors all suffered massive losses to their lifespan.

Young as they were, they had all turned white-haired and decrepit, resembling doddering elders.

Yet they were not entirely without gains.

Li Fan studied the insights they had compiled.

“Compared to those driven purely by self-interest, those bearing bone-deep hatred are better able to maintain clarity of mind while exploring the blood-colored long river.”

“The projection of the blood-colored long river seems to exhibit a kind of homophily in its attractions. For instance, our group of avengers more easily observes those cultivators from history who similarly harbored sky-high grudges.”

“Beyond mere recorded images, it may even be possible to truly summon things from history into existence. This appears to be a manifestation of the power of the [blood-colored long river]. However, the trigger probability is far too low; since the founding of [Seeking the Hidden], this phenomenon has occurred only once. We are currently researching the conditions required to achieve the goal...”

Li Fan recorded all of this extremely valuable research material.

At the same time, he memorized the name of the cultivator who had diligently compiled and documented these results.

Chen Chen.

“Not bad. Earlier, among the group, this person was unremarkable. Only now, in the desperate straits of the federation's downfall, has he finally revealed his leadership abilities.”

“A talent worth cultivating. In the next life, I can promote him earlier. Hmm, let me see which small world he came from...”

After achieving his objectives at the blood-colored long river, Li Fan did not appear to disturb them.

Instead, he let the group continue their own mission.

After all, a year later, the outcome would be the same regardless.

Li Fan quietly departed and made a round of visits to places like the Great Li Realm, the Empire, the Dark Realm, and many others.

He harvested the results and repeatedly tested and verified his escape plan.

...

The river of time surged onward.

On this day, as Li Fan tinkered with a Tianxuan small mirror, he suddenly raised his head and gazed into the distance.

The first Heaven and Earth Myriad Spirits Great Formation had been completed.

In an instant, Li Fan's gaze pierced through layer upon layer of spatial barriers, arriving at the mortal world where the great formation stood.

The cultivator responsible for constructing the great formation here felt the cultivation reward bestowed by the Compassionate True Immortal within his body and revealed an expression of excitement.

Guided by an inexplicable intuition, he did not hesitate in the slightest and immediately activated the great formation.

This mortal world was somewhat larger in scale than Li Fan's homeland, Great Xuan.

But as the Heaven and Earth Myriad Spirits Great Formation activated, the originally clear blue sky suddenly took on a trace of eerie blood-red hue.

The blood-red swirled back and forth in the sky like a paintbrush, ultimately forming a grotesque and terrifying blood-colored giant eye.

The giant eye coldly regarded the teeming masses below, like the eye of a god.

In that moment, an inexplicable surge of extreme terror rose in the hearts of all living beings between heaven and earth.

Under this instinctive fear, their bodies trembled uncontrollably, and they collapsed to the ground.

Even though nothing had happened yet, an aura of despair had already begun to spread across the land.

Of course, there were those who dared to resist this blood-colored giant eye.

But all they could do was point to the heavens and curse, raging impotently.

And the cultivator who had single-handedly forged this world's Heaven and Earth Myriad Spirits Great Formation stood there in a daze, staring at the blood-colored giant eye in the sky.

He muttered to himself: “No, that's not right. How could it be like this?”
