Chapter 1125: Crossing Calamity, Yet Unable To Cross One’S Own

"The Great Formation of Heaven and Earth's Myriad Spirits communicates with the myriad realms of the heavens. How could such a bizarre change occur after the formation is complete? There must be something wrong somewhere."

A surge of extreme unease welled up in his heart, and this cultivator was just preparing to shut down the formation. But... It was already too late.

For some unknown reason, the formation suddenly began operating on its own. The blood-red giant eye slowly opened, revealing a strange vertical pupil.

Boom! The entire mortal world trembled violently.

Countless beams of blood light rose from the ground, like pillars of light, converging into the blood-red giant eye in the sky. After a moment of stunned silence, once he figured out what had just happened, the cultivator's face filled with endless despair and terror.

Because the instant the blood-red vertical pupil appeared, all living beings in this mortal world were killed in a flash. They turned into blood light and were absorbed by the blood-red pupil.

The cultivator's body trembled uncontrollably, an endless chill enveloping his heart. Although he had committed no small sins in the past, compared to the act of instantly slaughtering an entire world's beings at this moment, it was not even on the same level.

"We were all deceived. What Compassionate True Immortal..."

"This is clearly an utterly terrifying evil god!"

Scenes from the past four years flooded his mind, and an indescribable fear gradually swallowed the cultivator. But at this moment, the last vestige of conscience in his heart suddenly awakened him from despair.

A spark of hope burst in the cultivator's eyes; he suddenly remembered something: "No, I cannot let the others fall into the same trap as me. I must hurry and notify them..."

The cultivator was about to flee this place in a panic. Yet he discovered that the blood-red vertical pupil in the sky had, at some point, locked onto him.

And just as the cultivator felt a chill throughout his body, convinced he would die under the blood-red giant eye. A breeze seemed to sweep through heaven and earth, and then a phantom inexplicably manifested.

It instantly drew the gaze of the blood-red vertical pupil in the sky. Meanwhile, far away in the Xuanhuang Realm, Li Fan's eyes narrowed slightly. He was no stranger to that phantom.

"Mr. Bai?"

"Was it because too many living beings were killed at once, causing the Immortal-Mortal Miasma to erupt violently in a short time and trigger a counteraction?"

The phantom stood between heaven and earth. Although its facial expression could not be seen clearly, boundless anger could be felt emanating from it. The phantom's gaze swept over this heaven and earth, then locked onto the sole survivor here.

That is, the cultivator who had set up the formation. The cultivator immediately sensed he could not move, a crisis of death surging into his heart. He struggled to say something.

The phantom did not listen to his explanation at all. A ripple instantly crossed a thousand miles, descending from the phantom onto the cultivator's body.

The cultivator's body suddenly went rigid. Then strands of his hair turned from black to white, his skin wrinkled like withered wood... In the blink of an eye, the cultivator transformed from a middle-aged man into a decrepit elder on the verge of death.

And then, he completely turned into a skeleton. He fell from the air.

After completing this act of vengeance, Mr. Bai's phantom gazed at the empty world, now devoid of any other living beings. A trace of sorrow appeared on his face. He cast one last reluctant glance, then dissipated with the wind.

...

In the Xuanhuang Realm, Li Fan shook his head slightly. "I never expected the Immortal-Mortal Miasma to have this kind of form."

"No wonder the cultivators of the Xuanhuang Realm quickly agreed to the proposal for mortal migration back then. They must have discovered this secret."

"Debts have their owners, and grievances their culprits. Go find whoever set up the formation; it has nothing to do with me." Li Fan said with a cold laugh. "It even saves me the trouble of cleaning up myself."

The passing of one thug did not attract the attention of the other thugs. As one great formation after another was established and joined the Spirits-Refining Network. Li Fan grew ever closer to his final goal in this lifetime.

He gazed at the countless sentient beings' consciousnesses in the Spirits-Refining Formation. Even as the mastermind behind this, self-proclaimed to have attained Truth and thus qualified to disregard all things in the world. But he still felt a chill in his heart and dared not stare at it for long.

“myriad power of all living beings; truly terrifying and awe-inspiring.”

“I suspect that even a True Immortal would pale in comparison amid this sea of sentient beings.”

Li Fan's mind, as unyielding as millennial ice, actually wavered ever so slightly in that moment. But soon, his gaze hardened once more.

“All of this is necessary. With the immortal path severed, even figures like the Immortal Ancestor, Xuantian King, and Mr. Bai are trapped here forever. Only by finding the true path to liberation can we resolve everything at last. Otherwise, even if I do nothing, the ultimate fate awaiting the entire Xuanhuang Realm is destruction.”

...

Li Fan's gaze grew utterly cold once more. Under his directives, the construction of the Myriad Spirits Great Formation accelerated further. In the end, this supreme great formation, spanning numerous mortal worlds, took full shape.

And just then, five paper figures from the Du'e Sect appeared opportunely at Li Fan's side. This time, however, they did not nag at him. Instead, they all turned their gazes toward the skies of the Xuanhuang Realm.

“How strange. How did things get so out of control and speed up just from a quick nap?”

“Could it be that the suction from the Immortal Ruins has intensified again?”

“For so many years, nothing this peculiar has happened. The changes were subtle; we didn't notice right away...”

The five little figures chattered among themselves, utterly ignoring Li Fan's presence. After careful observation, Li Fan realized they were not the same ones who had occasionally emerged before to admonish him.

These were entirely new figures, ones who had never appeared prior. They seemed to hold high positions in the Du'e Sect, overseeing the crises of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Li Fan had no intention of communicating with them. Instead, he directly activated the formation, forcibly drawing them back into the Book of Du'e.

“Hm?” This unusual disturbance quickly alerted the five little figures. They stared at Li Fan, their expressions slowly turning to horror.

“So you've been... all along...”

“This is bad!”

The Du'e Book trembled violently, its pages flipping wildly without wind. It seemed as if a horde of paper figures was about to pour forth from it. Yet before they could achieve anything, under the constraint of an invisible force, those five little figures were dragged forcibly back into the Du'e Book.

The book then slowly closed. It still twitched and resisted ceaselessly. Li Fan's expression remained unchanged as he continuously reinforced the sealing formation. Until the Du'e Book finally fell completely still.

“Since you've chosen to be a turtle hiding in its shell, then stay that way forever. The Xuanhuang Realm has no need of your salvation.”

Li Fan said flatly, suppressing the Du'e Book in the deepest recesses of the Five Elements Cave-Heaven. The Heaven and Earth Ten Thousand Spirits and All Beings Spirits-Refining Great Formation was now fully arrayed.

With Li Fan's final safeguard in place, he was ready to prepare for the ultimate plan. “From my observations over this past year, I've confirmed that the Immortal Ancestor is not in the Xuanhuang Realm. Just like before.”

Chapter 1126: Indeed Before The Immortal

Nine Mountains Prefecture

"Today is a good day." Zhao Xian wrote his full name on the golden contract in front of him. As a strand of golden light flew into his body and the contract was established, he laughed heartily with joy.

The group from the Five Elders Association in the shop put away the contract and also showed joyful expressions, chiming in agreement.

For the rest of the afternoon, Zhao Xian negotiated more detailed terms on the trade agreement with the Five Elders Association. Only after sending them to the welcoming Cave-Heaven in the city for rest did Zhao Xian finally let out a long sigh of relief.

He did not return to his own Cave-Heaven but instead turned back to the shop and entered the underground secret chamber.

Looking at the Faceless Stone Statue hidden in the darkness, Zhao Xian's expression was complicated, his thoughts swirling endlessly.

Ever since the Federation of All Worlds was exterminated by the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance and placed on the permanent kill list, Zhao Xian, as a hidden agent of the Federation in Nine Mountains Prefecture, had lived in constant fear.

Every day, he was restless, often hallucinating that the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance had come knocking and suddenly arrested him.

Without the Federation supplying materials, the shop's stock had been greatly affected. Foot traffic declined daily, and it almost could not hold on. In the end, he begged and pleaded everywhere, using up all the family assets he had accumulated over the years, to barely rebuild the supply chain.

"Good thing everything is over now." Zhao Xian thought to himself.

The storm of the Federation had subsided.

During the days when the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance's search was most intense, they had not discovered Zhao Xian's true identity. There should be no danger in the future either.

After several years of his painstaking management, the shop's business had returned to normal. Recently, he had even reached a commercial agreement with a merchant team from the Five Elders Association. Just the fixed trade goods each year were worth over ten million contribution points.

"Although there are no more rewards from the True Immortal, the shop's profits alone are enough to support my cultivation all the way to the Soul Transformation realm."

"With the Federation destroyed, I no longer have to hand over profits; they all go straight into my pocket. I'm living even more comfortably than before..." Zhao Xian thought with some pride.

"Indeed, fortune and misfortune are intertwined; the ways of the world are unpredictable."

Finally glancing once more at the faceless stone statue, Zhao Xian had already decided in his heart to seal this underground secret chamber permanently.

From now on, the secret of his past as a member of the Federation of All Worlds would never be known to anyone.

"The True Immortal is merciful and surely will not blame me." Zhao Xian respectfully performed one last salute, then sealed the secret chamber shut.

After completing all this, Zhao Xian felt as if a heavy burden had been lifted, reborn anew.

But on the way back to his cave mansion, as he looked up at the azure sky of the Xuanhuang Realm, a sudden wave of unease surged in his heart for no reason.

Zhao Xian had always trusted his instincts highly, so he immediately stopped in his tracks.

Carefully recalling every detail of his recent life, after pondering for a long time, Zhao Xian still could not find anything amiss.

"Strange..."

The chaotic feeling in his heart grew stronger. Suddenly sensing something, Zhao Xian looked toward the people around him.

At first glance, it made cold sweat break out instantly.

It was not just him.

In the free trade city of Nine Mountains Prefecture, at this moment, every cultivator strolling on the streets seemed to vaguely sense something wrong. Their hearts were in turmoil, their expressions tense.

Some even had cold sweat beading on their foreheads, their steps unsteady.

"This..." An image suddenly flashed in Zhao Xian's mind: ants collectively relocating before a storm hits.

"Could it be that a calamity spanning the heavens is about to occur?"

Zhao Xian suddenly recalled the origin of this Nine Mountains Trade Prefecture.

Originally, this place was a chaotic domain of dragons and snakes, with nine mountains standing in contention. Because of the clash between the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance and the Five Elders Association, a glance from a Immortal Ancestor's Dharma Manifestation turned mountains and rivers into plains.

That was how the prosperous Nine Mountains Prefecture of today came to be.

"Could it be that something like that day is about to happen again?" As this thought arose in Zhao Xian, he could not help but shiver.

Although reason told him that, whether it was the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance or the Five Elders Association, for the sake of both sides' interests, neither would start a war again in Nine Mountains Prefecture.

But the abnormality before his eyes made him certain: a disaster was definitely about to happen!

"Run!"

Just a moment later, Zhao Xian made his decision.

Yes, he loved money.

He also wanted to become a Soul Transformation True Monarch, dominating a region.

But all of that presupposed surviving with his life intact.

With the abilities he had honed in Nine Mountains Prefecture over these years, even if he returned to poverty and started over somewhere else, he could rise again.

Moreover, the most valuable items in the shop were always kept on his person.

Thinking of this, Zhao Xian immediately turned direction. Ignoring the city ban, he sped toward the border of Nine Mountains Prefecture.

His unusual behavior naturally drew the attention of the cultivators in Nine Mountains City. As if triggering a chain reaction without reason, they too began fleeing together.

Just like the instinctive struggle of wild beasts sensing a life-or-death crisis.

And facts proved that these cultivators' premonitions were not wrong.

They had not fled far when a terrifying fluctuation descended from behind.

The sky instantly darkened.

Countless cultivators did not even have time to cry out before they were swallowed by an unknown horrific existence.

Feeling the thousands upon thousands of auras vanishing in an instant behind him, extreme terror seized his mind.

In this moment, his thoughts raced at an unprecedented speed.

"There must be some terrible disaster descending on Nine Mountains Prefecture."

"With my strength, it is obvious I cannot survive this disaster."

"Now, there is still a long distance to the border of Nine Mountains Prefecture. There is not enough time to escape to a safe place."

"The reason Nine Mountains Prefecture has no teleportation formation is because the spatial structure here is extremely unstable. Activating a teleportation rashly would not only fail to reach the target location but instead randomly send one elsewhere. It is even possible to get lost in the void forever..."

"But there is still some hope of survival..."

Zhao Xian's thoughts raced, and his body reacted instinctively.

He directly activated the random teleportation formation diagram in his storage ring.

The surrounding scene twisted and blurred.

After a brief dizziness, Zhao Xian escaped Nine Mountains Prefecture and arrived in an unfamiliar place.

But the scene before his eyes left him with no joy of escaping death.

Instead, it was as if he had fallen into an icy abyss, his mind blank.

It was as if he were floating high in the sky, overlooking the Xuanhuang Realm.

On the earth below, over a thousand black spots had densely appeared.

Like pitch-black abysses, they swallowed everything around them.

The entire world seemed about to be torn apart by these black spots, forming curled fragments of paper.

The cultivators on the earth, no matter how they struggled, could not persist for even a moment in the black dark domains.

They all turned into twisted rays of light and vanished into the black spots.

...

"So the disaster is not just happening in Nine Mountains Prefecture?"

"What is this?"

"Is the apocalypse coming, and the world about to be destroyed?" Zhao Xian stood stunned.

But soon, his attention was drawn to another wondrous sight that contrasted with this apocalyptic scene.

Above the Xuanhuang Realm, which had been torn into over a thousand twisted sheets of paper in an instant, a painting suddenly appeared, as if countless pigments had been splashed randomly.

In the ink-splashed painting, countless scenes changed rapidly with every instant.

And in front of this bizarre painting stood a figure with hands behind their back.

Seeming to sense Zhao Xian's distant gaze, that figure turned their head.

Before he could even see the other's face clearly, Zhao Xian's body and soul, unable to withstand the immense pressure, exploded into countless fragments.

Dead without an intact corpse.

"Hmm?"

A moment later, Li Fan already knew why this unlucky fellow had come here.

He shook his head slightly, paying no more attention to him, and instead focused on the countless transformations of heaven and earth surging out from the Xuanhuang Realm before his eyes.

Those countless black spots were naturally the immortal power drawn from the Immortal Ruins by the bait puppets after he activated the world-destroying plan, flying toward the Immortal Ruins.

The power of the immortals in the Immortal Ruins, following the connection sensed through those puppets, was transmitted into the Xuanhuang Realm.

Causing boundless world-extinguishing catastrophe.

And as Li Fan expected, the moment the immortal power from the Immortal Ruins appeared, it triggered a spontaneous reaction from the Faceless True Immortal statues placed by Li Fan beside the bait puppets.

These Faceless True Immortals had no physical forms. They were purely condensed from the source power essence of the Faceless True Immortal by Li Fan.

When the Immortal Ruins' world-destroying power swept toward them.

An indescribable force inexplicably surged from the source power essence.

Like a butterfly emerging from its cocoon, or a flower wilting to reveal fruit.

The source power essence melted away like ice and snow, vanishing directly. In its place, a power beyond mortal comprehension collided with the Immortal Ruins' immortal power.

Like two massive waves crashing against each other, splashing forth myriad droplets.

In an instant, the Xuanhuang Realm tore into over a thousand fragments.

But it seemed that precisely because of this clash between the two forces, the already riddled Xuanhuang Realm did not shatter completely in that very moment.

“It seems my worries were unnecessary after all.”

So thought Li Fan.

“Extract the essence of heaven and earth to obtain its spirit.”

Muttering incantations, he drew the bizarre ink-wash painting before him entirely into the phantom of the Five Elements Great Cave-Heaven behind him.

Before Li Fan's eyes, scenes of the Xuanhuang Realm's past, present, and the trajectories of what might have been in the future played out like a lantern show.

Beyond that, Li Fan sensed an intensely strong desire for unwilling survival.

The images spun like a vortex, as if something was about to be born from within.

Upon careful contemplation, Li Fan actually detected the Xuanhuang Realm's intent to beg for cooperation.

“A world on the verge of death is no different from a person.”

“Having clearly discovered that I was falsely impersonating the Heavenly Dao, yet for the sake of a possible slim chance of survival, it not only does not blame me but is even willing to cooperate with me...”

“This is something I had not anticipated before.”

Li Fan snorted coldly and did not agree to the Xuanhuang Realm's request.

Nor did he directly refuse.

Instead, he accelerated the speed at which he extracted the essence of heaven and earth.

As countless impending changes and transformation merged into his body, the Five Elements Great Cave-Heaven began to undergo earth-shaking transformations.

In stark contrast to the Xuanhuang Realm, which had reached its end times, the interior of the Five Elements Great Cave-Heaven presented an unprecedented scene of prosperity.

As the five elements cycled and flowed, they tended toward fusion.

Metal, wood, water, fire, and earth united as one; the once starkly evident barriers between the various cave-heavens slowly ceased to exist.

A brand-new world, brimming with boundless vitality, was taking shape.

...

At this moment, Li Fan, on the verge of successfully advancing to the Soul Transformation realm, inexplicably felt a trace of danger.

A beam of light arrived from beyond the void in an instant.

“No!”

After clearly seeing the current state of the Xuanhuang Realm, it let out a roar of fury.

Then, in a flash, it crossed the vast spatial distance and locked onto Li Fan.

Their four eyes met, and both faces showed some astonishment.

Li Fan was astonished because he had originally thought the one returning would be the Immortal Ancestor.

Yet he had not expected that the figure appearing in this life...

Would be the Heavenly Doctor.

And the Heavenly Doctor was astonished because Li Fan, the nominal last successor of the Heavenly Law Realm, had no idea why he was here.

Moreover, he was mercilessly extracting the essence of heaven and earth from the Xuanhuang Realm.

“You've returned quite quickly.” Li Fan sighed inwardly.

“[Truth].”

Without any hesitation, Li Fan immediately intoned it in his mind.

Under the influence of the Immortal Ruins' immortal power, Li Fan sensed that the True Immortal Net's restraints on him had loosened.

The Heavenly Doctor had also discovered him.

So he no longer coveted more and directly activated [Truth].

The surrounding scenes abruptly turned illusory.

At that moment, Li Fan clearly saw the shock on the Heavenly Doctor's face, as well as the sudden burst of light in his turbid eyes.

“What has he discovered?” Li Fan's heart tightened abruptly.

But the scenery around him had already nearly come to a standstill.

Even someone as powerful as the Heavenly Doctor appeared to move with excruciating slowness in Li Fan's eyes.

A thick fog enveloped all sights.

All things in the world were about to turn real into false, reality into illusion.

At that very moment...

From the myriad black points of the Xuanhuang Realm, a brilliant radiance suddenly erupted.

The Immortal Ruins' immortal power, capable of easily tearing the Xuanhuang Realm apart, was actually suppressed in this instant.

In the midst of all the frozen scenes, only this splash of brilliance was least affected.

Even in Li Fan's view, it still moved in slow motion.

But it still surged toward Li Fan at an extremely fast speed.

“The Faceless True Immortal!”

Li Fan's heart felt as if it had been viciously gripped by a hand.

The threat from the Heavenly Doctor was instantly cast aside.

“You have finally discovered me after all...”

“Heaven and Earth Myriad Spirits, All Beings Refine the Spirits!”

A blood-colored giant net unfurled around Li Fan.

Layer upon layer, dense and intricate.

It was unmistakably composed of countless ferocious human faces.

The red giant net rose from Li Fan and expanded wildly toward the surroundings.

The beings within the net, billions, trillions, all simultaneously emitted deathbed wails.

That brilliant radiance ignored the red giant net and advanced unyieldingly toward Li Fan.

Li Fan had never felt that activating [Truth] took so long.

As the brilliant white light was about to reach right before him.

His body involuntarily stiffened and grew ice-cold.

Even his thoughts began to freeze.

Fortunately...

The red giant net did provide that slight hindering effect after all.

In the instant before the brilliant radiance locked onto Li Fan.

Li Fan successfully activated [Truth].

All scenes became illusory.

Only that splash of brilliant white light remained dazzling.

...

Anchor: 1 year.

Jieli Mountain Peak.

Having escaped death by a hair's breadth, Li Fan was drenched in sweat from head to toe, gasping for breath in great heaves.

“This is the might of a True Immortal?”

“Even the thoughts of trillions of beings are so pale and powerless before it.”

As Li Fan thought this, he suddenly felt that his body seemed somewhat abnormal.

At this moment, he was not viewing things with his eyes, because...

Li Fan reached up to touch his face, and his body trembled slightly.

His face had vanished without a trace.

Chapter 1127: Immortal Power Turns To Nothingness

[This simulation ends.]

[You can choose one of the following options to retain:

An item you held during this simulation.,

Your cultivation realm from this simulation.,

The simulation memory of one person closely related to you. This memory can be inherited by that person.,

Forfeit the above choices to accelerate the recharge progress.],

...

The prompt for [Truth] appeared "before" Li Fan's eyes, but now he had no mind to make a choice.

Instead, he was sensing the changes happening to his body.

Just like when he had previously come into contact with the Immortal Ruins immortal power, even after [Truth], he still felt an overwhelming hunger.

This time, under the radiant immortal power of the Faceless True Immortal, Li Fan, who had returned in a sorry state through [Truth], also failed to completely shake off the corrosion of the True Immortal's power with just one [Truth].

"The side effect brought by the Immortal Ruins immortal is a hunger that wants to devour everything around. As for the Faceless True Immortal..."

Li Fan carefully groped his face.

It was as if a layer of skin had been draped over it, covering all his facial features.

Only touch remained.

Although divine sense assisted in perception and did not affect normal actions or daily life.

It still made Li Fan feel a slight chill in his heart.

"Not only has my face disappeared, but also..."

Li Fan used his divine sense to "look" at his body.

It unexpectedly showed a faint transparency from time to time, like a ghost.

"My [existence] is also gradually disappearing."

He had just returned to Anchor Year 1, and [Truth] was still recharging. If he died now, it would truly be death, with no chance to start over.

Faced with such a desperate situation, Li Fan did not panic at all.

Instead, he thought rapidly with utmost calm.

The experience of previously using several [Truth] to thoroughly dilute the influence of the Immortal Ruins immortal power made Li Fan understand that the influence the Faceless True Immortal had imposed on him this time should similarly be resolved through multiple [Truth].

This did not mean that [Truth]'s ability was starting to fall short when facing a True Immortal. Rather, Li Fan himself was too weak.

But now several key problems lay before Li Fan.

"First, [Truth] requires recharging. And before that, if I do not do something, I am afraid I will not last that long."

"Second, under the influence of the Faceless True Immortal's radiant immortal power, I have become like this. Compared to my previous life as a True Immortal's emissary, the influence on me now is even deeper. In other words..."

"From the very beginning of this life, I have already fallen into the True Immortal's net. Based on the final experiences of my previous life, even if I trigger the myriad Transformations of changes in Xuanhuang, it may not necessarily attract the Faceless True Immortal's attention. But once I activate [Truth], it will inevitably draw Him here."

...

Li Fan walked out of the wooden hut on the mountaintop and came to the edge of the boundless sea of clouds.

Looking at the red sun rising at the horizon, under the sunlight, Li Fan felt no warmth at all.

Instead, a thread of uncontrollable chill emerged in his heart.

Li Fan sat quietly by the cliff, thinking seriously.

At the same time, it seemed as if he were waiting for something.

Until the sun reached midday, a full half day passed, and Great Xuan Jieli Mountain remained calm and peaceful.

No accidents occurred.

Only then did Li Fan let out a long breath, a feeling of narrowly escaping disaster surging in his heart.

"The Faceless True Immortal did not come."

He stood up and patted the dust off his body.

Feeling the increasingly ethereal body, Li Fan gazed at the light screen “before his eyes,” where the [Truth] prompts continued to flicker incessantly.

He still made no choice and simply ignored it for the time being.

Returning to the wooden cabin at the mountain peak, he lay on the bed and pondered.

“What is the essence of [Truth]?”

“When the true becomes false, the false becomes true.”

“For a bug like me, who appeared without warning in the True Immortal Net, the Faceless True Immortal chose to turn a blind eye. Either it means He cannot detect the specific situation in the Xuanhuang Realm's small world. Or...”

“Before this, the world did not exist. The moment I returned, time began to flow, and everything truly gained meaning. From my perspective, it was due to the karma of my previous life that I suddenly appeared in the True Immortal Net. But for that Faceless True Immortal, I have always existed within the True Immortal Net. So there is no anomaly at all. That is why He has not descended.”

Countless thoughts churned in Li Fan's mind.

“If it were a reversal of time, then the Faceless True Immortal at this current time node should keenly sense the changes happening to Himself. For a True Immortal, to have something occur beyond His control—He would certainly not overlook such an anomaly and would trace the thread right to the door.”

“But none of that has happened.”

Li Fan repeated in his heart once more the seven words that accompanied each appearance of [Truth].

“When the true becomes false, the false becomes true.”

“Turning the illusory into the real, reorganizing all things. With such a divine object at my side, yet trapped in this wretched state—it truly does not add up.” Li Fan shook his head and said with self-mockery.

In the end, he had somewhat underestimated the power of a True Immortal.

He had not expected that even after activating [Truth], that brilliant immortal power could still trace back to its source.

Moreover, it seemed that the stronger the existence, the more sensitive it was to the power of [Truth].

The Faceless True Immortal's sudden outburst, suppressing the Immortal Ruins' immortal power and rushing over in haste, clearly indicated that He knew something about [Truth].

“The manifestation of [Truth] is closely tied to my own strength. Before advancing to the Longevity Immortal Realm, it is best not to provoke Them.”

So Li Fan thought.

After making this interim speculation and summary about the True Immortals and [Truth], Li Fan finally began to consider how to resolve the current crisis.

“I am now at the third anchor point. [Truth], as a Foundation Establishment artifact, and I'm at late Foundation Establishment cultivation. If I choose to inherit my previous life's cultivation, it would instantly complete the transformation from the Foundation Establishment stage to the Soul Transformation stage.”

“But I am currently within the True Immortal Net. The reason I have not attracted the Faceless True Immortal's attention is that my own strength is negligible. But if a Foundation Establishment ant instantly becomes a Soul Transformation ant, it might draw His gaze...”

“So the method to resolve this life's crisis is clear. Hide in the Dark Realm, and at the moment of inheriting my previous life's cultivation, activate [Truth] once more. Avoid the Faceless True Immortal's further corruption, while using [Truth] to wash away the mark He left on me. Repeat this process.”

“But before that...”

Li Fan reached out, intending to pick up a teacup placed in the wooden cabin.

At first, there was no issue, but as his body became somewhat transparent, the teacup slipped from his hand and fell directly to the ground.

“So this is the power of the Faceless True Immortal. Existence itself being stripped away...”

What Li Fan needed to address first was finding a way to stabilize his own existence before [Truth] finished recharging.

Otherwise, all would be for naught.

And from his current physical condition, the time left to Li Fan was not much.

Chapter 1128: Heavenly Emperor Qi As Anchor

Fortunately, Li Fan was no longer the mortal Grand Preceptor who had just entered the cultivation world back then.

After numerous reincarnations, he had already grasped far too many secrets of the cultivation world.

Scenes from the past flashed rapidly through his mind, and soon he found a feasible countermeasure.

"To minimize the chance of the Faceless True Immortal noticing, it's best if my own cultivation realm doesn't undergo drastic changes. But I still need to increase my strength as much as possible, that is, my 'existence' in the mortal world..."

"Heavenly Emperor Qi Classic."

A secret manual not belonging to the Xuanhuang Realm instantly appeared in Li Fan's sea of consciousness.

This was a peerless technique that Senior Stone Tablet had fished out from the Fishing Pond.

Senior Stone Tablet had shown no interest in it at the time and casually tossed it to Li Fan.

Yet now, it had become the perfect lifesaver for Li Fan.

The Heavenly Emperor Qi Classic allowed one to become a worldly emperor, condensing the qi fortune of the masses and the dynasty. Without needing to cultivate, one could become a powerhouse comparable to the Longevity Immortal Realm.

Though becoming a Longevity Immortal powerhouse required the successive efforts of hundreds of years and over a dozen generations, Li Fan had no intention of reaching the highest realm of the Heavenly Emperor Qi Classic.

He only needed to condense the qi fortune of the masses to help him tide over this current crisis.

"No time like the present. Late Foundation Establishment cultivation is already enough to sweep the world clean."

Li Fan stood at the peak of Jieli Mountain, gazing into the distance toward Xuan Capital City.

His figure drifted away, vanishing in an instant.

...

Three days later, a piece of news spread like wildfire across the land.

The Great Xuan Emperor was muddle-headed and tyrannical, inciting the wrath of both heaven and man.

Fortunately, the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor had descended, slaying the tyrant monarch and treacherous ministers, reopening a golden age, and remaking the cosmos.

It sounded utterly divine, but the officials and commoners across Great Xuan knew at a glance that this was a dynastic change.

Though it was indeed earth-shattering, after a few days of chaos, everyone quickly calmed down.

After all, from ancient times, imperial authority had never extended to the villages. No matter who changed at the top, those who truly controlled the world remained the same group.

This was what the people of Great Xuan initially thought.

But before long, the extraordinary aspects of this Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor began to gradually reveal themselves.

Overnight, towering platforms sprang up across the Great Xuan lands.

No one knew how these hundred-zhang-tall structures had come into being, but the existence known as the "Holy Emperor Bases" completely transformed the official hierarchy of the Great Xuan Empire.

Because these Holy Emperor Bases could project the image of the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor onto the celestial canopy.

Every commoner, even those living in remote deep mountains, could listen to the Holy Emperor's teachings through these Holy Emperor Bases scattered throughout the empire.

What terrified the local officials even more was that these Holy Emperor Bases also had the function of connecting to the capital.

In Qing Province, a drought had struck, leaving the land barren for three months with corpses littering the fields from starvation.

Yet shortly after the construction of the Holy Emperor Base, a contingent of the Imperial Forest Army emerged from it, bringing vast quantities of grain to provide disaster relief.

While saving countless commoners, this event was instantly broadcast across the realm through the Holy Emperor Base.

Moreover, Holy Emperor envoys emerged one after another from the Holy Emperor Bases, summoning local officials. This also corroborated the authenticity of the relief effort.

The Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor, being the reincarnation of an immortal, had once displayed various divine abilities in the imperial court. These feats quickly became known to all within a short time.

"Commoners everywhere may pray to the great Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor through the Holy Emperor Bases. As for local officials who are muddle-headed or corrupt, once verified, they will be severely punished without mercy." This was the Holy Emperor envoy's guidance to the people of Great Xuan.

"Do not think you can simply end it all with death. The Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor's methods are beyond your imagination. Those hundred-plus fat pigs tied up at the Xuan Capital's vegetable market entrance are the former officials who exploited the commoners. They are still using their pig trotters every day to write repentance letters on the ground. I have come this time to take you to appreciate the sight." This was the Holy Emperor envoy's warning to the officials across Great Xuan.

After the bureaucrats of Great Xuan collectively visited the Xuan Capital's vegetable market entrance, they all returned with faces as pale as death.

Upon returning to their respective territories, they swiftly became upright and stern in a short period.

Xuan Capital City.

Li Fan floated above the capital, gazing at the dynasty's qi fortune that had slowly shifted from black to azure. He nodded slightly.

"By using the Holy Emperor Bases to achieve strong centralization, the Great Xuan Dynasty has completed its transformation in a short time. Next is to gradually consolidate the people's hearts and elevate the Qi fortune."

Li Fan examined his own body.

The over one hundred million living beings of Great Xuan were now all tied to him alone.

It was equivalent to a weighty "anchor," fixing his existence in place.

His form no longer flickered transparently like a ghost.

It would still occasionally revert to that state of intangibility.

Clearly, it had not yet fully stabilized.

"No major issue. As long as Great Xuan encounters no problems and grows ever more prosperous, in another three years, I should be able to completely shake off the side effects of 'Intangibility.'"

"But that would only resolve the side effects. To truly escape the True Immortal's Web, I still need to head to the Dark Realm for activating [Truth] ."

Li Fan lightly swung a fist toward the front.

Without invoking his own Foundation Establishment spiritual power, he relied solely on the Heavenly Emperor Qi Classic, drawing upon the condensed dynasty qi fortune.

A whistling sound tore through the air as distant white clouds were cleaved in two.

Faintly, an azure dragon phantom emerged in the sky, rearing its head and roaring.

"White, azure, scarlet, purple, gold."

"The five realms of the Heavenly Emperor Qi Classic: in just one short month, I have cultivated to the second layer."

"Now equivalent to an ordinary late Golden Core cultivator. Such rapid progress is due, first, to Great Xuan's solid foundation; it could barely be called a feudal golden age. Second..."

Li Fan looked down at the towering Holy Emperor Base.

"Immortal methods are utter dimensional suppression against a mortal dynasty. After harshly punishing a batch of corrupt officials and resolving disasters across the lands, proclaiming it all to the realm through the Holy Emperor Bases caused the people's hearts to coalesce almost instantly."

"For an ordinary feudal mortal emperor to achieve what I have would take at least a decade or more. And they would still face backlash from officials and local powers."

"But I need not worry about such things."

With these thoughts, Li Fan slowly descended from the clouds into the palace.

"Greetings, Holy Emperor!"

"Greetings, Teacher!"

The guards in the courtyard immediately knelt to the ground.

"Rise." Li Fan raised his hand, lifting them all up.

The young men's faces flushed red with excitement.

They were all personally selected by Li Fan from across Great Xuan. Besides serving as guards for the imperial palace, their primary duty was to execute corrupt bureaucrats in various places, intimidate evildoers, and maintain the Great Xuan Dynasty's qi fortune in flourishing prosperity, unyielding and undefeated.

Among them, the one addressing Li Fan as Teacher was the exceptionally talented youth named Sun Erlang from the foot of Jieli Mountain, along with Wang Xuanba.

They bore an even more important mission.

"Erlang, how has your progress been with the Sitting Immortal Art I taught you?"

After dismissing the others, Li Fan turned to Sun Erlang and asked gravely.

Chapter 1129: The Meaning Of The Anchor Point

A profound black long robe, embroidered with nine golden dragons entwined around one another.

The imperial headdress's beads draped down, veiling the face and obscuring his features.

"Worthily the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor; mortals can scarcely hope to glimpse the dragon countenance."

Sun Erlang felt Li Fan's gaze upon him, his body trembling slightly as he instinctively lowered his head.

At the same time, he said hoarsely: "In response to Teacher, this disciple is incompetent. I only broke through to the first layer of Qi Condensation yesterday."

"With merely the scant spiritual qi gathered within the palace, for you to complete the drawing of qi into the body in such a short time is no small feat." Li Fan did not reproach him; instead, he nodded in consolation.

Hearing this, Sun Erlang's face showed even greater guilt: "If not for the Xuanhuang Immortal Heart Mantra that Teacher imparted, I fear that even now I would still be plagued by the miasma within my body, forever barred from the immortal path."

With that, Sun Erlang kowtowed several resounding times in profound gratitude.

Li Fan did not stop him.

Once Sun Erlang rose again, he asked indifferently: "How has the overall progress been for the Imperial Guard in learning the Immortal Heart Mantra?"

"As of yesterday, a total of one hundred sixty soldiers have successfully entered the gate and shed their mortal frames. The rest who failed the trial have all been thoroughly dealt with." A flicker of reluctance passed through Sun Erlang's eyes, but it was quickly concealed.

"You all shall henceforth be the main force in my conquest of myriad realms; you must cultivate diligently day and night, without slacking."

"As for the matter of spiritual qi, worry not. In three days, you will personally board this immortal boat and head to the Eastern Ruins Abyss."

With a casual point of his finger, Li Fan summoned a ten-zhang wooden boat before Sun Erlang.

The boat was inscribed all over with silver lines, emanating waves of profound aura.

"Truly an immortal treasure. For Master to entrust such a wondrous item to me so readily." Sun Erlang's heart tightened; he hastily nodded in assent.

The immortal boat then flew into his embrace.

A sensation of perfect control, like an extension of his own arm, immediately arose in Sun Erlang's mind.

"Withdraw." Just as he was savoring the boat's mysteries, Li Fan's voice timely sounded.

Sun Erlang bowed in salute and took his leave.

The entire rear garden of the imperial palace was now left with Li Fan alone.

Beneath the headdress's beads, in the shadows, the faceless visage appeared both strange and eerie.

"Heh, if not for the fact that this world is destined not to endure for long, and thus my casual attempt at imparting the law to others, I might have taken much longer to discover that the Xuanhuang Immortal Heart Mantra is not subject to the restriction of 'law of incompatible cultivation.'"

"Is it that the Immortal Heart Mantra does not fall under the category of cultivation methods, or is it a special grace left by the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao for the Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable?"

"Either way, the plan to select a suitable successor for the Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable in the future will prove far easier to execute than imagined."

Li Fan's thoughts turned slowly. With a flicker, he appeared atop the Holy Emperor Base tower standing tall in Xuan Capital City.

Strands of faint spiritual qi nourished Li Fan's body.

This kept his current late Foundation Establishment realm from regressing due to the lack of spiritual qi.

Having undergone over a hundred reincarnations, with his highest strength reaching the Soul Transformation realm, comparable to Dao Integration cultivators, Li Fan now, though returned to Foundation Establishment, beheld the world through eyes that differed from his past lives.

For instance: a mortal small world like Great Xuan was not utterly devoid of spiritual qi, after all, it was still connected to the Xuanhuang Realm.

It was merely due to the small world's unique structure, combined with the blockade of the Immortal Severing Great Formation, that the spiritual qi was exceedingly sparse, virtually equivalent to none at all.

Ordinary methods of cultivation could not utilize it whatsoever. Only those animals with long lifespans, accumulating it bit by bit over days and months, could eke out a limited harvest of spiritual qi.

For instance, on Jieli Mountain, that serpent hundreds of feet long.

"All the spiritual qi of an entire world extracted could merely sustain a Foundation Establishment cultivator's bare survival..."

"But borrowing some from the Xuanhuang Realm would be no issue."

The Taiyan Boat that Li Fan had given to Sun Erlang was newly fitted with some formations. The purpose was to automatically fly into the Xuanhuang Realm, absorb spiritual qi for storage, and then return.

Sun Erlang inside the Taiyan Boat naturally could not see what was happening outside the flying boat. All he needed to do was bring the boat to the edge of the Ruins Abyss.

Although with Li Fan's current level of formation dao, the act of stealing spiritual qi from the Xuanhuang Realm under the formations' concealment would be hard for anyone to detect.

But it was not a long-term solution after all.

To create something from nothing and generate spiritual qi, he still needed to rely on the Dao One Insect.

The Dao One Insect was a peculiar exotic beast that Li Fan could not fabricate out of thin air.

However...

Skipping over the prompt to retain the previous life's options for Truth, Li Fan looked at his own panel.

...

"Physical age has returned to the 399 years befitting a Foundation Establishment realm, but no need to mind it. Before the lifespan limit arrives, I should have long since undergone activating Truth."

"The Mental age upper limit, even after once breaking through to Soul Transformation, did not grow accordingly; it seems it has hit a threshold..."

Li Fan merely glanced over these before skipping past them.

He focused his attention primarily on the anchor point quantity and the bound items.

Originally, the available anchor slots was 2, but after absorbing the newborn Five Elements Cave-Heaven, it became 3.

As for now, only one remained.

That was because Li Fan had used a blank anchor point to complete the replacement of bound items.

The "Thousand Mechanisms Jade Cosmos Golden Sutra" had been replaced by Xuantian Mirror Origin.

The Halted-Step Remnant Stele (Damaged) had become the Myriad Beasts Condensation Crystal.

Anchor points were originally hard-won, but in the process of absorbing the newborn Five Elements Cave-Heaven and generating anchor points, Li Fan had gained new insight into the essence of anchor points.

Heaven And Earth Wonders, Golden Core laws, and Nascent Soul Cave-Heavens.

The reason Truth required absorbing these things to serve as anchor points was very likely because Truth was completing its cognition of "external objects."

Just as the Tianxuan Mirror elevated itself through measuring heaven and earth, Truth simply and crudely did so through devouring and absorbing experience.

"So it's the exact opposite of what I thought before."

"The closer something is to the world's essence, the more recharging progress it provides. Foundation Establishment wonders and Golden Cores law are too thin; they simply cannot satisfy Truth's needs."

"So the number of generated anchor points from absorption is instead limited."

"Conversely, Nascent Soul Cave-Heavens, life transformations of birth, and even the spirit of heaven and earth will provide massive amounts of recharging progress."

"With the colossal Xuanhuang Realm as nourishment, the quantity of anchor points is temporarily not something to worry about overly."

Thus, Li Fan unhesitatingly activated the anchor point replacement function directly.

The Xuantian Mirror Origin had been obtained by a stroke of fortune in his previous life.

As for the Ten Thousand Beasts Condensation Crystal...

In his previous life, right before ultimately executing the world-destruction plan, Li Fan had gone to various small worlds for a final inventory.

The Ten Thousand Beasts Wheel was the harvest from the Empire as well as from Xu Ke's place.

Li Fan's thoughts slowly drifted back to that time.

"The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's sudden assault; the Federation of All Worlds headquarters annihilated?" Xiao Qing's face turned deathly pale upon hearing Li Fan's words.

"If I had not severed the Spiritwood Realm's connection to you in time, I'm afraid the Empire would already have become a pile of ruins by now." Li Fan sighed slightly.

The Imperial Daughter and Xiao Hong exchanged a glance, their hearts both filled with lingering fear.

"But you need not worry excessively. Although the Spiritwood Realm was destroyed, the vast majority of headquarters members escaped safely and are temporarily in hiding. Once the storm passes, the Federation of All Worlds can rise again in an instant."𝘰𝘷𝘦𝘭.𝒸ℴ𝓂

Upon hearing this blatant lie, the Empire and the others instead felt reassured.

While listening to the Empire and the others' report on recent events, Li Fan arrived at the Empire's treasury.

Chapter 1130: Inferior Version Of The Sky-Patrolling Arrow

All the empire's research achievements have backups stored here.

Li Fan strolled through this vast treasury, walking all the way to the end, to the location of the Myriad Beast Furnace, before finally turning around.

He took out an object and showed it to the two of them.

It was a mere palm-sized disc, yet the pressure it casually emanated caused Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong's bodies to tremble; they even felt an uncontrollable urge to kneel.

"This is?"

They forcibly suppressed their bodies' instincts and looked at the disc in Li Fan's hand, their faces filled with horror: "The Ten Thousand Reincarnation Beast Wheel?"

With their lives and deaths in the other's single thought, this sensation immediately reminded them of that legendary supreme treasure of the demon beast race.

However, after staring intently at the disc for a long time, they still noticed something amiss.

As demon beasts, their bloodline memories contained the memories of their ancestors regarding the Ten Thousand Reincarnation Beast Wheel. But clearly, it did not match the object in Li Fan's hand.

The true Ten Thousand Reincarnation Beast Wheel had an even more ancient and profound aura. As for the one before them...

It did indeed have some controlling effect over them. But if they resisted with all their might, they might not be unable to break free. It was still a far cry from the rumored absolute control over life and death.

"A replica?"

The thought arose simultaneously in Qing and Hong's minds as they looked at Li Fan with shock and uncertainty.

"You guessed correctly. It is precisely the replica crafted by the Federation Elders, based on the research materials on the Ten Thousand Reincarnation Beast Wheel that you provided earlier."

"Besides restraining the Ten Thousand Beasts, it has another function, which is also the main purpose of my visit here this time."

Li Fan suddenly sighed: "Although the destruction of the Spiritwood Realm resulted in minimal personnel losses, our escape was too hasty, and many precious materials and research data were not brought out in time. So, after reflecting deeply on the matter, the Elders decided to activate the multi-world backup plan, copying and preserving everything of value from each small world..."

Before Li Fan could finish speaking, Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong's expressions had already changed.

But Li Fan continued calmly: "This is also for your own good. After all, your empire's self-defense capabilities are truly too weak; the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance could dispatch just one or two Dao Integration cultivators and utterly destroy you."

"Leaving a seed of civilization in the Federation of All Worlds means that even if something unforeseen happens, reconstruction can happen swiftly."

Li Fan fiddled with the disc in his hand and looked toward the countless frozen exotic beast specimens in the empire's treasury: "Your greatest wealth is actually all these various exotic beasts that you have nurtured over the years. I know you keep more than one backup of each; I am only taking one of them."

With that said, without regard for Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong's reactions, a beam of light shone from the disc, sweeping over these frozen exotic beasts. All those illuminated vanished in an instant from their spots.

And on the disc in Li Fan's hand, faint phantoms of those exotic beasts seemed to appear.

It appeared as if these frozen exotic beasts had been absorbed into it for preservation. In reality, they had long been directly decomposed by the disc's light beam. At the same time, information on their structures, bloodlines, tissues, and so forth had all been recorded into the disc.

"All the demon beasts under heaven originate from [Emperor Yi]. And the empire's exotic beasts are all derived from the demon beasts of old, nurtured and evolved. As the saying goes, no matter how it changes, it stays true to its roots; as long as this information is mastered, all exotic beasts can be 'conjured' out of thin air."

As the recording of exotic beast information increased, the phantoms on the disc grew more numerous. Not only did the aura it emanated become more majestic and powerful, but even the disc's own form changed because of it.

It became increasingly similar to the true Ten Thousand Reincarnation Beast Wheel in Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong's memories.

And during the process of collecting the exotic beast backups, Li Fan could not help but marvel at the wonders of creation.

Aside from some exotic beasts that were directionally and deliberately nurtured, the birth of the vast majority of exotic beasts in the empire basically originated from all sorts of coincidences. Different bloodlines intertwined and mutated, giving rise to new species that mutated into different offspring.

As if endless.

“Infinite possibilities.” Suddenly, before Li Fan's eyes appeared the words spoken by the Emperor Yi Beast in the Fallen Immortal Realm.

When Li Fan was about to, as usual, absorb the information of a frozen exotic beast, he suddenly stopped. Because from this exotic beast, he sensed a familiar aura.

“Sky-Patrolling Arrow...”

Seeing Li Fan's actions pause and his gaze fall on that spot, Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong immediately understood.

They hurriedly explained: “This exotic beast is precisely the core of the arrow that the superior emmisary previously instructed us to research. We named it [Sky-Splitter]. Back in the Beast Taming Sect, we once saw similar demonic beasts, but they were far less ferocious and swift than this one. Moreover, it had stronger self-awareness and was difficult to control. The offspring we have currently nurtured still fall short of the parent body that the superior emmisary entrusted to us. So we have not reported it yet.”

After a thorough search, Li Fan indeed found similar kin to the Sky-Splitter exotic beast nearby.

“The core is insufficient, but I can compensate with a formation. Even if it cannot reach the destructive power of the true Sky-Patrolling Arrow from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, having half of that would still be extremely practical.” With this thought, Li Fan absorbed the information of all these Sky-Splitter exotic beasts into the round disc.

...

His thoughts returned to the present.

That round disc, which bore the essence of the empire's research, was naturally the [Ten Thousand Beast Condensation Crystal] .

With the artifact-refining level of the Heavenly Mechanism Realm, plus the detailed and specific data on the Ten Thousand Beasts Reincarnation Wheel obtained from Xu Ke, creating a replica was not a difficult task.

“Condensing exotic beast entities from the Ten Thousand Beasts Condensation Crystal requires a massive amount of spiritual power. With my current Foundation Establishment late-stage cultivation, I cannot sustain it. But as long as we wait for Sun Erlang to pilot the Taiyan Boat back from the Xuanhuang Realm, it will be fine. With the Dao One Insect, plus Sky-Splitter, Great Xuan would possess the capital to conquer other small worlds.”

To head to the Dark Realm, one needed the You Clan people to initiate a special ritual.

Do not think that in his previous life, Li Fan easily suppressed the You Clan people; that was because he possessed the Five Elements Great Cave-Heaven, and his true combat power surpassed ordinary Dao Integration experts.

In this life, Li Fan, already ensnared in the True Immortal's Web, could not easily elevate his own realm. Relying solely on the Great Xuan Realm, the You Clan people were not so easy to deal with.

Therefore, it was necessary to enhance the combat power of Great Xuan's soldiers and civilians as much as possible, as well as to elevate Li Fan's own [Heavenly Emperor Qi Classic] to a higher level.

Li Fan stood atop the [Saint Emperor Seat] , beginning to contemplate suitable targets for conquest.

One month later, Sun Erlang successfully piloted the Taiyan Boat back.

This also marked the first step in Great Xuan's ascension to a spiritual energy world.

With the successful forging of the first Heaven and Earth Furnace, spiritual energy began to diffuse within the boundaries of Xuan Capital City.

The Great Xuan Imperial Army was the first to sense the existence of spiritual energy.

Overnight, the over a hundred Imperial soldiers who bore the [Xuanhuang Immortal Heart Mantra] all successfully drew Qi into their bodies, thereby stepping onto the path of cultivation.

And among them, the outstanding one, Sun Erlang, even leveraged the might of the [Sitting Immortal Art] to directly advance to Qi Refining completion in one go.

Chapter 1131: Great Xuan Conquers The Myriad Realms

If not for the fact that advancing further required a heaven-and-earth treasure, Sun Erlang would likely have directly broken through to the mid-Foundation Establishment stage.

Sun Erlang's temperament was steady, and he did not show any overt signs of joy. He merely took the initiative to report the matter to Li Fan.

"The Great Xuan Imperial Army, and in the future all the soldiers and civilians conscripted from the various realms, will all rely on your cultivation of the 'Sitting Immortal Art' for aid."

"Within five years, I need you to help me forge an army capable of both attack and defense, one that is truly usable."

Li Fan hovered in midair upon the "Saint Emperor Seat," looking down at Sun Erlang kneeling below, and spoke faintly.

"As for the essential items needed for your breakthroughs in realm, you need not worry about them."

Sun Erlang's emotions surged, his blood boiling with fervor, and he acknowledged the order before withdrawing.

He then went off to drill his subordinate Forbidden Army soldiers.

As for Li Fan, he took out a Tianxuan Small Mirror and probed it with his divine sense.

This Tianxuan Small Mirror was an unexpected gain from the Taiyan Boat's journey to the Xuanhuang Realm this time.

While the Taiyan Boat was drawing in the spiritual energy of the Xuanhuang Realm, a Golden Core cultivator happened to pass by.

It was unclear whether it was mere coincidence or if this cultivator was exceptionally gifted, but he faintly sensed the anomaly of the Taiyan Boat beneath the formation's concealment.

The man thought he had discovered some hidden treasure and excitedly circled around to explore it.

In the end, however, he triggered the Taiyan Boat's defensive formation and was unfortunately drawn into the boat, where he was suppressed.

At this moment, he was imprisoned by Li Fan in the Great Xuan Dungeon, left to stew for the time being.

"It is a pity that it is only the most ordinary Tianxuan Small Mirror, without the direct item transfer function."

Even though in this life he had not yet joined the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, and even though this Tianxuan Small Mirror did not belong to him.

Li Fan still connected to the Tianxuan Mirror without any obstruction.

It was precisely because of the existence of the Tianxuan Mirror's Origin.

In the previous life, at the same time the world destruction Plan unfolded, Li Fan had used a blank anchor point to swap the "Thousand Mechanisms Jade Cosmos Golden Sutra" for the Tianxuan Mirror origin.

On [Truth] 's panel, it had then become [Xuantian Mirror Origin (Incomplete)] .

The Tianxuan Mirror seemed not to have noticed this inexplicably extra clump of origin in the world.

And in this life, as the true master of this clump of origin, when Li Fan entered the Tianxuan Mirror once more, the feeling was different from the previous life.

In the previous life, the Tianxuan Mirror's origin had been a gift from the Tianxuan Mirror's human incarnation.

Although it indeed came with various permissions unique to the Tianxuan Mirror, at its root, those permissions still originated from the Tianxuan Mirror itself.

If the Tianxuan Mirror wished, it could reclaim the gifted origin at any time, rendering the permissions invalid.

But in this life...

Li Fan wandered freely through the countless streams of information in the Tianxuan Mirror, sensing true "freedom."

He possessed completely independent permissions.

He could even accomplish certain things that the Tianxuan Mirror itself could not.

For instance, deleting certain transaction records.

Perhaps influenced by the Xuantian King's past refinement of it, the Tianxuan Mirror was unable to engage in forgery, deception, or similar acts.

To maintain the stable operation of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, all data in the Tianxuan Mirror is indelible and traceable.

In his previous life, Mo Rubin's act of using the Tianxuan Mirror to deduce the whereabouts of the Xuantian King was ultimately uncovered by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

This was not a betrayal by the Tianxuan Mirror, but rather a result of its inherent properties.

But now, with the enhancement of this newly emerged origin power, Li Fan could achieve this feat.

He erased the information of the original owner of this small Tianxuan Mirror, and then registered a completely new blank identity for himself.

By selling key information, he quickly earned over ten thousand contribution points in a short time.

Using that as capital, he engaged in high-frequency trading, and in less than half a month, the contribution points in Li Fan's account had ballooned to hundreds of thousands.

He purchased the necessary materials in large quantities.

He set the agreed trading location in the sea area near the exit of the Great Xuan exit.

Li Fan remotely controlled the Taiyan Boat to retrieve the items.

...

Although it seemed troublesome, Li Fan handled the operations with ease and grace.

He bought all the materials needed for promoting the Great Xuan Imperial Army.

Without the shackles of heavenly treasures, the strength of the Great Xuan Imperial Army experienced its first surge.

Foundation Establishment cultivators emerged one after another, and Sun Erlang, as the leader of the group, advanced to the peak of Foundation Establishment in a short time.

However, with the blessing of Great Xuan's national fortune, if they truly clashed, Sun Erlang would not be able to withstand even a single move from Li Fan.

Sun Erlang's realm had advanced too quickly, so Li Fan ordered him to temporarily suppress his strength.

He also taught him the Taishang Sect's [Calamity Body] method, storing all the excess feedback cultivation.

Anchored at three years, the Great Xuan Imperial Army now had fifty-six Foundation Establishment cultivators and eight hundred forty-two Qi Condensation cultivators.

On this day, Li Fan lectured on the Dao above Great Xuan capital.

His image was transmitted throughout the land via the Saint Emperor Seat.

As Li Fan's words, each seemingly containing infinite principles of the great Dao, rang out, various phenomena appeared on the Great Xuan sky.

Celestial flowers rained down, accompanied by countless golden dragons dancing in the air.

The heaven and earth furnaces, previously sealed by Li Fan's formations, began to release the spiritual qi they had produced over the three years toward the surroundings.

Even mortals could sense this wondrous change in the world at this moment.

Not to mention the cultivators in Great Xuan.

Among the fifty-six Foundation Establishment cultivators, three directly gained enlightenment and advanced to Golden Core.

Sun Erlang was naturally among them.

However, it was merely a pure advancement in realm.

In terms of actual combat power, they were still far inferior to ordinary Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance cultivators.

But for Li Fan, it was already sufficient.

He did not expect them to personally engage in battle; they only needed to serve as spiritual power fillers to operate the formation.

He passed the formation to Sun Erlang and ordered the Great Xuan Imperial Army to practice it day and night, ensuring flawless coordination.

Great Xuan's strength grew dramatically with each passing day.

Anchored at five years, after accumulating power for a long time, Great Xuan finally launched its first campaign of conquest.

The target was the Spiritwood Realm, which Li Fan was very familiar with.

Since there were no Dao Integration cultivators, the entrance to the Spiritwood Realm could not be directly relocated to Great Xuan.

Thus, the route for this conquest was to pass through Xuanhuang and then invade the Spiritwood Realm.

To avoid detection by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, the expeditionary force consisted only of fifty cultivators led by Sun Erlang.

Riding the Taiyan Boat, they arrived silently in the Spiritwood Realm.

When they saw that colossal tree piercing the heavens and standing on the earth, all the Great Xuan Forbidden Army members were momentarily dazed.

But their years of grueling training quickly refocused their minds.

The formation was instantly set up, and a streak of afterimage whistled toward the Spiritwood.

It was the Sky-Patrolling Arrow!

The assault of the Sky-Patrolling Arrow naturally enraged the treefolk race.

They tried to intercept it.

But even treefolk elders at Nascent Soul strength were as fragile as paper before the Sky-Patrolling Arrow.

Screams echoed endlessly as the treefolk piled up their lives in vain, unable to stop the Sky-Patrolling Arrow from ultimately striking the Spiritwood.

Boom!

A terrifying hole was pierced through the center of the Spiritwood.

The pulsing heart of the Spiritwood was faintly visible within.

Sun Erlang, clad in black-gold battle armor, flew forward.

Ninety Sky-Patrolling Arrows orbited around him.

"Submit, or die."

Chapter 1132: The Creation Cauldron Furnace Changes

Ninety Sky-Patrolling Arrows, only half of them were truly lethal physical objects.

As for the other half, they were mere illusions conjured by formations, all show and no substance.

But the treefolks of the Spiritwood Realm had already been terrified by the destructive power displayed by that one earlier Sky-Patrolling Arrow, leaving them utterly unable to distinguish real from fake.

The central giant tree was intimately linked to the Spiritwood Realm.

When the wood flourished, the realm prospered; when the wood was destroyed, the realm perished.

That massive cavity in the giant tree's trunk left the treefolk race of the Spiritwood Realm with not even the slightest intent to resist.

The female treefolk chieftain led her entire clan in a humiliating kneel before the Great Xuan Imperial Army, pledging their submission.

Afterward, Sun Erlang followed the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor's instructions and engraved layer upon layer of formations onto the Heart of the Giant Tree.

He did not know the meaning behind this formation, but carrying out orders was the sacred duty of the Great Xuan Imperial Army.

With Sun Erlang's current strength at Golden Core perfection, activating the formation was still somewhat taxing.

Moreover, he had to remain vigilant against any counterattack from the treefolk race.

Fortunately, the deterrent power of the Sky-Patrolling Arrow was overwhelmingly strong, and in the end, the treefolk race did not dare to stake everything on a desperate fight.

Once the formation was complete, a brilliant green light erupted from the Heart of the Giant Tree.

The spirit tree's wound began to heal slowly, and a figure emerged from the glow.

"The spirit of the giant tree..." Sun Erlang murmured thoughtfully.

As he had suspected, after the spirit of the giant tree appeared, it did not side with the treefolk.

Instead, it transmitted a message to Sun Erlang: "Are you Senior Brother Sun? Teacher asked you to leave half the Imperial Army here to assist in constructing the formation."

"By the way, my name is Lin Ling."

Lin Ling transmitted a segment of footage, and upon hearing the familiar voice of the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor in the projection, Sun Erlang nodded slightly.

Without losing a single soldier or pawn, expending only one Sky-Patrolling Arrow, Sun Erlang had successfully conquered the Spiritwood Realm. Yet Sun Erlang knew full well that this had nothing to do with the Great Xuan Imperial Army's combat prowess. It all relied on the Sky-Patrolling Arrow's terrifying power, capable of annihilating heavens and extinguishing worlds.

Sun Erlang shook his head slightly, dispelling the frustration in his heart. He led the remaining troops toward the second target small world.

"Great Li? Hmph..." A flash of killing intent crossed Sun Erlang's eyes.

...

After the Great Xuan Imperial Army departed, Lin Ling began overseeing the treefolk race according to the formation taught by Li Fan.

Toward these treefolks, Lin Ling felt little emotional attachment.

On the contrary, Li Fan, who had lectured on the Dao, taught her skills, and resolved her doubts at the moment of her birth, was currently the person Lin Ling felt the closest to in this world.

"The Heaven and Earth All Spirits Great Formation?"

"When the formation is complete, it will open a passage to the world where Teacher resides. It will also raise the overall spiritual energy density of the Spiritwood Realm, allowing the world to sublimate."

"First, complete the construction of the foundational formation. The remaining key materials will be delivered by the Great Xuan Imperial Army later."

Thinking of the formation's description imparted by Li Fan, and recalling the promise that she alone could go before the Great Xuan once the Heaven and Earth All Spirits Great Formation was built, Lin Ling felt a surge of motivation. She urged those sluggish treefolk onward once more.

...

Great Xuan.

Sensing the somewhat blurry images transmitted from the Spiritwood Realm, Li Fan nodded to himself.

Although in this life, he had already planned to activate [Truth] in the Dark Realm, borrowing the power of the darkness to weaken the perception of the Faceless True Immortal.

But Li Fan also could not predict just how much this weakening would amount to.

Therefore, the [Heaven and Earth Myriad Spiritual, All Beings Refine the Spirits] great formation was also something that must be constructed, to guard against the unforeseen.

If one were to say that when he first saw the scene of billions of living beings perishing at the same time, Li Fan's mind still had some fluctuations.

Then the second time, and the times after that...

Li Fan truly felt no psychological pressure at all.

He reined in his mind and looked toward the nominal second disciple of this life before him.

Wang Xuanba.

Unlike the treatment given to Sun Erlang, Li Fan had not imparted to him the method of new law cultivation.

Instead, he had taught Wang Xuanba the [Creation Furnace Technique] that he had researched himself.

This version was already worlds apart from the initial one, which had merely crudely pried open the body's hidden reserves and violently strengthened the physical body.

In the previous life, after receiving guidance from the Heavenly Doctor and fully integrating the [Heavenly Doctor Immortal Scripture] and the Mending Heaven Record, Li Fan had gained a whole new understanding of how to stimulate the body's potential.

However, turning theory into practice still required some experimentation.

Wang Xuanba's special constitution was the perfect experimental subject.

“Xuanhuang heaven and earth, in the instant before imminent destruction, erupted with myriad wondrous transformations.”

“Humans should be the same.”

“In a normal state, the human body remains in balance. Only when invaded by foreign substances or the when the body faces a fatal crisis does it call upon its own potential.”

Before Li Fan sat Wang Xuanba, who was only seventeen years old, with his eyes closed in a slumber.

His height had already reached nearly three zhang, his entire body bulging with muscles, and the surface of his skin covered in a layer of golden, armor-like keratin.

Although one could still vaguely discern human features, at a sudden glance, he resembled an ancient ferocious beast.

“I have already implanted flesh and blood from eighteen kinds of alien beasts, of which only seven have been fused and absorbed by him. The rest all triggered severe rejection reactions; if not for my intervention to save him, he would likely have perished from bloodline exhaustion by now.”

“However, as his physique grows even stronger, perhaps another attempt can be made.”

With this in mind, Li Fan injected a mass of flesh and blood from the Heavenly Silk Fox mutated beast into Wang Xuanba's body.

A one-sided emphasis on strengthening power seemed to have caused some imbalance in the development of Wang Xuanba's physical body, putting it on the verge of collapse. He needed reinforcement in the aspect of agility.

Li Fan was very eager to see just where the limit of the human physical body lay.

...

Great Li Realm.

The conquest of Great Li was even a bit easier than that of the Spiritwood Realm.

Not even the Sky-Patrolling Arrows were used; merely the sight of that group of Great Xuan Imperial Army flying in the sky caused the emperor in the capital city, along with the civil and military officials, to all kneel on the ground.

Adhering to the principle that there could only be one emperor, the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor, Sun Erlang mercilessly executed the emperor of Great Li.

He then selected Prince Zhennan, Xiao Tianyou, as the proxy ruler.

As the Holy Emperor Bases appeared across Great Li, the people soon accepted the fact of the sudden dynastic change.

As well as the existence of that Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor.

Great Li also gradually began transforming into a spiritual energy small world.

Anchor Year 8.

“85%.”

“Compared to before, it's actually a bit slower.”

“Is it because of my current physical state?”

Li Fan looked at the number on the [Truth] panel and fell into deep thought.

Over the course of three years, as the Spiritwood Realm, Beast Realm, Great Li Realm, and many other small worlds were incorporated into the Great Xuan territory, his [Heavenly Emperor Qi Classic] had also successfully advanced to the third layer.

The Great Xuan national fortune resembled a rising sun at dawn, emanating scorching red light and vibrant vitality.

And the actual combat power he could manifest was equivalent to the early Soul Transformation stage in the Xuanhuang Realm.

However, Li Fan had already sensed the bottleneck in the Heavenly Emperor Qi Classic.

To advance further to the fourth layer, the Purple Qi Realm.

At the current rate of development, it would probably take several decades.
