Chapter 1133: The Power Of Dark Faith

To achieve the highest realm of the "Heavenly Emperor Qi Classic" and become a peerless emperor comparable to a Longevity Immortal Realm powerhouse, Li Fan estimated that he would need to establish a vast dynasty with influence spanning the entire Xuanhuang World.

"Hmph, with such strength, why would I need to practice the Heavenly Emperor Qi Classic?"

However, since this was merely a temporary measure to anchor himself, Li Fan did not harbor grand illusions about it.

In truth, his goal had already been achieved.

As the Great Xuan Dynasty flourished, the strange phenomenon of his body flickering in and out of existence had almost completely ceased.

Even the face he had once lost showed signs of reappearing.

"The Faceless True Immortal's power seems to lie in severing all connections with the world," Li Fan mused.

"As the Holy Emperor of the Great Xuan Dynasty, with billions of beings tied to me, I have, to some extent, influenced the power of the True Immortal's web."

Li Fan carefully contemplated the interplay of these two forces within himself, and a sudden realization struck him.

"The correct way to use the Heaven and Earth All Spirits Great Formation may not be to simply block the enemy. Instead…"

"It's like splashing a bucket of dog blood, forcibly linking the lingering thoughts of billions of beings to the Faceless True Immortal."

"In simpler terms, make those unjustly deceased souls believe that the Faceless True Immortal is the culprit behind their demise."

With his thoughts racing, Li Fan, after a deduction using the Final Dissociation Disc, quickly made subtle adjustments to the Ten Thousand Spirits Formation.

He then summoned Lin Ling to his side.

"Master," Lin Ling greeted, appearing before Li Fan clad in battle armor, exuding a valiant and heroic aura.

Unlike her experiences in the previous life, in this life, ever since awakening her consciousness, Lin Ling had borne the heavy responsibility of leading the Great Xuan Imperial Army to conquer the myriad worlds.

Constantly on the move, and under Li Fan’s explicit instructions, she had never formed a connection with the Earth Vein Ancient Tree.

Her demeanor had become bold and unrestrained, like that of a seasoned soldier.

After Li Fan explained the subtle modifications to the Ten Thousand Spirits Formation, Lin Ling was about to leave to carry out her task when she suddenly recalled something.

"Hmm?" Li Fan looked at her.

"Master, while traveling through the Xuanhuang World, a consciousness noticed me and kept trying to communicate," Lin Ling said.

"The Earth Vein Ancient Tree?" Li Fan frowned slightly.

"No, but it felt similar…" Lin Ling answered honestly.

Noticing Li Fan’s expression, she quickly added, "I strictly followed your teachings and ignored it."

"That consciousness seemed deranged, not even revealing its identity. It only urged me to meet it…"

Upon hearing this, Li Fan immediately understood.

However, he wondered why Ruomu had detected Lin Ling’s presence.

After inquiring about the exact location of the encounter, Li Fan designed a formation for Lin Ling to shield her from external perceptions.

With that, he sent her on her way.

After some thought, he decided that the primary task in this life was still to break free from the influence of the Faceless True Immortal.

Li Fan merely noted the time and place of Ruomu's appearance, with no intention of engaging further.

"Later, I can give the Earth Vein Ancient Tree a surprise."

Li Fan snorted coldly.

With a flicker, he arrived at the secret chamber beneath the Holy Emperor's throne.

The room was pitch black, imprisoning over a dozen members of the You Clan.

After Great Xuan gained initial combat strength, Li Fan ordered Sun Erlang and Wang Xuamba to launch an attack on the You Clan.

Even with the aid of the Sky-Patrolling Arrows, the results were mixed.

The Great Xuan Imperial Guard suffered over a hundred casualties but managed to capture some You Clan members alive.

After some coercion, they acknowledged reality and agreed to open a passage to the Dark Realm for Li Fan.

However, since the Truth charging was not yet complete, Li Fan was in no hurry to enter.

Seeing Li Fan approach, the You Clan members hurriedly knelt.

Li Fan stood still, sensing a statue of himself not far away in the darkness.

After becoming the Holy Emperor of Great Xuan's Heavenly Mandate, Li Fan had attempted to replicate a power akin to the source power essence.

Since it resembled the power of faith, he ordered statues of himself built and erected across the land, hoping the vast population would condense the source power essence.

Clearly, he had been overly optimistic.

Though the source power essence was something discarded by True Immortals, it was subtly superior to ordinary spiritual energy.

Without special methods, mere collective prayers from mortals were evidently insufficient to gather and refine it.

However, things changed after these You Clan members were captured.

Perhaps due to their innate, fervent faith in the Dark Realm, the moment they submitted, Li Fan faintly sensed a peculiar aura within himself.

It was a crimson hue, akin to the fortune of the Great Xuan Dynasty, and it felt...

Remarkably similar to the golden source power essence from the True Immortal of Salvation and Mortal Compassion in his previous life!

Later, Li Fan moved one of his statues into this dungeon, ordering the dozen You Clan members to pray day and night.

The results were striking.

The source power essence produced by these You Clan members surpassed that of Great Xuan's billions of citizens combined!

Li Fan studied their prayer methods and had the Great Xuan Imperial Guard learn and imitate them.

Yet, without exception, they failed.

After much thought, Li Fan could only attribute it to the unique constitution of the You Clan.

"It seems it’s related to the darkness power of the Dark Realm. King Xuantian..."

The Dark Realm, capable of partially interfering with the True Immortal’s network, clearly held many secrets.

However, these were not mysteries he could explore in this lifetime.

"Unfortunately, the You Clan's reproductive ability is rather weak."

"Otherwise, without relying on the Faceless True Immortal Statue, I could weave a faith network of my own."

The ability to freely grant, strip, or even transmit power remotely was simply too powerful and convenient.

Having tasted its benefits, Li Fan was reluctant to abandon it.

Thus, even though Truth was not far off, he continued researching this power.

Clearly, Li Fan lacked the talent for it.

"I’ll have to wait until it’s completely safe and let those guys study it."

Li Fan sighed softly.

"Great Holy Emperor..." At that moment, one of the kneeling You Clan members, trembling, spoke up, seemingly with something to say.

"Hm?" Li Fan’s interest was piqued.

This was the first time they had actively tried to communicate with him.

With Li Fan’s permission, the You Clan member continued shakily, "During my prayers, I discovered that the master I once served..."

He glanced cautiously at Li Fan, "That is, the Dark Realm, doesn’t seem particularly angry about our betrayal."

He swallowed hard, "Our clan’s ancestral teachings always said betraying the Darkness would surely bring divine punishment."

"But I’ve tested it now, and it’s simply not true."

Chapter 1134: Following The Soul Transformation

This You Clan person, who appeared somewhat young, trembled as he explained to Li Fan, occasionally glancing up to gauge Li Fan's expression.

But how could a crude fellow like him discern the countenance of the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor?

Growing increasingly fearful in his heart, he recounted all his discoveries from this period: "Holy Emperor, please discern the truth. After submitting to you, this lowly one has harbored no disloyal thoughts. Yet praying to the Darkness has become as instinctive as breathing. So during this time, while offering prayers to you, Holy Emperor, I inevitably also naturally besought the Dark."

"The Dark... responded to me, just as before..."

At this point, the more than ten You Clan people in the secret chamber all knelt down in utter terror.

Evidently, such a phenomenon had occurred to all of them.

Li Fan gently waved his hand, lifting them all to their feet.

In truth, Li Fan in this life cared little about these You Clan people serving two masters.

In his expectations, these individuals were merely tools for his brief research into the "power of faith" before activating [Truth].

Once he soon initiated [Truth]., everything in the Xuanhuang Realm would be annihilated.

Let alone these You Clan people.

What he truly wanted to know was why the "Darkness" would tolerate such duplicitous behavior.

If a follower of the Faceless True Immortal dared to serve two masters, they would likely have been torn to shreds by the True Immortal's power long ago.

"Either the dominator of the power behind the Dark is so noble as to be abhorrent, tolerant of such conduct even toward its own followers."

"Or else..."

Li Fan's heart suddenly surged with waves: "It has other designs. It seeks to use the betrayal of its followers to infect existences on the same level as itself."

With this realization, Li Fan immediately grew vigilant.

He had always been inclined to conjecture the most malicious intentions toward the powerful existences in this world.

He carefully examined the strands of that crimson power within his body, which, though not abundant, were extraordinarily potent.

But after repeated verification, he indeed found no signs linking it to the Dark's power.

"Am I overthinking it?"

After a brief moment of contemplation, Li Fan made his decision.

He would not allow any variables to affect the process of [Truth] in this life.

First, he verbally praised these You Clan people for their candor.

After leaving the underground secret chamber, however, Li Fan ordered Wang Xuanba to grant them eternal slumber.

Not some euphemism, but true, profound sleep.

Li Fan was unwilling to taint himself further with this impure power of faith, yet he also worried that directly slaughtering the You Clan people might draw the attention of the Dark's dominator.

Thus, he simply turned them into living corpses.

"It seems I need to find a way to address the You Clan people's fertility issues. Cultivating followers must start with the new generation."

Wang Xuanba soon completed the task and returned to report to Li Fan.

After years of continuously devouring and fusing exotic beast tissues, combined with practicing the enhanced version of the Creation Furnace Technique, Wang Xuanba had transformed from his previous three-zhang giant form into a normal human size.

He merely appeared somewhat burly and robust.

With the abilities of numerous exotic beasts integrated into one body, plus an utterly terrifying physical self-healing speed, even Sun Erlang at Golden Core perfection would not be Wang Xuanba's match in a deathmatch.

"Xuanba, in your recent cultivation, have you encountered any points of confusion or perplexity?" Li Fan asked.

It was to resolve Wang Xuamba’s doubts and to understand his physical condition, with the intent to improve the Creation Furnace Technique.

“Master, my body has indeed been getting stronger lately. Every moment, it feels like I have endless energy. But…”

Wang Xuamba organized his thoughts, scratching his head. “I’ve gradually developed two feelings. The first is an inexplicable sense of crisis, as if there’s some kind of natural enemy in the heavens and earth. This feeling is especially strong when passing through the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“The second is the occasional whispering voices. I can’t quite make out what they’re saying, but they give me a familiar, intimate feeling.”

After listening, Li Fan said calmly, “Though your body has become like a furnace, your mind has not. The stray thoughts of the exotic beasts you’ve devoured still linger in your heart. For now, stop cultivating and focus on recuperating.”

Li Fan naturally knew the reason for Wang Xuamba’s abnormal condition but did not elaborate.

He simply instructed him to rest quietly in the Great Xuan Small World.

As the deadline for the [Truth] charge approached, Li Fan needed to eliminate all unstable factors.

Thus, under his will, the Great Xuan’s conquest of other realms came to a halt.

It was called “rest and recovery, preparing for the enemy.”

The people and soldiers of the Great Xuan praised the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor for his great benevolence, and time passed swiftly.

At the end of Anchor Year 8, Li Fan, seeing the fully charged progress bar, resolutely rose from the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor’s throne.

Without alerting anyone, he quietly entered an underground chamber.

He awakened the slumbering You Clan member and opened the passage to the Dark Realm.

Deep in the Dark Sea.

Li Fan activated the Truth panel.

[You can choose one of the following options to retain:

An item you held during this simulation.,

Your cultivation realm from this simulation.,

The simulation memory of one person closely related to you. This memory can be inherited by that person.,

Forfeit the above choices to accelerate the recharge progress.],

…

The flickering light had been present for eight years.

“Inherit cultivation.”

Li Fan silently chanted, his vigilance at its peak.

At that moment, across the skies of the Great Xuan, the Great Li Realm, the Beast Realm, and other conquered small worlds, silver threads suddenly crisscrossed, forming a vast net that enveloped all beings.

Few within the net could sense the impending deadly crisis; some even looked up at the sky in wonder.

Only those with some cultivation, the “strong,” felt a vague unease in their hearts.

In the eternally unchanging Dark Sea, an invisible wave suddenly surged.

A tempest-like fluctuation struck Li Fan, triggering a violent storm around him.

Foundation Establishment, Golden Core, Nascent Soul…

Earth-shattering changes erupted within Li Fan’s body.

Li Fan saw his misty [Truth] Foundation transform instantly into a Golden Core.

Then, in an explosion that devoured all light, it evolved into a Five Elements Cave-Heaven.

In an instant, the Cave-Heaven completed an evolution that would normally take millions of years.

The majestic Five Elements, a grand spectacle.

Then, the boundaries between the Five Elements blurred.

Various transformations unfolded within the Cave-Heaven, too rapid to follow.

“The Xuanhuang World Destruction Transformation.”

The Five Elements unified, and all changes in the newly formed Cave-Heaven were in response to a world-destroying crisis that did not exist in this realm.

In the original wood-attributed Cave-Heaven, the towering giant tree’s leaves fell, leaving only bare branches.

Its form gradually became as hard as gold and metal.

Chapter 1135: Six Lifetimes To Drive Out Immortal Power

An endless water-attribute Cave-Heaven sea began to freeze for thousands of miles.

In the scorching fire-attribute Cave-Heaven, the flames grew dim.

But they did not extinguish.

Instead, they continuously contracted and condensed.

Transforming into clusters of strange, wandering free flames.

All sorts of inconceivable changes successively occurred on Li Fan's body. His realm also broke through Nascent Soul and surged wildly forward in the Soul Transformation stage. Soul Transformation early stage, Soul Transformation mid-stage, Soul Transformation late-stage... It did not stop until reaching Soul Transformation perfection.

Powerful. Full of vitality. Highly aggressive. This was Li Fan's current sensation of himself.

But Li Fan had no time to savor all this in detail. As he had expected, he within the True Immortal's Web had, in a short time, achieved such a transformation, already drawing the attention of that Faceless True Immortal beyond the High Wall.

The surroundings no longer seemed so dark, as specks of light appeared. It was as if waves were constantly surging; the silent dark sea had an uninvited guest arrive.

"[Truth]," Li Fan said decisively.

The surrounding scene became still. Then it rapidly misted over. Perhaps because the Faceless True Immortal did not fully probe the disturbance in the net, or perhaps because the dark sea shielded most of the Faceless True Immortal's power.

In any case, this Returning to [Truth] process went very smoothly. The void white mist did not last long before it suddenly dispersed. Light reappeared.

Anchor 1 year, Jieli Mountain Peak!

"Safe return."

"This proves my method is feasible."

Li Fan let out a breath of relief and first ignored the [Truth] prompt. He carefully examined his own face. The influence of the Faceless True Immortal's power, after another activation of [Truth], had indeed weakened to a certain degree.

Li Fan's face was no longer a smooth, empty void. Instead, there were faint outlines of facial features slightly protruding. However, this appearance seemed even more eerie than pure facelessness.

"Next, I just need to follow the same procedure."

He flew toward the Great Xuan Capital City with practiced ease.

What followed in Great Xuan and several other small worlds was almost completely identical to the previous lifetime, that is, the 120th lifetime. The only possible difference was that Li Fan was more proficient and skipped many probing steps.

For safety's sake, Li Fan did not cause any extraneous trouble; he even stopped his research on the You Clan people's power of faith. Minimizing all variables.

In the 121st lifetime, the [Truth] recharge progress sped up a bit. It completed charging three months early. Hiding in the dark sea, inheriting cultivation, Returning to [Truth]. All in one seamless flow.

Next came the 122nd lifetime, 123rd lifetime... Up to the 125th lifetime. In the 8th year of anchoring, Li Fan once again arrived at the dark sea.

He had a premonition that after this activating of [Truth], he would completely shake off the Faceless True Immortal's influence. Now his face was already no different from a normal person's. He could still sense certain flaws undetectable by the naked eye or divine sense. The True Immortal's Web's influence still existed. But it was fragile, ready to shatter at a touch.

Li Fan suppressed the impulse in his heart to court death. He took a deep breath and silently recited in his mind.

"[Truth]!"

The scene instantly shifted between illusion and reality. The mist condensed and then shattered in an instant.

126th lifetime, anchor 1 year, Jieli Mountain!

"Hu!"

A completely different feeling from his previous lives welled up in Li Fan's heart. It was unbound, true freedom and ease.

"Long confined in a cage, now returning to nature."

Li Fan murmured softly as he slowly walked to the edge of the cliff.

"Xuanhuang Realm, I am back!"

Facing the boundless sea of clouds, Li Fan chose to inherit his cultivation.

Above the Great Xuan, the winds and clouds shifted dramatically in an instant. The entire world seemed to buckle under the pressure, emitting creaking groans as if overwhelmed. But the change lasted only a moment. The vast aura emanating from Li Fan quickly subsided and dispersed. The sky returned to normal.

"At Soul Transformation perfection, even the Great Xuan world struggles to bear it."

"If I were at Dao Integration perfection, the world might shatter into fragments the instant I inherit my cultivation."

"But no matter. By then, I can inherit it within the Xuanhuang Realm. Or first upgrade the Great Xuan world."

Li Fan shook his head slightly, then shifted his thoughts to the plans for this life.

"In this life, I will stay behind the scenes."

"Unless absolutely necessary, I will not act myself."

"Everything else is incidental, only to verify my previous conjecture."

Li Fan narrowed his eyes, gazing into the distance as if seeing the skies of the greater Xuanhuang world.

"What exactly is the connection between the Immortal Ancestor and the Heavenly Doctor..."

Setting aside the argument that the Immortal Ancestor and the Heavenly Doctor never appeared together. Li Fan's suspicion about their relationship stemmed from the 'Mending Heaven Record' passed down by the Heavenly Doctor.

At the end of the Mending Heaven Record, it described a method to heal the heavens, one that existed only in theory. That was "borrowing a shell to return the soul, breaking to be reborn." Even the mightiest cultivation world has an end to its life, just like any living being.

When a world inevitably heads toward ruin and decline, no matter how advanced the healing arts, it is powerless to reverse it. One can only watch helplessly as it perishes. That is the normal, theoretical situation.

Yet the Heavenly Doctor proposed a possibility for saving the world. That is, to find another cultivation world in its prime and perform a soul possession.

"The Heavenly Dao changes hands, the world flips."

"Though it seems absurd and inconceivable, for the Heavenly Doctor, it may not be impossible."

"And moreover..."

Since stepping into the Xuanhuang Realm, every scene he had witnessed flashed rapidly before Li Fan's eyes.

"The various phenomena speculated in the Mending Heaven Record during a world's shell-borrowing rebirth align too closely with the current state of the Xuanhuang Realm."

"Strangeness abounds, the Heavenly Dao mutates abruptly."

"More importantly..."

"The Heavenly Doctor hails from the Heavenly Law Realm, yet places such emphasis on the Xuanhuang Realm. Desperately wandering the Darkest Star Sea to gather fragments from other worlds."

"Combined with the Heavenly Doctor's previous words and actions..."

A glint flickered in Li Fan's eyes.

"The accuracy of the Heavenly Doctor's mending heaven plan can almost be confirmed."

"The only truly terrifying detail is the Immortal Ancestor's attitude."

"If the Immortal Ancestor clearly knew the Heavenly Doctor was from another world, seeking to borrow the Xuanhuang Realm for a corpse revival, yet offered no obstruction from beginning to end."

"Instead, like the Heavenly Doctor, venturing into the Star Sea to collect world fragments."

"..."

A heaviness settled in Li Fan's heart.

"Either, aside from this, the Immortal Ancestor has no better way to save the Xuanhuang Realm and is forced into it."

"Or..."

"The Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor and the Heavenly Doctor are actually the same person!"

"Or rather, the Immortal Ancestor is the Heavenly Doctor's clone!"

A sharp gleam flashed in Li Fan's eyes. In the hearts of countless cultivators in the Xuanhuang Realm, there was one question. Why, while the Immortal Ancestor wanders beyond the heavens, does he not leave a clone behind? As long as one clone remained, it would suffice to suppress all petty threats in the Xuanhuang Realm.

Chapter 1136: The Mystery Of The Heavenly Doctor’S Dharma Transmission (Combined Two - )

If the Immortal Ancestor had a clone suppressing the Xuanhuang Realm, there would be no possibility of large-scale war between the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and the Five Elders Association.

Perhaps the Five Elders Association was deliberately tolerated by the Immortal Ancestor, used as a tool to maintain the delicate balance of the Xuanhuang Realm. For the wars between them, the Immortal Ancestor actually did not care, and might even be pleased to see it.

But the Xuanhuang Realm harbored too many demons, ghosts, and monsters in secret, and the Immortal Ruins loomed in the distance.

In a situation where a single disagreement could destroy the Xuanhuang Realm, it was undoubtedly more prudent to leave a clone in the Xuanhuang Realm.

However, the Immortal Ancestor, who regarded the Xuanhuang Realm as a treasure, had not done so.

In Li Fan's two experiences of world destruction, once the Immortal Ancestor arrived late from beyond the heavens and let out a helpless roar of rage.

The other time, he simply did not appear at all, with only the Heavenly Doctor showing up.

This seemed extremely suspicious.

Was refining a clone difficult for the Immortal Ancestor?

Clone techniques were rare in the Xuanhuang Realm, but Li Fan had repeatedly stirred up trouble through clone arts.

Not to mention this ancestor of the new method?

It was clearly something he could do effortlessly, yet he simply did not.

In fact, cultivators of the Xuanhuang Realm, regarding the question of why the Immortal Ancestor did not leave a clone to suppress it, although they dared not discuss it openly, had many speculations in private.

It was nothing more than the Immortal Ancestor having transcended the material world, no longer caring much about everything in the Xuanhuang Realm.

It was obvious they neither understood the Xuanhuang Realm nor the Immortal Ancestor.

And in Li Fan's view, if the Immortal Ancestor himself was a clone...

Then everything would make sense.

It was not that he did not want to, but that he could not!

In the 119th lifetime, the strength of the clone Li Qing had already approached infinity close to the original body, reaching the peak of Nascent Soul.

It was only one step away from surpassing the original body.

At that time, Li Fan had wondered in his heart whether a clone's strength could exceed the original body's.

Later, by borrowing the origin authority of the Tianxuan Mirror and consulting various classics, Li Fan had a vague realization.

A clone's strength surpassing the original body, or a clone refining another clone.

The latter should be somewhat more difficult to achieve.

Because if a clone could also refine its own clone, then it could extend infinitely onward. The final outcome would be that the entire world eventually had only one person left.

It was not absolutely impossible, but it had already surpassed the limits of mortal laws.

In comparison, it was still easier to have a clone's strength surpass the original body.

" Heavenly Doctor, Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor..."

Two faces successively appeared in Li Fan's mind, and he suddenly recalled that when he first entered the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters, he saw the statue of the Immortal Ancestor in the illusion.

At that time, his body's instinct had spontaneously warned due to some sense of familiarity.

And before that, the only existence above Dao Integration he had seen in the Xuanhuang Realm was the Heavenly Doctor.

"Although their appearances are not the same, there is a subtle resemblance between them."

"In the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters, a trace of the Immortal Ancestor's aura remains. Cultivators going there for the first time will all have some sensation in their hearts and produce illusions. My original body saw the statue of the Immortal Ancestor, while the clone Li Qing saw..."

The Dao Deviation Stone emitted a faint white light, faithfully reproducing the recorded images from that time.

In a blurry light cluster, several figures stood.

Fragmented voices of dialogue came from within.

“On this journey, how certain are you of success?”

“I will do all that I can and leave the rest to fate.”

“That doesn’t sound like something you would say.”

“The difference in strength is too great. All I can do is fight with my life on the line.”

“Enough. Go then. Whether it is fortune or calamity, none can tell.”

...

Li Fan recalled again the scene he saw in Hanhai's chaotic consciousness when treating him in the 119th lifetime.

"The Venerable Heavenly Doctor has been gone for a long time, and it is unknown whether the matter can succeed."

Hanhai sat upright in the sect's grand hall, while Taiyi, the Turtle-Snake at his feet, no larger than an ordinary turtle, spoke in a glum tone.

"Before departing, the Venerable Heavenly Doctor said there was at least a ninety percent chance of success. You and I should have some faith in Him," Hanhai replied, unlike Taiyi's gloomy demeanor, appearing quite relaxed and offering a comforting smile.

...

Li Fan touched his chin.

Under the assumption that "the Immortal Ancestor is the Heavenly Doctor's clone," all the scattered clues he had obtained in the past instantly connected.

Li Fan reviewed his past memories again.

Sure enough, he found more clues.

...

Yin Yin Island.

Master Yin pointed at the two human skeletons he cherished, leaning close to Li Fan and saying in an extremely low voice: "These two people are not the same kind of person."

"Not the same kind of person?" Li Fan was stunned. "What do you mean?"

Master Yin gestured with his hands for a while, pondered for a while, and seemed unable to find suitable words to describe it.

After struggling for half a day, he suddenly clenched his fist, his face full of distress: "It is just a feeling; you understand, right? Clearly both are human, but these two humans, the eras they lived in are different, and the regions they lived in are also different. Perhaps they look quite similar, and both were dug up by me from the bottom of the Congyun Sea, but there is an extremely special boundary marking the difference between these two corpses..."

Master Yin spoke mysteriously, talking for a long time yet unable to explain a specific reason.

...

During the second simulation of Tianyang's Eternal Remnant Will.

To escape Tianyang's weak body as much as possible and awaken from coma quickly, Li Fan studied the Heavenly Doctor Immortal Scripture and prescribed a supplementary formula for himself, instructing his senior brother to refine it.

It was indeed effective, but later when he woke up, his second senior brother Ling Xiao said this to him.

"Little junior brother, speaking of which, you were also lucky. In the pill formula you gave, there were several medicinal ingredients we had never heard of. We asked around all the nearby markets and could not find anyone selling them. Some even said that these kinds of medicines do not exist in the cultivation world at all."

"Heh, I did not believe it. How could it be that in one pill formula, all other ingredients exist, but these few do not?"

"At the time, we argued forcefully and quarreled with the other party."

"Fortunately, at that moment, a disciple of the Medicine King Sect passed by and heard our argument. Out of curiosity, he asked us for the specific pill formula."

"Then, based on pharmacology, he selected several common herbs as substitutes for us."

"Little junior brother, that is how you were able to drink this medicine!"

Back when he had an audience with Immortal Sage Jicheng and received the reward of the Derivative Jade Token.

Immortal Sage Jicheng had pointed to a [Bluecloud Willow] in his courtyard and asked him, Han Yi, and Ximen Yue whether they knew its details.

Han Yi and Ximen Yue both did not know, but only Li Fan, who had thoroughly read the Heavenly Doctor Immortal Scripture, revealed it in one sentence.

Later, Immortal Sage Jicheng gave Li Fan many pointers because of this.

Li Fan had originally been puzzled, but after inquiring further, he finally understood.

The Heavenly Doctor Immortal Scripture recorded over a hundred kinds of plants and creatures such as Mogu Bamboo, Qingye Wood, Myosotis Sylvatica Flower, Hundred Spirit Eagle, and Nine-Refined Deer. These should have been quite common flora and fauna, yet in the current Xuanhuang Realm, they had become scarce.

The Bluecloud Willow now only grew in small quantities in certain areas of Luoyan Prefecture. Immortal Sage Jicheng's hometown was also Luoyan Prefecture. At that time, Immortal Sage Jicheng had mistaken Li Fan for a fellow townsman. That was why he had given extra care.

...

"The Heavenly Doctor comes from the Heavenly Law Realm cultivators. Different cultivation realms have subtle differences in species. So the many spirit medicines I saw in the Heavenly Doctor Immortal Scripture are simply not found in the ancient Xuanhuang Realm. The Body Transformation into Dao master and disciples naturally asked around the surrounding cultivators, and the answers they got were all that no such medicinal materials existed."

"But the Bluecloud Willow, which was originally only found in the Heavenly Law Realm, now appears in certain prefectures several years later. This is precisely a sign of the Heavenly Law merging with Xuanhuang."

"The fusion of the two realms."

"The two human corpses at the bottom of Congyun Sea: one is a native of Xuanhuang, and the other is a remains from the Heavenly Law Realm humans. Though both are humans, because they belong to different cultivation realms, there are subtle differences. These differences are so minute that even with Master Yin's abilities, he could not research them."

"Not only does Congyun Sea come from the Heavenly Law Realm. There are even more prefectures in the Xuanhuang Realm that also originate from the homeland of Heavenly Doctor."

"Ximen Yu's Study of Ancient and Modern Mountains and Rivers deduced the changes in mountains and rivers through the ages. The Xuanhuang Realm does not seem like a simple outward expansion after devouring other small worlds or Cave-Heavens, but rather like it was dismembered and divided, then filled in the gaps with fragments from other worlds."

"In the Fallen Immortal Realm, Ningyuan City was not too far from the Purple Heaven Sect. But in the real world, the underground chamber of Mr. Bai in Ningyuan City is deeply buried under Taixu Mountain. Whereas the Purple Heaven Sect ruins are far away in Linlang Prefecture in the central part of the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance. In the same prefecture, after several thousand years, they are worlds apart."

"Fusion, transformation."

"It is just like performing a precise surgical operation..."

"Heavenly Doctor..."

"In the span of several thousand years, the Xuanhuang Realm underwent such earth-shattering changes. Yet ordinary cultivators were completely unaware. Even someone like Ximen Yu, with his exceptional talent and ability to pierce through the fog to uncover the truth, had his work banned and suppressed by the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance, unable to spread."

Putting all the clues together, Li Fan roughly pieced together a possible vague truth.

After the disappearance of Xuantian King and the destruction of the Xuantian Sect, the Xuanhuang Realm ushered in the era of rule by the Ten Great Immortal Sects.

However, the threat of the Immortal Ruins did not change with the shift in power in the Xuanhuang Realm.

The Ten Great Immortal Sects still had to resolve the crisis of falling into the Immortal Ruins.

The [Floating Starry Sky Formation] of the Xuantian Sect was evidently the most effective method among them.

Thus, the Ten Great Immortal Sects continued to promote the great formation, capturing other worlds in the void.

Finally, one day, they unexpectedly encountered the Heavenly Law Realm.

From the combat prowess of Tian Jue, Tian Yu, and others, it was clear that the overall strength of the Heavenly Law Realm back then was not much weaker than that of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Especially since the Xuanhuang Realm had also gone through a civil strife, with the supreme powerhouse Xuantian King and his twelve Dharma Kings all missing or dead.

The Heavenly Law Realm naturally would not sit idly by.

The two sides erupted into a massive bloodbath of unprecedented scale.

The Cloudwater Heavenly Palace in the Heavenly Law Realm and the Body Transforming into Dao Sect in the Xuanhuang Realm.

Neither was particularly powerful among sects. Yet in this war, they still nearly exhausted all their disciples to death.

"Even the Floating Starry Sky Formation might have been damaged during that time."

"In the later stages of the war, both sides were in a difficult position, trapped in a stalemate."

"At that moment, Heavenly Doctor planned to use the Dharma Transmission of Immortal Ancestor scheme, infiltrating the Xuanhuang Realm and using new cultivation method to shake its foundations, disintegrating it from within."

"But for some reason, something went wrong. By the time the Immortal Ancestor appeared on the land of Xuanhuang, a considerable amount of time had already passed."

"And when the Ten Great Immortal Sects were destroyed and the strength of the Xuanhuang Realm greatly diminished..."

"When Heavenly Doctor returned to the Heavenly Law Realm, what he saw was a scene of ruins everywhere and all living beings in devastation."

...

Li Fan tried his best to reconstruct the history shrouded in fog.

Soon after, he slightly frowned.

Although most of it seemed to connect, he still felt there were a few places that did not quite fit.

"First is the timing of the Immortal Ancestor and Heavenly Doctor appearing in the Xuanhuang Realm. Or rather, a time discrepancy occurred in the process of Heavenly Doctor traveling back and forth between the two realms."

Thinking of the miraculous journey of the Medicine King Sect, Li Fan could not help but glance toward the Immortal Ruins beyond the Xuanhuang Realm.

"Could it be influenced by the immortal power of the Immortal Ruins?"

"Besides that, there is another point of confusion. The Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao should naturally produce a certain degree of rejection reaction toward outsiders and foreign objects. Just like in the process of world fusion, due to differences in laws, strangeness is born."

"If the Immortal Ancestor is truly an outsider, then when he widely spreads the Dao and attempts to subvert the world's foundations, it would definitely trigger heavenly killing intent. Moreover, I recall that the Ten Great Immortal Sects once investigated the background of the Immortal Ancestor..."

"Although his specific true identity might not have been ascertained. But it should not be unclear whether he is a cultivator of the Xuanhuang Realm or not."

"The identity of the Immortal Ancestor seems to have something strange about it."

...

At the peak of Jieli Mountain, Li Fan stood for a long time, his thoughts unable to calm for a long while.

Half a month later, he finally awoke from his contemplation.

He began preparing plans for this lifetime.

"Since he hides behind the scenes, the main body will never show up under any circumstances."

"Using a clone to create a target to attract attention is perfect."

"Heh heh, the Faceless Holy Emperor who unifies tens of thousands of small worlds..."

Li Fan descended the mountain gracefully and used the essence blood of the youth Sun Erlang and Wang Xuanba to refine a clone.

"The Heavenly Emperor Qi Classic has no requirements for aptitude. Moreover, these two people's cultivation talents are not too bad."

"Sever all memories related to [Truth] and me..."

As a faceless figure slowly took shape before Li Fan, after observing it for a long time, Li Fan nodded in satisfaction.

"Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor, treading flat the myriad heavens, all worlds submit."

"You will be called Li Ping!"

Thinking this, Li Fan's figure gradually faded.

When the clone Li Ping awoke, he first scanned his surroundings.

Then, as if pondering something, he headed straight for the Great Xuan Capital City.

Not long after, Li Fan's main body slowly reappeared.

"Although it only has Nascent Soul True Lord realm without actual combat power. But for the mortals of Great Xuan, it is more than sufficient."

"The trajectory of Great Xuan in this life should be the same as before."

"But I will adjust the subtle details."

...

Li Fan gazed far into the Eastern Ruins Abyss of Great Xuan.

Actually, with his current strength at the peak of Soul Transformation True Monarch, invincible below Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign, and able to fight ordinary Longevity Immortal realms.

Even before the [Truth] energy charge is complete, if he goes to the Xuanhuang Realm, as long as he does not actively court death, he should not encounter any fatal dangers.

But after thinking it over...

Li Fan still shook his head, not acting on impulse.

"No rush for the moment."

He turned and returned to the thatched hut at the mountain peak, sitting in meditation.

Chapter 1137: The Holy Dynasty Reveals Its Portents

"The transformations of heaven and earth, the changes of yin and yang, all reside within a single heart..."

Li Fan's true body hovered above Jieli Mountain, overlooking the Great Xuan Realm. Sensing the interplay between the life-transforming phenomena within himself and the external heaven and earth, he could not help but sigh inwardly.

"The Heavenly Doctor must have used a method similar to what I am doing now when he dismembered and altered the Xuanhuang Realm back then. If he had not acted first, with my current strength, I could actually transform certain regions of the Xuanhuang Realm."

At this moment, the anchoring was still set to one year.

The clone Li Ping proceeded according to plan, heading to Xuan Capital City. With the speed of thunder and the suddenness of a spark under one's ear, he slew the tyrannical emperor, aligned with heaven's will, and established a new dynasty.

This lifetime's imperial dynasty was not a hasty tool forged to escape the Faceless True Immortal. Rather, it was preparation for probing the true identity of the Immortal Ancestor. Naturally, it had to be more formal; the old dynasty's name could not be reused.

Thus, seven days later, Li Ping used spells to proclaim to the world that the new dynasty was named [Qi].

As [Holy Imperial Bases] rose one after another across the Great Xuan lands, the absolute authority of the Qi Dynasty rapidly established itself among the people. Especially after the [Heaven and Earth Furnace] was forged and placed within the Holy Imperial Base, the spiritual energy concentration in Great Qi continued to rise day by day.

Meanwhile, Li Fan's true body, during the process of spiritual energy revival, quietly used his vast spiritual power within to accelerate and transform this process.

"What I released in Great Qi is one of the changes that naturally arose in the Xuanhuang Realm of the previous lifetime after it faced an existential crisis."

In Li Fan's vision at this moment, the small world of Great Qi was like a clay embryo, constantly changing form under the shaping of his hands.

"The so-called transformations of heaven and earth give rise to heroes. Because heroes often align with heaven's will and are favored by it, their aptitude and talent far exceed the ordinary. In folk legends of the mortal world, there are often tales of chaotic era heroes being stars descending from the heavens. The principles behind this are interconnected. This transformation is called [Heaven Opens Its Favor, Myriad Stars Descend to the World]."

As the power of the world's life-transforming phenomena dispersed from within his body, Li Fan could clearly sense the changes occurring in the Great Qi world under its influence.

In the coming decades, true children of destiny like Mr. Bai and Su Yuqing would emerge like bamboo shoots after rain. The actual aptitude of a child of destiny is closely tied to the world's intensity.

Among children of destiny, Mr. Bai naturally far surpasses Su Yuqing, an ordinary person from the Li Realm. The batch of children of destiny born in Great Qi would likely be somewhat weaker than the prodigies of the Xuanhuang Realm at the start. But as Li Fan continued to transform Great Qi, the small world would gradually ascend in rank. The quality of geniuses in Great Qi would step by step catch up to and surpass those of the Xuanhuang Realm!

"This is the transformation of heaven and man responding to each other, the way of nurturing people with heaven."

Li Fan carefully experienced the subtle changes as the world slowly took shape in his hands, and a sense of pride and detachment from the mundane welled up in his heart.

"This is the true act of casually altering heaven and earth with every gesture. What I am changing now is merely a small mortal world. I truly wonder what it feels like to casually mold the Xuanhuang Realm as the Heavenly Doctor does..."

World and man. Vast and insignificant. Yet now, the insignificant human was in turn shaping the world.

The intense pleasure from this stark contrast even surpassed the joy of advancing in cultivation or realm. It made Li Fan nearly addicted, unable to extricate himself.

Fortunately, the Xuanhuang Immortal Heart Mantra activated on its own, soon jolting Li Fan awake.

"Hmm... This is the natural bewilderment of the heart that arises after grasping power from a level originally beyond one's own. If one indulges in it continuously, there is a high chance of being assimilated by the world's heavenly dao in turn. Losing self-awareness and becoming part of the world's heavenly dao."

Li Fan narrowed his eyes, pondering inwardly.

During the process of shaping the entirely new mortal world of Great Qi, Li Fan also discovered that the life-transforming phenomena of the world was not a power that would persist indefinitely.

"As the world operates according to the transformation, it consumes this portion of energy. For example, with the [Heaven Opens Its Favor, Myriad Stars Descend to the World] that I have activated, if no further support is added later, relying solely on the initial impetus, it would probably last only about fifty years. It might even shorten due to various unforeseen factors."

...

During the time Li Fan waited for [Truth] to complete its charging, he continuously contemplated and comprehended the principles of the world's heavenly dao, as well as the future dao path that belonged to him.

Meanwhile, within the Great Qi Dynasty, earth-shaking changes that could be called terrifying were occurring every moment.

With the anchoring at 1 year, [Body Purifying Spirit Pools] were newly constructed in various places. Accompanying them was a method called the [Clear Heart Mantra]. People everywhere could go to the spirit pools and, with the power of the Clear Heart Mantra, expel the mortal impurities from their bodies.

Anchored at 2 years, the spiritual energy concentration in Great Qi had already reached the average level of the Xuanhuang Realm. The Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor manifested his form in the sky and personally imparted immortal methods to all living beings. It was named [Enlightenment Spirit Origin Technique].

All Great Qi citizens who had shed their mortal bodies could cultivate it. Those who had achieved minor accomplishment in the technique could all go to the Holy Capital to meet the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor and receive his personal guidance.

At the same time, in a secret underground base in the Holy Capital, over ten thousand robust soldiers began to temper their bodies with the [Creation Furnace Technique] and absorb the power of exotic beasts.

Anchored at 3 years, the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor issued a policy to encourage childbirth. Families with three children could receive a fifty percent tax reduction. For more children, the court would grant additional rewards.

To support this policy, [Holy Medical Institutes] were established across Great Qi. The physicians in the Holy Medical Institutes were all cultivators who had embarked on the immortal path. They had received medical arts directly from the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor, and each possessed superb medical skills. Though they could not resurrect the dead or regrow flesh on white bone. They could easily resolve common illnesses and pains in daily life, as well as various dangers encountered during childbirth.

Besides the [Holy Medical Institutes], structures like [Holy Academies] and [Holy Heart Halls] gradually rose.

As the name implies, the Holy Academies were places for teaching. Through synchronized image transmission, citizens across Great Qi could receive personal instruction from renowned teachers in the Holy Capital at the Holy Academies in their own cities.

Even the great Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor would occasionally lecture to them. The Holy Emperor's gaze seemed able to see all living beings under heaven simultaneously. When he lectured, he would also question students from various places. Those who were too nervous to answer would not be rebuked by the Holy Emperor; instead, he would encourage them kindly.

As time passed, the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor's status in the hearts of the people grew ever more exalted and unshakable. Thus, more and more people went to the Holy Heart Halls each day to pray.

In the Holy Heart Halls stood towering statues of the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor. Their faces were indistinct, but their auras were sacred and inviolable. Great Qi citizens with worries, those seeking something, those repenting... Nothing could not be confided to the Holy Emperor.

The Holy Emperor embraced all and would not blame anyone. It was even said that he occasionally bestowed holy grace, silently resolving the heartfelt concerns of his faithful followers.

Besides the various measures of holy favor, the imperial dynasty did not lack necessary punitive institutions. The Holy Supervisory Court oversaw the world; any behavior violating the imperial laws would receive the severest punishment.

The most terrifying punishment was not death, but having one's consciousness extracted and imprisoned in the Holy Heart Hall. One would have to face the curses of Great Qi's people for eternity.

...

The entire Great Qi Dynasty was like a flying vessel with ever-increasing power, its strength surging forward and growing day by day. Yet it had not launched any external wars, only continuously accumulating strength.

Anchored at the beginning of the eighth year. Looking at the fully charged [Truth], Li Fan abruptly stood up. He stretched his muscles and bones, then headed toward the Eastern Ruins Abyss.

Hiding behind the scenes did not mean he had to stay in the Great Qi small world forever. Just like in the final moments of his 119th life, when he hid in the Nether Prison. The enemy's heartland, the blind spot under the lamp, might be the safest and most unexpected place.

Upon returning to the familiar Xuanhuang Realm, Li Fan did not linger; he transformed into a wisp of shadow and rushed straight to Tianyu Prefecture. Based on the secret information he had glimpsed in the Tianxuan Mirror earlier, there was an identity in Tianyu Prefecture that was perfect for disguise.

In the central part of Tianyu, the Luojia Mountains. In an underground cavern. Li Fan slipped in like a ghost, silent and traceless. After discerning the surrounding aura fluctuations, he revealed a faint smile.

"Soul Transformation cultivator Sun Ang discovered a Spirit of Heaven and Earth here. He sealed the underground cavern with a formation and spent a full three years alone wearing it down until it died. In the end, he achieved Dao Integration Immortal Sage."

"The Sun Ang family had generations serving important roles in the Guardian Hall. Shortly after he became a Dao Integration Immortal Sage, he was selected to enter the Guardian Hall headquarters to guard the Derivative Law Jade. He was one of the several elders I met when I faced the Derivative Law Jade back then."

"Sun Ang's sacrificed Spirit of Heaven and Earth is extremely mysterious. After achieving Dao Integration, he never acted again, so even I do not know his full details. But it does not matter..."

As Li Fan's thoughts turned, he had already spotted his target.

The originally pitch-black underground cavern suddenly became resplendent with rainbow colors. Sun Ang sat cross-legged, his expression solemn, his hands constantly forming seals. He emitted streams of rainbow light, carefully binding the illusory shadow ahead.

And that Spirit of Heaven and Earth, for some reason, was in a deep slumber. It showed no awareness of Sun Ang's actions, allowing him to do as he pleased.

"Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Purple Luminous. Bestowed with heavenly duty, it nurtures the Luojia Thousand Crystal Hundred Flowers..."

The moment Li Fan saw the illusory shadow of the Spirit of Heaven and Earth, he understood its essence.

"The Luojia Thousand Crystal Hundred Flowers are a famous spectacle of the Luojia Mountains. They appear once every five hundred years, emerging from the earth, blanketing the mountains like flowers. In truth, they are special crystals like spirit stones..." Various pieces of information instantly surfaced in Li Fan's mind.

"One of the heavenly orders, unrelated to me."

In fact, Li Fan had no intention of acting against this Spirit of Heaven and Earth. He turned his gaze to Sun Ang, who was focused on casting spells to imprison Purple Luminous, and his eyes flashed.

In an instant, he laid down layers of formations, trapping him in the center. Only then did Sun Ang awaken in shock.

"What..."

Before the word could leave his mouth, darkness fell before his eyes, and he lost consciousness.

Li Fan toured his sea of consciousness, viewing all his memories. Then he extracted a strand of his essence blood and casually suppressed him in his internal small world.

Li Fan immediately activated the Mysterious True Spirit Transformation, shifting into Sun Ang's appearance.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1138: Heat Death Ice-Seal Transformation

Transforming into Sun Ang and infiltrating the Guardian Hall headquarters, the primary target was actually not the Derivative Law Jade.

It was indeed for the purpose of hiding.

From the information gathered so far, the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance managed the cultivators guarding the Derivative Law Jade very strictly.

Not only did their backgrounds need to be spotless, but they were basically selected from families with generations of service in the Guardian Hall.

Before joining, they had to undergo rigorous scrutiny.

Moreover, the key point was that once chosen to guard the Derivative Law Jade, it meant they could not step out of the Guardian Hall headquarters for three hundred years.

While guarding, they themselves became prisoners.

So among the various hereditary families of the Guardian Hall, almost no one was willing to take on this grueling task.

Even if the rewards were ever so generous.

After all, once reaching Dao Integration Immortal Sage, true longevity was hopeless.

What they pursued was nothing more than power and enjoyment.

Sitting in prison for three hundred years was not something everyone could endure.

But these special conditions happened to form Li Fan's ideal hiding place.

The Guardian Hall headquarters was already located in the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance headquarters.

The space containing the Derivative Law Jade had even more formations and restrictions set up.

There might even be contingency for Immortal Ancestor too.

Even for Li Fan now, there was no one hundred percent certainty of forcing his way in and out.

By changing identity and hiding inside.

When the outside world was turned upside down, no one would think that the true culprit behind it all had hidden in this core land of the Immortal Alliance hundreds of years earlier.

"Actually, the Derivative Law Jade holds little use for me now."

"To advance further, unless it is an Immortal method that fits perfectly. Otherwise, it still requires my own comprehension of the Dao and the principles of the world."

"The ordinary techniques derived by the Derivative Law Jade..."

Li Fan shook his head slightly.

After all, it was merely the Wonder of the Xuanhuang Heaven. From Li Fan's current height, to contend with the Heavenly Doctor, the Immortal Ancestor, or even the True Immortal, the help the Derivative Law Jade could provide was extremely limited.

Li Fan had reincarnated through many lives, surveying ancient and modern secrets.

After obtaining the origin of the Tianxuan Mirror, the many secrets of the Ten Thousand Immortals Alliance were laid bare.

With the guidance of the Heavenly Doctor inheritance...

Li Fan's current insight and level were actually among the absolute top in the entire Xuanhuang Realm.

For example, with the Mysterious True Spirit Transformation, the version originally derived by the Derivative Law Jade only applied up to the Nascent Soul True Lord realm.

But when Li Fan broke through to Soul Transformation in this life, during the eight years waiting in the small world, he had easily extended it to the Soul Transformation realm relying solely on himself, without much effort.

In a certain sense, in deriving techniques most suited to himself, Li Fan faintly surpassed the Derivative Law Jade.

After all, Li Fan possessed [Truth], a fact that the Derivative Law Jade could never know.

"Unless..."

"Like with the Final Dissociation Disc, I help it complete its ascension and transformation."

Li Fan's gaze flickered.

While pondering, he flew away from the underground cavern in the Luojia Mountains.

The Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Purple Luminous, was an existence even weaker than the initial Crimson Flame; Li Fan had no interest in devouring it.

As for not devouring it, how then to disguise as a Dao Integration cultivator...

Among the [Xuanhuang World-Destroying Transformations] that Li Fan had absorbed, there were several powers no less than those of a Spirit of Heaven and Earth.

Their external manifestations and forms of power were also extremely similar to those of a Spirit of Heaven and Earth, making them hard to distinguish.

In the end, a Spirit of Heaven and Earth was a concretization of the laws of the Xuanhuang Realm.

In the instant before destruction, there was naturally no time to manifest a corresponding Spirit of Heaven and Earth; instead, it manifested directly as the power of the world.

On the way back to the Sun family by flight, Li Fan's clothing and appearance quietly changed.

His eyebrows and hair turned snow-white, and his clothes seemed to be covered in frost.

The space he passed through during flight seemed to produce many tiny ice crystals due to the low temperature.

"[Heavenly Heat Death, All Things Frozen], one of the derivations born in the Xuanhuang Realm to cope with life-and-death crises."

"It plunges all things in heaven and earth into absolute darkness and silence, seeking to evade the calamity. In theory, it is indeed feasible."

"Unfortunately, the external force was too powerful, instantly destroying the entire system."

"This Heat Death Frozen Transformation is almost indistinguishable from the Spirit of Heaven and Earth of ice-based powers. The occasional energy fluctuations I deliberately leaked simulate the appearance of having just advanced, with an unstable realm."

As Li Fan pondered, Tianyu Sky City came into view.

Without the slightest pause, he flew straight into the Sun family within the city.

Although the Sun family had generations of members serving important roles in the Guardian Hall, and now had three Dao Integration cultivators in the clan, they remained extremely low-key.

In Tianyu Sky City, they had only purchased two adjacent cave dwellings that appeared utterly unremarkable on the outside.

Only upon entering could one discover the vast inner space of these connected cave dwellings.

"Sun Ang? You still know to come back?"

As soon as Li Fan entered by flight, a voice tinged with anger followed.

It was Wu Ninglu, the Dao companion of the body he had possessed, Sun Ang.

Wu Ninglu's cultivation was similar to Sun Ang's previous level, both at the late Soul Transformation stage.

However, their relationship was not good; they had become Dao companions initially due to pressure from both families.

These years, they had not produced any offspring.

"Huh? You..." Wu Ninglu approached aggressively, as if coming to hold him accountable.

But when she clearly saw Li Fan's current appearance, she could not help but be greatly shocked.

Li Fan merely glanced at Wu Ninglu, without answering her.

"I am tired."

With extreme coldness, he said this, then flew toward Sun Ang's residence.

Within the Sun family's cave residence was a circular valley.

Important structures like the family council hall and treasury were located in the center of the valley.

The residences of various clan members were distributed within the encircling mountain walls.

Regarding Sun Ang's previous departure without a word and subsequent inexplicable disappearance, Wu Ninglu had long been harboring a belly full of anger.

At this moment, seeing him return yet still with that indifferent, unresponsive attitude, Wu Ninglu's anger reached its peak.

But for some reason, as she was about to erupt, when she saw his icy gaze, her full belly of fury instantly extinguished.

She opened her mouth, but even lacked the courage to call out to him.

This inexplicable emotion only slowly dissipated after Sun Ang's figure completely vanished from sight.

"I was actually intimidated by him?!" Wu Ninglu, unable to accept it, was both shocked and furious.

However, recalling Sun Ang's performance just now, a bold guess could not help but surface in her mind.

"Has Sun Ang broken through?"

Just now, facing Sun Ang, Wu Ninglu had indeed felt the fear and unease that only arose when facing the family's elders.

Thinking of this, the anger and dissatisfaction in Wu Ninglu's heart completely dissipated.

With disbelief and joy, she hurriedly followed him back home.

"Sun Lang?"

As if returned to the time when they had just met, Wu Ninglu cautiously greeted.

Next

Chapter 1139: The Sun Clan’S Hidden Backhand

Li Fan lay on the bed with his eyes closed, as if he had not heard Wu Ninglu’s words at all.

Wu Ninglu’s beautiful eyes flickered slightly. She began to undo her garments, preparing to climb onto the bed as well.

However, just as her body was about to touch Li Fan, a bone-piercing chill erupted from him. Instinctively, she shuddered and stepped back.

Looking closely, Wu Ninglu was shocked to discover that a thin layer of frost had formed on her skin in that instant.

Had she not instinctively pulled away, she was certain she would have been frozen solid by her own husband.

Her heart was a mix of joy and anger.

Before she could speak the thousand words swirling in her chest, Li Fan’s calm voice suddenly sounded.

“I just broke through, and I can’t perfectly control my power yet. You should avoid getting too close to me for now.”

Although her attempt to show affection was rejected, Wu Ninglu did not get angry this time.

Her face brightened with delight as she nodded repeatedly. “Alright, alright. I didn’t expect that after vanishing for over half a year, my dear Sun Lang would return with such a surprise. This is a great matter. I must inform the clan so everyone knows.”

As she turned to leave, an ice wall instantly formed, sealing the doorway shut.

Startled, she turned back to look at Li Fan.

His eyes remained closed as he spoke again, seemingly about something entirely unrelated.

“Regarding the appointment for the Guardian Hall, have the three family elders decided who will take the position?”

Wu Ninglu paused for a moment, then quickly realized what he meant.

“The patriarch naturally cannot leave his duties. My eldest uncle and third uncle pushed the matter back and forth for quite some time. But a few days ago, I heard the eldest uncle finally gave in…”

Her voice trailed off as she slowly retracted the foot she had lifted.

“Our branch is already not well regarded in the clan. If word of your advancement to Dao Integration spreads, they will surely send us to the Guardian Hall. That would mean being confined there for at least three hundred years without being able to leave.”

“Hmph.”

“If I hadn’t been injured during my breakthrough, causing my realm to become unstable and risking exposure, I wouldn’t have come back so hastily.”

Li Fan sighed softly.

“Although serving in the Guardian Hall comes with material rewards from both the clan and the Immortal Alliance, such things are meaningless to me now. And three hundred years… is far too long.”

Li Fan suddenly opened his eyes and looked straight at Wu Ninglu.

“So for now, do not reveal anything about my breakthrough.”

Meeting his gaze, Wu Ninglu’s body trembled as she instinctively nodded.

Li Fan then closed his eyes once more and resumed his meditation.

The room fell silent again.

A faint field of freezing energy lingered in the air. Wu Ninglu had to circulate her spiritual power at full strength just to maintain her body temperature.

Curled up in the corner of the room, her heart was filled with both grievance and envy.

“So this is the might of a Dao Integration cultivator… I never thought he would be the one to reach this realm first. I had always believed Sun Weizhou would be the first among the younger generation to break through.”

As she watched her husband in quiet cultivation, countless thoughts churned through her mind.

Only when his breathing steadied and his aura gradually calmed, yet he remained silent with his eyes closed—did Wu Ninglu pout slightly, stand up, and quietly leave the room.

…

“If I’m not mistaken, before long, that foolish woman will expose the fact that her Dao companion is already a Dao Integration-level expert.”

“She only did it for the small benefits promised by Sun Luchang.”

“Well, it cannot be said that she is truly stupid. After all, only the benefits actually in one’s own hands are real. As long as Sun Ang is a Dao Integration cultivator, no matter where she goes, others will show her some respect as the Dao Integration cultivators’s wife.” Li Fan’s eyes turned deep, as if he could see through the flow of time itself.

Just as Li Fan had predicted—

Half a month later, when Wu Ninglu personally saw her elder uncle and third uncle promising each other all sorts of generous conditions just to avoid taking up positions in the Guardian Hall, she could not help but feel tempted.

After all, her days with Sun Ang in the past were not particularly good.

However, due to Li Fan’s prior warning, she did not speak directly.

Yet, her slightly abnormal expression did not escape the eyes of the Sun family’s Dao Integration experts.

Moreover, when Li Fan had returned earlier, he had deliberately released a faint ripple of spiritual fluctuation, planting a seed of suspicion in their hearts.

After a round of questioning, Wu Ninglu half-reluctantly and half-willingly revealed the matter.

Sun Luchang was overjoyed at once, and immediately pulled his two elder brothers along to confront Li Fan together.

Li Fan feigned helplessness and anger as he looked at Wu Ninglu.

The three Dao Integration elders hurried to defend her, excusing her actions.

Then, they earnestly persuaded Li Fan, reasoning with both emotion and logic.

For the sake of the family’s greater interests, he ultimately had no choice but to agree to take the post at the Guardian Hall in her husband’s stead.

“Lu’er, don’t worry. While you’re away, we’ll make sure to take good care of Ninglu. We won’t let her suffer in the slightest,” Sun Luchang promised.

Lu’er was Sun Ang’s childhood name.

In theory, since they were both Dao Integration cultivators now, it was improper for an elder to still call him by his childhood name. Yet Sun Luchang continued to do so deliberately, to remind him that “Sun Ang” was still a member of the Sun clan.

To ensure that Sun Ang could pass the inspection of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, the three Dao Integration experts of the Sun family jointly tested his actual combat ability.

Li Fan only defended throughout, occasionally releasing one or two weaker spells.

After several hundred rounds, he gradually showed signs of fatigue.

“Lu’er, the Spirit of Heaven and Earth you devoured truly isn’t ordinary. Though your offense is somewhat lacking, that freezing domain which seals everything around you can block the majority of attack techniques,” the three Dao Integration elders remarked with satisfaction.

Even as a newly advanced Dao Integration cultivator, such strength was more than enough to satisfy the Immortal Alliance’s assessment.

Before departing for the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, Li Fan quietly called Wu Ninglu over.

“I know you’ve been avoiding me lately. Are you afraid that I’ll blame you?”

“How could that be?” she replied.

“I understand. Even if you hadn’t said anything, my advancement to Dao Integration couldn’t have remained hidden for long. Some things are simply inevitable.”

“Besides, you and I are one. I’ve struggled for three hundred years. Getting a few benefits for you isn’t a big deal.”

Wu Ninglu was taken aback by Li Fan’s words, and for a moment, she even felt moved.

“While I’m gone, you must be careful on your own.”

“Those old men in the family speak kindly, but who knows what they’ll actually do.”

“During this trip, I made a friend. He helped me greatly in achieving this breakthrough.”

As he spoke, Li Fan handed her a jade communication talisman.

“If you ever encounter something you truly cannot handle, contact him.”

“He should lend you his aid.”

“But remember, this person is also a Dao Integration cultivator, and his temperament is rather eccentric. Unless it’s absolutely necessary, do not seek him out.”

“Remember this well.”

Chapter 1140: The Childhood Immortal Ancestor Image

Wu Ninglu accepted the jade talisman handed over by Li Fan but hesitated to speak.

“It’s only three hundred years! I’ll go.”

Li Fan simply waved his hand and said nothing more.

Following the Sun Family Patriarch, he headed toward the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters.

The jade talisman Li Fan gave to Wu Ninglu, aside from serving as a communication talisman, was in truth a miniature composite formation. It contained one of Li Fan’s split souls.

It could completely monitor Wu Ninglu’s movements, but more importantly, it served as Li Fan’s “eye” in the outside world, allowing him to constantly observe the movements of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

This split soul contained the origin of the Tianxuan Mirror, enabling it to hide within the mirror and supervise the Alliance’s affairs at all times.

After all, during the period he would be guarding the Derivative Law Jade, he might be in complete isolation, unable to freely use even the Tianxuan Mirror.

His clone, on the other hand, was still within the Great Qi small world and would not leave easily.

It was therefore necessary to leave behind a contingency plan.

Given Li Fan’s current cultivation strength, this split soul’s combat ability was almost on par with an ordinary Dao Integration cultivator.

The only drawback was that, unlike normal cultivators, it could not quickly recover after battle.

Without the support of his true self, every bit of energy used was permanently consumed.

As for the “friend” Li Fan mentioned to Wu Ninglu, that was none other than the Holy Blood Embryo he had created.

However, lacking the infusion of the True Immortal Web and Source Power Essence, that embryo had only barely advanced to the Qi Condensation Realm.

Li Fan had sent it into the ruins of the Heavenly Sword Sect, where it was currently subduing the black sword, Heavenly Wonder.

With its previous experience, there should be no problem. It merely required patience and time.

Patriarch Sun Luyuan of the Sun Family had been in the Dao Integration Realm for over five hundred years.

In his youth, he too had guarded the Derivative Law Jade.

On the way to the Alliance Headquarters, he explained the details of this “duty” to Li Fan.

“The Derivative Law Jade is the foundation of our Alliance. Though it lies at the heart of our headquarters and seems absolutely safe, one must remain constantly vigilant against malicious plots.”

“In the past thousand years, the Five Elders Association has orchestrated no fewer than three assaults against it. They have used every means possible to approach the Derivative Law Jade and then launched mental and spiritual attacks upon it with a spell known as the ‘Curse of Malice.’”

“The Derivative Law Jade itself has nearly no resistance to such attacks. Once afflicted, it becomes paralyzed for at least a hundred years. The last time the Five Elders Association succeeded, the Alliance suffered immense losses. Yet the matter could not be made public. As a result, the exchange prices of cultivation methods in the Tianxuan Mirror nearly doubled, causing great unrest among Alliance cultivators…”

Sun Luyuan sighed as he recalled the events of his youth.

“So, this duty is not as simple as it looks.”

Li Fan asked calmly, “Patriarch, if during my tenure I fail to stop such attacks and the Derivative Law Jade is damaged, what then?”

Sun Luyuan halted his steps, turned around, and looked at Li Fan. After a pause, he replied, “In any case, our promise stands. We will take good care of Ninglu. You need not worry.”

Li Fan was momentarily speechless.

He had not noticed before that this Sun Luyuan actually had a subtle sense of humor.

As the light and shadows shifted, Li Fan returned once more to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance Headquarters.

As before, a phantasmic vision appeared before his eyes.

However, this time the scene differed entirely from the previous Immortal Ancestor statue he had seen.

An endless field stretched out before him, smoke curling from kitchen chimneys. On the roof of a rustic farmhouse lay a simple, honest-looking boy, gazing blankly at the sky as if lost in thought.

“Goudan, come down for dinner.”

The scene ended abruptly there.

Emerging from the illusion, Li Fan was momentarily dazed.

Sun Luyuan, thinking Li Fan was shocked by the vividness of the vision, explained, “The Alliance Headquarters retains a trace of the Immortal Ancestor’s aura, so…”

Li Fan naturally understood this.

He was thinking of something else entirely.

“That simple boy called Goudan… could that have been the Immortal Ancestor in his youth?”

Recalling the image in his mind, he did find some resemblance to the elder version of the Ancestor.

“Could it be that the Immortal Ancestor was actually born in the Xuanhuang World?”

“No… that doesn’t seem right.”

He replayed the short scene several times in his mind.

“That boy’s appearance seems completely unrelated to the legendary Immortal Ancestor who once dominated Xuanhuang.”

“The confusion in his eyes, followed by the dramatic transformation in his life… that looks more like…”

A word surfaced in Li Fan’s mind.

Transmigration.

“If my earlier deduction is correct, then this boy named Goudan must have been the Heavenly Doctor’s soul transmigrated from another world.”

“He came from the Heavenly Law Realm, with his consciousness slumbering within a fetal body until awakening years later.”

“So this was how he escaped the pursuit of the Ten Immortal Sects and the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao?”

Just as Li Fan was now viewed by the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao as a native cultivator, the Immortal Ancestor back then would have appeared the same, completely undetectable to outside forces.

Li Fan did not yet understand the exact mechanism of such “transmigration.”

But he knew for certain it was very different from mere possession.

“Through some secret art, he must have concealed his fate from Heaven and reincarnated as a Xuanhuang native.”

Li Fan’s thoughts continued to turn slowly.

While he pondered, Sun Luyuan casually asked about the illusion he had seen.

Li Fan replied truthfully that he had seen the image of the Immortal Ancestor.

Sun Luyuan nodded slightly. “It seems, Lu’er, that your future achievements are destined to surpass mine.”

Before Li Fan could ask further, Sun Luyuan explained on his own.

“Every cultivator who comes here for the first time sees a different illusion. It is said to be closely related to one’s true Dao path. Everyone knows that all cultivation methods in the world originate from the Immortal Ancestor. Thus, the closer one’s vision is to the Ancestor himself, the greater one’s future potential.”

“They say that true geniuses can even witness the Ancestor’s very first sermon.”

Sun Luyuan spoke with open admiration.

Li Fan, however, fell silent, his heart growing cautious.

This time, his rapidly rising power had allowed him to glimpse the Ancestor’s childhood.

If he were to advance any further, it might draw the Ancestor’s direct attention.

The two of them traversed the void within the Alliance Headquarters, using formations for teleportation.

Before long, they arrived at the Hall of Guardians.

“Follow me,” Sun Luyuan commanded softly, forming a hand seal.

The Spirit-Transforming Stones beneath their feet grew dim, and the suppressed spiritual power within their bodies once again became active.

“This suppression still affects me somewhat, but far less than it used to at lower realms,” Li Fan thought. “If I were to fight now, I would still be unmatched among Dao Integration cultivators.”

With that calm thought, he followed Sun Luyuan into the Hall of Guardians.

Unlike the waiting chamber where one entered with the Derivative Jade Token, this was the true, inner sanctum of the Hall itself.

A circular dome stretched above them, filled with countless shimmering stars.
