Next

Chapter 1141: The Flowing Gap Left By Time

Looking closely, those star points resembled countless tiny windows.

Through the windows refracted scenes of varying degrees.

Countless characters fluttered about, clearly forming various cultivation techniques.

Under the dome, no Guardian Hall cultivators were visible; only streams of flowing light shuttled continuously through the space.

Sun Family Patriarch Sun Luyuan seemed to be reporting to someone. Soon after, a figure descended from the dome and landed before Li Fan and the other.

It was an avatar formed entirely of flowing characters, not a real person.

He examined Li Fan and nodded slightly: "Little one, I saw you over a hundred years ago; you were just a Golden Core cultivator then. In the blink of an eye, you've grown so much..."

"Follow me."

With that, the characters paved a road straight to the dome.

Li Fan followed Sun Luyuan onto this character road. Moments later, his body seemed to dissolve into countless characters as well, about to scatter across the road like a flowing essay.

A sudden sense of crisis arose in his heart.

Li Fan realized that under the influence of these surrounding characters, the memories in his soul were unconsciously spilling outward, like chapters of a book from his past life.

"Is this a method to probe the visitor's past experiences by sensing soul memories?"

Li Fan pondered this and, in an instant, recreated the memories of Sun Ang's early life in his soul before the possession. He then released them, allowing the surrounding character road to perceive them.

The characters he transformed into bore unique personal markers, distinct from the road beneath his feet.

As for Sun Luyuan, he seemed unaffected by any scrutiny.

The character road soon reached its end, and Li Fan and the other arrived within a starlight window atop the dome.

Their forms reverted from characters to flesh, though Li Fan's experiences had separately formed into a thin booklet.

The guiding cultivator had now revealed his true appearance.

He looked even younger than "Sun Ang," like a seventeen or eighteen-year-old youth.

His long hair draped over his shoulders, with a vermilion dot between his brows. He appeared refined and serene.

"This is Guardian Hall's Elder Nangong," Sun Luyuan introduced to Li Fan.

Nangong glanced at the booklet in his hand and nodded slightly: "No issues. You've passed the first trial."

With a casual toss, the booklet recording "Sun Ang's" life turned into a stream of light and flew off to an unknown destination.

Li Fan, meanwhile, observed his surroundings.

It felt as if they were in the void outside the Xuanhuang Realm, with a massive light cluster floating ahead.

It resembled the miniature model of the Xuanhuang Realm that Li Fan had once seen in the super-distance teleportation formation.

"Pass through the [Xuanhuang Realm]; if you don't cause any oscillation in the light cluster, you've passed the second trial," Nangong said, taking the lead by transforming into escaping light.

Li Fan followed without hesitation.

The yellow halo was dreamlike and illusory, even evoking an inexplicable warmth, like returning to the mother's womb. Without any mishaps, Li Fan soon emerged from the light cluster.

As he left, a faint reluctance even stirred in his heart.

Li Fan also keenly noticed that the circulation speed of his [Xuanhuang Immortal Heart Mantra] had increased several times over its usual rate!

"This is to verify if one is a native cultivator of the Xuanhuang Realm?"

Enlightenment dawned in Li Fan's mind.

The third trial verified whether one was a cultivator of the Five Elders Association.

In the void stood five statues with indistinct faces, encircling the area.

Li Fan needed to stand motionless amid the statues for half an hour.

Only if nothing unusual occurred during this time would he pass the trial.

In this life, Li Fan was clean and untainted. He had no connections whatsoever to the Five Elders Association.

Thus, he passed effortlessly.

"These statues hold some profundity. They seem to carry a trace of the Five Heavenly Sovereigns' power of reversing principles..."

While observed by the Five Elders Association statues, Li Fan observed the five Heavenly Sovereigns in turn.

A realization struck him: "Even if it's not the Heavenly Sovereigns in person, it's not far off."

"If I came after experiencing Senior Sister Zhao's illusion, or even if just a clone had..."

"It would likely trigger a reaction from the statues."

As the five statues dispersed, Li Fan faced the final trial before induction.

The surroundings suddenly plunged into utter nothingness.

Sun Luyuan and Elder Nangong, who had been at his side moments ago, vanished without a trace.

Li Fan frowned slightly and scanned left and right.

He detected no anomalies.

It was no trap, nor any conspiracy.

This was simply a realm of pure void.

Aside from Li Fan, nothing else existed.

"What is this testing?"

Li Fan understood and felt no urgency.

Standing in the void, Li Fan observed what tricks the Guardian Hall was playing.

In the nothingness, even time seemed to lose all meaning.

He did not know how long had passed, but even with Li Fan's level of patience, he began to feel a bit irritated.

It was as if he had been forgotten by the entire world; aside from himself, everything else in existence had vanished.

Elder Nangong still showed no sign of appearing.

"Will they just leave me hanging here like this?"

Due to his innate distrust of others, this thought involuntarily arose in Li Fan's mind.

He even considered revealing his true cultivation, breaking through the mechanism here, and leaving directly.

But in the next instant, Li Fan suppressed it.

Calming his emotions, Li Fan opened the [Truth] panel.

Confirming it was still anchored at eight years, all his unease and irritability vanished completely in that moment.

In the nothingness, he continued to wait.

It felt as if another hundred or thousand years had passed when Li Fan suddenly thought of something.

He began to show signs of impatience.

No longer standing quietly in place, he flew around exploring, as if searching for an exit.

Then, Li Fan gradually amplified the emotions he was displaying.

Yet he never lost control; he merely revealed some negative feelings.

In this way, another hundred or thousand years seemed to pass...

Finally, light returned to the dark nothingness.

Elder Nangong and Sun Luyuan appeared once more.

Li Fan narrowed his eyes slightly, feigning a dazed expression as if emerging from another world. He even examined the two carefully, as if pondering their identities.

"You have passed this final trial as well." A trace of a smile finally appeared on Elder Nangong's face.

Looking at Li Fan, he praised, "Your mental state is quite good. In the Flow of Time Gap, it took over two hundred years for signs of losing control to faintly emerge. And you still maintained your sanity. This result is quite impressive among all previous tests."

After hearing this, Li Fan fell silent for a long time.

As if digesting the information.

Elder Nangong continued to explain, "This trial tests a cultivator's mental stability."

"After all, one must accompany the Derivative Law Jade for over three hundred years without leaving for any reason midway. If one cannot endure the torment and descends into madness, that would be troublesome."

Li Fan suddenly spoke up and asked, "Then, has there ever been a cultivator who remained completely calm from start to finish, showing no impatience at all?"

Previous

Next

Chapter 1142: The Derivation Law Never Rests

Elder Nangong seemed momentarily stunned by Li Fan’s question, as if he had not expected it. After a brief pause, his gaze turned deep and serious.

“If there truly is such a person,” he said slowly, “then all the more reason not to let him approach the Derivation Law Jade.”

“To live alone for several hundred years and yet keep one’s emotions calm and unchanged from beginning to end… such a person must either be a madman or one with an extremely radical temperament.”

Li Fan interrupted, “I have heard that peerless geniuses in seclusion can cultivate for a hundred years without distraction. If they do not lose their composure from isolation, is that not quite normal?”

Elder Nangong chuckled faintly and shook his head with certainty. “Such geniuses would never come here, unless they have ulterior motives.”

Li Fan was momentarily speechless.

At the side, Sun Luyuan’s expression also turned slightly awkward.

“Enough of that,” Elder Nangong said. “Welcome to the Hall of Guardians. Next, I will explain certain matters that require special attention. Beyond the many regulations of the Immortal Alliance, there are additional precautions you must observe when dealing with the Derivation Law Jade.”

“Brother Sun, you may leave now. Your task is considered complete.”

This was precisely the moment Sun Luyuan had been waiting for. Relieved, he sighed softly, offered Li Fan a few more words of advice, and then took his leave.

For nearly half an hour, Elder Nangong continued speaking, his words unhurried and detailed. Then he took Li Fan with him, flying out from the spatial chamber.

They completed the formal procedures of appointment, and Li Fan received the standard materials for a Hall of Guardians Elder.

He changed into the distinctive black-and-gold robe of the Hall and followed Elder Nangong toward the very center of the dome, where the brightest star-like point of light floated.

“When I send you inside to complete the final handover, my duty will finally be over,” Elder Nangong said with a genuine smile of relief.

“Oh, one last reminder,” he added. “You will not be the only Elder guarding this place. There are two others. Unless absolutely necessary, do not attempt to transmit messages or speak to them.”

“The space where the Derivation Law Jade resides records every fluctuation of sound transmission. Designated personnel inspect those records regularly.”

Elder Nangong spoke this via a secret voice transmission, his expression completely impassive, as if the warning did not come from him.

Li Fan cupped his hands in thanks.

After bidding farewell to Elder Nangong, he leapt into the white point of light.

Before his eyes appeared a scene that was already deeply familiar—the realm where the Derivation Law Jade existed.

A colossal sphere composed of countless runic characters floated in the void.

Every instant, the runes shifted endlessly, transforming without pause.

Occasionally, the entire sphere of runes would freeze for a brief moment, emit a pulse of light, and vanish, only to reappear and begin another endless cycle of transformation and deduction.

Beneath the massive sphere, a desk floated quietly in the void.

On it lay a little girl who appeared only five or six years old, her small body resting lazily on the table.

She stared at the characters that continually surfaced across the tabletop, looking listless and bored—exactly as she had each time he had seen her before.

Sensing a new presence, Derivation Law Jade lifted her head slightly.

Upon seeing Li Fan’s stern face and the black-and-gold robe he wore, she pursed her lips, then lowered her head again.

“Another one who looks ugly,” she muttered under her breath.

Li Fan froze for a moment, his expression stiffening slightly.

“Heh, you’ll get used to it,” came an elderly voice beside him.

It was one of the other Dao Integration Elders stationed here, who had been hiding within the space.

“Zhang Xu,” the old man said simply by way of introduction, and then fell silent once more.

As for the other colleague, he had never greeted Li Fan.

Li Fan did not take it to heart and flew toward his “work post.”

The so-called “work post” was actually a special position within this space.

It resembled a key node of a formation. Standing there felt like being high above, allowing one to overlook and perceive every surrounding energy fluctuation clearly.

Most of the time spent guarding Derivation Law Jade was passed at this particular position.

“This is truly worse than being imprisoned. No wonder those few Dao Integration cultivators from the Sun Family were all unwilling to come here.”

Arriving at his post, Li Fan could not help but sigh.

He pretended to observe the surroundings as if familiarizing himself with the environment. In truth, he was secretly contemplating where the weaknesses of the surveillance space might lie.

Other than the constantly shifting light and shadow on the massive sphere of runic characters, the place was quiet—so quiet that it almost felt suffocating.

Within a short time, Li Fan had no clue how to contact Derivation Law Jade secretly or how to break through the restrictions.

Thus, about half a year quietly passed.

The little girl who had been bending over her desk suddenly straightened her back, raised her hands high, and cried out excitedly, “Yay! It’s rest time!”

After saying that, she immediately flopped back down and began to snore softly in her sleep.

“Six hours.”

At that moment, Elder Zhang Xu and another Dao Integration guardian spoke simultaneously.

Six hours—this was the rare rest period granted to Derivation Law Jade.

Once the time was up, she had to be awakened on schedule.

Looking at Derivation Law Jade sleeping so soundly, Li Fan silently sighed in his heart. “This is a bit inhumane. She should at least rest for twelve hours.”

During the half year he had been stationed here, for some reason, no cultivators had entered this space using the Derivative Jade Token.

After some thought, Li Fan finally voiced his doubt to Elder Zhang Xu, since it technically fell within his duties.

Zhang Xu remained silent for a moment before replying, “Three years ago, a spy from the Five Elders Association somehow obtained a Derivative Jade Token and infiltrated this place with ill intentions. Although we discovered and stopped him in time before any major harm occurred, the incident still alarmed the upper ranks of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.”

“Until responsibility for that matter is fully investigated, all Derivative Tokens interactions have been completely suspended.”

“It may take several more years before they resume.”

Zhang Xu said only a few words and then fell silent again.

Li Fan, however, fell into thought. “Each Derivative Jade Token is an immensely precious item, bestowed by the Immortal Alliance upon cultivators who have rendered exceptional service.”

“To think that one actually fell into the hands of the Five Elders Association. And based on Nangong’s previous warning, spies from the Five Elders Association infiltrating the Alliance are not rare. Practically every few decades, an incident like that occurs.”

“In open confrontation, the Five Elders Association cannot match the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. But when it comes to schemes and subterfuge, the Alliance is far inferior.”

Thanks to the heaven-defying abilities of the Tianxuan Mirror’s source power, Li Fan already had a rough idea where the Derivative Jade Tokens that had gone missing were currently located.

“As one of the three stationed guardians, I am under strict surveillance, so taking direct action would be difficult.”

“Perhaps I could take an indirect route—use a Derivative Jade Token to create a private opportunity to meet Derivation Law Jade alone.”

Li Fan pondered silently.

Twelve hours soon passed.

However...

Derivation Law Jade was still lying on the long table, sleeping soundly, showing no sign of waking up.

From the other two Dao Integration guardians, Li Fan faintly sensed a trace of helplessness.

“Brother Sun, it’s your turn.”

Elder Zhang Xu said with a cheerful tone through a voice transmission.

Next

Chapter 1143: The Return Of The Xuanhuang Stars

Li Fan could keenly sense that Derivation Law Jade had long since awakened.

At this moment, she was still sprawled on the long table, clearly pretending to sleep.

The three Dao Integration Guardians were well aware of this, and their request for Li Fan to wake the little girl was merely because he was the "newcomer."

Li Fan understood Derivation Law Jade’s temperament well: childish, vindictive, and petty.

Anyone who disturbed her rest would certainly not receive a friendly look.

As Guardians, their role was solely to monitor her. They truly couldn’t do much to Derivation Law Jade.

When the little ancestor’s temper flared, they could only coax and indulge her.

Noticing the two divine senses fixed on him, Li Fan did not refuse.

Following the method Elder Nangong had taught him earlier, he invoked the sealing power of this Derivation Law Space, conjuring a bronze gong above the little girl’s head.

Then, he struck it hard.

Clang!

Derivation Law Jade’s body jolted, and she abruptly raised her head.

In an instant, she locked onto Li Fan, the culprit, her small face brimming with anger.

Li Fan shrugged, feigning innocence: “Your rest time has exceeded six hours. It’s time to get up and work.”

Derivation Law Jade continued to glare at him with wide eyes, as if he had committed some unforgivable crime.

Li Fan, maintaining a stern expression, continued: “Oh, and per the rules, the excess time will be deducted from your next vacation.”

He paused, touching his face: “Also, the time you’re spending staring at me counts.”

Li Fan seemed unbothered by Derivation Law Jade’s persistent glare, as if inviting her to keep looking.

Upon hearing that her next vacation would be shortened, Derivation Law Jade reacted like a cat with its fur standing on end.

Her large eyes welled up with tears, and she looked aggrievedly at the other two Guardians.

Zhang Xu and the others promptly turned their heads away.

Li Fan, meanwhile, flashed a gentle smile, observing Derivation Law Jade as if watching a good show.

He even conjured an hourglass to track the passing time.

After a tense standoff, as the sand piled up at the bottom of the hourglass, Yanfa Jue finally couldn’t hold back.

She shot Li Fan a fierce glare and slammed her small hand on the table.

The character-filled light spheres, which had been somewhat stagnant, instantly began evolving rapidly again.

Li Fan’s smile remained unchanged.

For hundreds of years here, the only way to pass the time was by teasing the little girl.

While Li Fan successfully infiltrated and grew closer to Derivation Law Jade...

Elsewhere in the Great Qi, Holy Capital:

After eight years of transformative development, the aura of the Holy Dynasty had shifted from crimson to purple.

Li Ping, his clone, had also successfully advanced to the fourth level of the "Heavenly Emperor Qi Classic" through cultivation.

“By harnessing the vast fortune of billions of beings, I now have the strength to contend with a perfected Soul Transformation cultivator of the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“If I develop this small world to its fullest potential over a few more decades, I might even rival a Dao Integration cultivator.”

“But to take it further, to compare with a Longevity Immortal…”

The clone Li Ping shook his head slightly.

"The Great Formation of Myriad Spirits of Heaven and Earth has been preliminarily established. It is time to expand our territory."

With a thought from Li Ping, Sun Erlang and Wang Xuamba soon appeared before him.

"We pay our respects to Master," the two said in unison, bowing respectfully.

With a wave of his hand, the clone Li Ping conjured a map that shimmered like a sky full of stars.

Each white dot represented a Cave-Heaven or a small world.

The location of Great Qi, however, glowed with a sacred purple light.

Sun Erlang gazed at the map, lost in thought.

As his divine sense focused on the white dots, information about the corresponding small worlds flooded his mind.

"I have already explained the current situation of the Xuanhuang Realm to you," Li Ping said.

"For now, we have not yet confronted the forces of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance or the Five Elders Association directly. Therefore, our national policy for the next century is to unite the power of various small worlds and expand the territory of our Holy Dynasty."

"A Federation Of All Worlds, striking back at Xuanhuang."

The clone Li Ping did not shy away from acknowledging the current weakness of the Great Qi Holy Dynasty. Instead, he painted a bright future for his two beloved disciples.

Sun Erlang and Wang Xuamba were not entirely ignorant of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. They even knew of the existence of its highest authorities, such as the Dharma-Transmitter and the Mirror-Holder.

Hearing the grand plan of the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor, Sun Erlang felt no sense of futility, as if they were eggs dashing against a stone.

Instead, his blood boiled with enthusiasm, eager to take action.

This was not due to Sun Erlang’s naivety but because, over the past eight years, he had witnessed the extraordinary changes in the Holy Dynasty. Comparing them to what he had seen of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, he firmly believed that one day, the Holy Dynasty would vanquish the Alliance and unify the Xuanhuang Realm.

In these eight years, the population of the Great Qi Holy Dynasty had quintupled, and the average lifespan had soared from forty to seventy-three years.

Gone were exploitation and oppression; everyone could now study and cultivate.

Compared to the ancient texts’ descriptions of the Sage’s Governance, this was a hundred times more splendid.

Over the past eight years, Sun Erlang had secretly visited nearby small worlds.

They were no different from the corrupt Great Xuan Dynasty of the past.

In some places, the people’s lives were even worse than those of Great Xuan’s citizens.

Sun Erlang believed that spreading the glory of the Holy Dynasty and the Holy Emperor to these decayed small worlds was a noble and honorable mission.

Thus, upon hearing the Holy Emperor’s clear declaration, boundless fighting spirit ignited in Sun Erlang’s heart.

The Great Qi Holy Dynasty now had nine legions.

Each legion commander had reached the Nascent Soul cultivation level.

As the leader of these commanders, Sun Erlang, who practiced the Sitting Immortal Art, had strength approaching the Soul Transformation realm.

If not for the Holy Emperor’s warning that his foundation was unstable and needed further consolidation, he might have already absorbed the life-transformations of heaven and earth to achieve a breakthrough.

To reach Soul Transformation in eight years—Sun Erlang believed this cultivation speed was astonishing, even in the Xuanhuang Realm.

Those so-called prodigies of Xuanhuang could not even compare to the legion commanders under his command.

…

The more he learned, the more confident Sun Erlang became in the Holy Dynasty’s future.

"You have one year to prepare," Li Ping declared.

"One year from now, the Holy Dynasty’s armies will set out from the Holy Capital to conquer the myriad worlds."

With Li Ping’s solemn proclamation, the purple dot representing the Xuanhuang Realm on the map suddenly extended countless purple threads. Like tentacles, they tightly bound the thousands of small worlds in the Xuanhuang Realm.

They wove into a vast net, enveloping the entire map of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Witnessing this, Sun Erlang and Wang Xuamba held their breath, their hearts surging with excitement.

Filled with this fervor, Sun Erlang eagerly departed to summon the legion commanders and plan for the upcoming war.

Wang Xuamba, however, stayed behind, entrusted with a secret mission from the Holy Emperor.

“‘All Stars Return to Their Position’?” Wang Xuamba murmured, puzzled by the mission’s codename.

“First, go to the Congyun Sea and Yin Yin Island to find a cultivator named Master Yin,” he repeated to himself.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1144: Witnessing Across The Ages

“After locating Master Yin, persuade him and bring him back to the Great Qi Dynasty.”

Looking at the brief command that contained only a few words, Wang Xuanba couldn’t help but scratch his head.

Although he didn’t fully understand what those words implied, it did not stop him from believing in them completely.

With a low growl, the shadow beneath his feet began to writhe and twist. Before long, a figure identical to Wang Xuanba crawled out from it.

This was the ability he had awakened after fusing with and absorbing the alien beast [Shadowlike].

The shadow clone possessed half the strength of the true body, and even if it were to die, the original body would not be harmed in the slightest.

For such a task that carried a certain amount of risk, it was the perfect substitute.

With that thought, Wang Xuanba’s shadow form transformed into a dark, serpentine dragon and vanished into the skies.

More than ten days later, he finally found the location of Yin Yin Island.

From the island came faint, mournful wails that sent chills down Wang Xuanba’s spine.

He called out several times, but no one answered. Entering the building, he saw a multitude of strange and grotesque creatures, each more disturbing than the last. The sight made his scalp prickle.

“These things are even more revolting than the alien beast I fused with…”

Going deeper inside, he finally found the one he was looking for—standing before a transparent cage.

“Sir, my master wishes to invite you for a meeting,” Wang Xuanba said calmly, neither humble nor arrogant.

Master Yin showed no response whatsoever.

Wang Xuanba repeated himself several times, yet the man remained motionless.

Seeing this, Wang Xuanba decided to speak directly as instructed by the Holy Emperor.

“Sir, do you wish to know who created the Immortal-Mortal Miasma?”

Master Yin’s body trembled. Slowly, his gaze shifted away from his experimental subjects and fell upon Wang Xuanba.

“What did you just say…?”

His overlapping voice echoed eerily, making Wang Xuanba swallow hard.

Even so, he gathered his courage and repeated his words.

Before the other could respond, he continued, “Sir, do you wish to know the way to perfect the ‘Foundation Built Upon Oneself’ method?”

Master Yin’s once-hunched figure gradually straightened.

He fixed his sharp eyes on Wang Xuanba, a dangerous glint flashing within them. “Who exactly are you?”

Wang Xuanba chuckled softly. “I’m merely a messenger. If you wish to know the answers, then come with me.”

“Lead the way.”

To Wang Xuanba’s surprise, Master Yin agreed without hesitation.

Behind him, dense fog gushed out from the cage, and the screams of several mortals echoed before fading into silence.

Wang Xuanba tensed up and quickly nodded.

“Ah, my master also asked me to remind you not to forget that rib bone—bring it along.”

Master Yin’s eyes grew cold, his killing intent barely contained.

But looking at Wang Xuanba’s fearless demeanor, he forced the murderous urge down.

After searching his body, he found nothing.

At last, he discovered the rib bone inside the den of a small dog with a human hand for a paw.

Kicking the dog aside, Master Yin signaled that they could leave.

Wang Xuanba flew ahead to guide the way, passing through the formation-concealed entrance to a small world and returning to the Great Qi Dynasty.

The dense spiritual energy of this realm made Immortal Master Yin slightly surprised, but soon his attention was drawn to the towering spire within the Holy Capital City.

He could sense an extremely powerful presence residing within.

“Fellow Daoist, what is the meaning of luring me here with such elaborate effort?”

Master Yin’s expression was far from pleasant.

“Nothing much. I have long admired your talent, that’s all.” Seated high upon the Holy Emperor’s Throne, Li Ping looked down at him and spoke faintly.

“How do you find this method?”

Before Master Yin could respond, Li Ping lightly tapped his fingers.

A curtain of light unfolded before him.

At first, Master Yin showed little interest, but when his eyes scanned the content within, his expression instantly turned solemn.

The solemn expression on his face instantly turned into shock.

“This…”

He repeatedly examined it several times before finally raising his gaze toward the clone, Li Ping, with disbelief in his eyes.

“I have heard that you possess a rib bone of great value, sir?” Clone Li Ping did not explain further but instead asked another question.

Elder Yin nodded. “Indeed. It is the bone of the very first cultivator in this world who was infected by the Immortal-Mortal Miasma.”

“May I take a look at it?”

Master Yin hesitated for a moment, but in the end, he still handed it over to Li Ping.

Li Fan held the rib bone in his hand, falling into deep reminiscence.

After a long sigh, his face showed a trace of melancholy and sorrow.

He then returned the bone to Master Yin.

Li Ping’s behavior left Master Yin uneasy and full of doubt.

“You…”

“To see an old friend again, only to find him reduced to such a state. I merely felt some emotion, sir. Please do not take offense.”

Li Ping’s words struck Master Yin’s ears like a thunderclap.

“What? Old friend?”

His eyes were full of disbelief as he stared fixedly at Li Ping.

Li Ping remained calm, his tone low and faint. “Heh… other than me, who else in this world still remembers him?”

“The Great Xuanhuang Celestial Venerable… truly a perfect being. Su Bai… Mr. Bai…”

As he spoke, an ethereal figure appeared before Master Yin.

In an instant, the image seized Master Yin’s attention.

The man in the vision was so flawless in appearance that not a single imperfection could be found upon him.

His eyes carried gentleness and affection. Even though the image was illusory, meeting his gaze stirred one’s very soul.

Master Yin stared blankly for a long time.

Then, recalling Li Ping’s words, his body trembled violently as realization dawned. “What did you say? The Great Xuanhuang Celestial Venerable?”

He subconsciously tightened his grip on the rib bone as though struck by lightning.

His eyes went dull, and he muttered under his breath. “To defy the will of Heaven itself… to defy the will of Heaven…”

“The Immortal-Mortal Miasma… Dual Heavenly Sovereigns…”

“How could this be possible?”

…

Master Yin staggered, collapsing helplessly to the ground.

It was as if he had suffered a tremendous blow. His entire being seemed lost, directionless, and overwhelmed by despair.

Li Ping waited patiently until Master Yin slowly regained his composure from the shock.

Only then did he speak again, revealing his true intent.

“Before us, The path to immortality lies ahead, yet it cannot be trodden. for the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor stands above.”

“Sir, would you be willing to aid me in fulfilling Mr.Bai’s unaccomplished wish—to use the New Foundation Method to shatter the very root of the Immortal Ancestor, and save Xuanhuang from calamity?”

Li Ping descended from the imperial throne, came before Master Yin, and respectfully helped him up as he spoke solemnly.

Master Yin quickly understood the meaning behind Li Ping’s words.

“You mean to say… this flawless ‘Foundation Built Upon Oneself Method’ was crafted by him?” Master Yin’s voice trembled slightly.

Li Ping nodded, sighing. “Indeed. Within the entire Xuanhuang Realm, who other than him could possess such talent and insight?”

Master Yin’s heart was shaken even further.

“Yes… yes…” he muttered repeatedly.

Looking once more at the glowing script upon the light screen, Master Yin felt as though he could see through time itself—his gaze meeting Mr. Bai’s across the ages.

“My thoughts and understanding… they actually align with his, despite thousands of years apart?”

“Moreover… he far surpasses me in every way…”

…

Li Fan-ping looked at the completely entranced Master Yin and knew that the plan had succeeded.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1145: The Immortal Ancestor And The Old Acquaintance

“Can you tell me his story?”

Master Yin sat paralyzed on the ground, his expression dazed, his voice faintly trembling as he finally broke the long silence.

Not long ago, still clinging to a sliver of hope, Master Yin had conducted a final test in the underground prison of the Holy Capital.

The result was exactly as Li Ping had said.

He no longer spoke further. Instead, he carefully stored away the rib bone of Mr. Bai.

Then he returned to the Holy Emperor’s throne hall, sitting before the projected image of Mr. Bai, staring blankly at it.

Li Ping did not disturb him, but also stood silently, eyes fixed on the same portrait, as if in remembrance.

At this moment, when Master Yin finally spoke, it was precisely what Li Ping had been waiting for.

He immediately began to recount the general story of Mr. Bai’s life.

Master Yin listened intently.

“I only knew that in the ancient Xuanhuang Realm, there existed Great Celestial Venerable above all Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns,” he murmured. “But I never expected…”

“That such a figure would sacrifice himself for mere mortals.”

Confusion and sorrow intertwined on Master Yin’s face.

“The departed are gone,” Li Ping said solemnly. “What we can do now is fulfill Mr. Bai’s final wish, to console his spirit in the heavens.”

“The reason I established this Holy Dynasty of Qi was also for the sake of these mortals. Tell me, sir, compared with the Xuanhuang Realm, how are the lives of mortals within my dynasty?”

Master Yin turned his gaze outward, as if in that instant his sight reached across every corner of Great Qi.

After a long silence, he nodded slightly, sighing with admiration. “Compared to the Xuanhuang Realm, where mortals are raised and treated like cattle and horses, your land can indeed be considered a paradise for ordinary people.”

“My vision,” Li Ping said proudly, “is to extend this system across all small worlds of Xuanhuang, and eventually to the Xuanhuang Realm itself.”

Master Yin studied Li Ping for a moment and sighed, yet did not refute him.

Li Ping smiled faintly. “Sir, there is no need for despair. Please, hear me out.”

“The strength of the Xuanhuang Realm lies in Immortal Ancestor. The invincibility of Immortal Ancestor lies in innovation. If we can use the method passed down by Mr. Bai to disrupt the very foundation of Immortal Ancestor…”

“Then once Immortal Ancestor collapses, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and even the Five Elders Association will be nothing more than clay chickens and pottery dogs.”

Master Yin stared blankly at Li Ping. He could not understand how a cultivator whose aura was only at the Soul Transformation realm could speak with such audacity.

Yet more astonishingly, he sensed from Li Ping an unwavering confidence.

“It does not sound like an empty boast. Could he be concealing his true strength?” Master Yin thought, carefully examining him again.

“To shatter the foundation of Immortal Ancestor,” Li Ping said with utmost solemnity, “there is no one in this world more suited than you, sir.”

Master Yin hesitated. “With my talent alone, I fear that may be impossible…”

Li Ping stepped forward, clasping both of Master Yin’s hands tightly. “Sir, I believe in you.”

His eyes shone with genuine sincerity. “If ten years is not enough, then a hundred. If a hundred years fail, then a thousand. I have waited so long for even this faint glimmer of hope. How could I possibly give it up?”

“Surely you do not wish to disappoint Mr. Bai’s expectations either, do you?”

Master Yin’s body trembled slightly. His fists clenched unconsciously.

“Just focus on developing a new Dharma method,” Li Ping continued. “Leave everything else to me.”

After this long conversation, Master Yin finally resolved to stay and devote himself to researching a new cultivation method that would follow after Foundation Establishment.

After all, for him, lifespan was no longer a concern. Without a meaningful purpose, it would only be a matter of time before he lost all self-awareness and merged completely with the wandering souls of the world.

Having gained Master Yin’s promise, Li Ping was overjoyed.

They immediately led him into a secret chamber beneath the Holy Capital City.

It was a vast underground area that had long been prepared as a private research facility for Master Yin.

“I have a plan, sir. Please see if it is feasible.”

“Select a large number of gifted but ignorant young children, and grant them this [Foundation Built Upon Oneself Method]. Then afterward…”

Li Fan explained in full detail the method he had once used.

Master Yin pondered for a moment, his expression turning grave. “It might be feasible. But to know for sure, it will still need to be tested in practice.”

Li Fan waved his hand. “The thoughts of children are unrestrained like wild horses. Even if they cannot truly stumble upon a path by chance, as long as it gives you a hint of inspiration, sir, that will be enough.”

Master Yin was clearly not a pedantic man. He nodded and approved of Li Fan’s plan.

As for the miserable lives of those chosen children, he felt little sympathy.

“By the way, there is another matter that has weighed heavily on my heart. I have long wanted to complete it, yet never found the way…” Li Ping paused, appearing somewhat hesitant.

Master Yin glanced at Li Ping, gesturing for him to continue.

“Sir Bai’s remains are scattered across the Xuanhuang Realm. I wish to gather them and bury them properly. The others are fine, mostly kept in private by cultivators. But…”

“His skull is kept by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance in their main headquarters, within the Divine Treasure Museum.”

A flicker of hostility flashed in Li Ping’s eyes as he said with hatred, “Those insects of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance actually dare to handle it carelessly, displaying it for public viewing without the slightest respect. Every time I think of this, I wish to rush in regardless of the cost and reclaim that skull myself.”

“Unfortunately, I have tried once. There was absolutely no chance of success. Within that Divine Treasure Museum seems to be hidden a mysterious expert. Placing Mr. Bai’s skull at the very center of the museum was also his idea. It is clearly a trap, meant to lure people like me who care deeply about Mr. Bai.”

“You have entered the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters before?” Master Yin was visibly shocked.

After hearing Li Ping describe every scene and detail of his infiltration, Master Yin’s gaze toward him subtly changed.

“I could infiltrate once, and I could do it again. But that Divine Treasure Museum alone…”

“…is truly difficult to deal with.” Li Ping sighed.

At this time, Master Yin spoke. “Fellow Daoist, do not be discouraged. In truth, I once had the same thought. But after discovering the identity of the museum’s curator, I gave up.”

“This person is one of the three Emperor Sovereigns of the ancient Demonic Beast Clan, existing since primordial times. Even in those days, the Immortal Ancestor treated him with courtesy.”

Li Ping frowned slightly. “Such power? In those days, Mr. Bai slaughtered those monster beasts like chickens and dogs. The Immortal Ancestor was even stronger than Mr. Bai. With his strength and status, why would he treat that Emperor with such respect?𝒍.𝙘𝒐𝒎

His tone was full of doubt. Clearly, Li Ping did not believe Master Yin’s words.

Master Yin shook his head slightly. “In any case, I am certain that when the Immortal Ancestor met that being, he truly acted so. His attitude was entirely different from how he treated others.”

“Either that being’s strength earned the Ancestor’s recognition, or…”

Master Yin paused. “They were already acquainted.”

“But that should have been their first meeting,” Master Yin said in a lowered voice, as if confused even himself.

Li Ping knew that Master Yin was a remnant soul of the old world devoured by the Xuanhuang Realm, possessing fragmentary memories of a bygone heaven and earth. Therefore, what he saw could not be false.

“Emperor Sanmo was originally an elder of the Beast-Taming Sect, one of the Ten Great Immortal Sects. After conflict arose between the Ten Sects and the Immortal Ancestor, he chose to side with the Immortal Ancestor. Thousands of years later, he transformed into the curator of the Divine Treasure Museum, and even became one of the three supreme ruling powers of the Immortal Alliance—the Illusory True Sage…”

“An old acquaintance then?”

Li Ping suddenly thought of another question.

Did the Heavenly Law Realm have any monster beasts?

The Xuanhuang monster beasts all originated from Emperor Yi’s transformations.

Then what about the Heavenly Law Realm?

…

At the same time, in the Derivation Space of the Immortal Alliance Headquarters, the true body of Li Fan suddenly revealed a faint, almost imperceptible glimmer in his eyes.

“Perhaps, I will need to make another trip to the Fallen Immortal Realm.”

Chapter 1146: Qingxuan Shang Shaojun

Emperor Yi Beast had long led the demon beast clan to flee the Xuanhuang Realm, while Emperor Sanmo was deeply hidden within the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, rarely seen by ordinary people.

However, both had appeared during the [Beast Taming Sect Upheaval] in the Fallen Immortal Realm, so one could seek them out directly for answers.

"Fellow Daoist Sun?"

Elder Zhang Xu's voice transmission interrupted Li Fan's thoughts.

His attention returned to the guarded space of the Derivation Law Jade. The little girl was handling an urgent derivation task.

Assigned an extra task for no reason, she looked visibly upset, her face full of resentment.

The task required deriving a Dao Integration technique, yet three days and nights had passed without success.

It was highly likely this little lady was slacking off on purpose.

In the derivation space, besides the three Dao Integration guardians, there was a young man in green robes.

Though his posture was rigid and unmoving, his demeanor betrayed his inner anxiety.

Considering the high level of this derivation task, any delay could cause trouble. Guardian Zhang Xu secretly sent a voice transmission, asking Li Fan to play the villain.

After all, in recent times, Li Fan’s image in the Derivation Law Jade’s eyes had already fallen into the category of the most detestable people in the world.

No need for a gong to serve as a reminder.

The moment a silent hourglass appeared above the Derivation Law Jade’s head, the little girl’s expression changed drastically, and the speed of the rotating character light orb increased several times over.

Not long after, she successfully derived the technique the green-robed youth needed.

The youth thanked her profusely and hurriedly left.

"He’s in such a rush. It seems the Qingxuan Army has run into trouble," Zhang Xu remarked.

After repeatedly stopping the Derivation Law Jade’s slacking behavior, Li Fan had gradually become familiar with the other two Dao Integration guardians.

Zhang Xu had arrived over a hundred years before Li Fan.

The other, named Hua Yueye, had a name that sounded somewhat feminine but was undoubtedly a fierce man.

He despised evil and had once fought a Dharma-Transmitter on the spot for their misdeeds.

The consequences were predictable.

Though spared from death with the help of friends and family, he was sentenced to guard the Derivation Law Jade for five hundred years.

Not even half that time had passed yet.

When Li Fan first arrived, Hua Yueye barely acknowledged him. Now, after growing closer, he was even chattier than Zhang Xu.

"Though I can’t stand Shang Shaojun’s attitude, guarding the Demon Abyss for centuries without stepping away even once… you have to admire that," Hua Yueye said.

"The Qingxuan Army…"

From their casual conversation, Li Fan recalled the identity of the green-robed cultivator.

He had met him once before—his name was Yan Huan.

A trusted subordinate of Shang Shaojun, stationed in the Broken Palm Immortal City within the white mist, responsible for guarding and suppressing the [Chizha Abyss] alongside a million Qingxuan Army soldiers.

"Young Lord Shang has a temper even more stubborn than mine. Once he sets his mind on something, even a Heavenly Sovereign might not sway him. That proposal, rejected by both the Dharma-Transmitter and the Mirror Holder, he still hasn’t given up on after all these years."

"Ahem, watch your words!"

Based on fragmented conversations and previously uncovered secrets, Li Fan pieced together a hidden chapter of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance’s past.

Years ago, the Alliance had once advocated for "venturing deep into the white mist regions to expand exploration."

This differed from the great exploration era Li Fan had experienced. In that era, the Tianxuan Mirror’s explorations were akin to scanning the entire white mist, marking out hidden regions and secret realms without delving deeply into them.

However, the proposal, which was vetoed by the Dharma-Transmitters, called for a thorough cleansing of these mist regions. The white mist barriers of the Xuanhuang Realm appeared too suspiciously, and the regions within were bizarre, concealing countless secrets.

From his own encounters, Li Fan recalled a world fragment destroyed by the Chaos Secret Script, an ice-sealing formation that reversed life and death, and a giant composed of countless corpses. Setting aside the dangers, Li Fan did not believe the monks of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance lacked curiosity about these regions.

Yet, this curiosity was decisively suppressed by the Alliance’s higher-ups.

"The Dharma-Transmitter’s dismemberment, the remaking of the Xuanhuang Realm, and the unified actions of the Alliance’s leaders clearly indicate that these mist regions are deeply tied to Him," Li Fan mused.

He recalled that, while in the white mist, he often heard wails and murmurs akin to those of the Heavenly Doctor. Following these sounds, he consistently discovered unknown regions. This led to a hypothesis.

"These regions, like the Congyun Sea and Luoyan Prefecture, are likely remnants of the former Heavenly Law Realm. However, perhaps due to the influence of disasters like the Chaos Script Art, their surviving laws are more distorted and chaotic, unsuitable for direct integration with the Xuanhuang Realm."

"Thus, they were hidden within the white mist, possibly alongside remnants of other cultivation realms."

"The Immortal Ancestor is waiting—waiting for the moment the Heavenly Law Realm successfully resurrects."

Though Li Fan’s attainments had not yet reached the level of the Immortal Ancestor, he could not fully discern the specific purpose of these world fragments. Still, this realization sparked inspiration.

"Though the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor holds absolute control, the cultivators of the Xuanhuang Realm harbor doubts about the drastic changes He has wrought. Perhaps I can guide them to uncover the truth."

Li Fan knew that the more something was forbidden, the more it fueled curiosity. Once these cultivators discovered the potential grand conspiracy involving the Immortal Ancestor, willingly or not, they would become his allies.

"Shang Shaojun will be the starting point," Li Fan decided, nodding slightly as he concealed the glint in his eyes.

…

At the ruins of the Heavenly Sword Sect, in the Sword Tomb, the Holy Embryo Li Fan sat cross-legged beside the Black Sword, immersing himself in the myriad sword paths swirling around him.

Though he had previously built his Foundation Establishment with the Black Sword Heavenly Wonder, being surrounded by countless swords in the Sword Tomb now sparked new insights in his heart.

"The Wonders of Heaven are manifestations of the Heavenly Dao’s laws," he reflected. "As the world shifts, its laws flow and transform, never static. The timing of my visits to collect the Black Sword varies, resulting in subtle differences in the sword paths it generates."

The Holy Embryo Li Fan felt a surge of joy, his understanding of the world’s operations deepening. With a sudden realization, he let out a long cry. The thousands of swords on the ground trembled faintly in response.

At the center of the myriad swords, the wooden sword left by Master Bai even bent slightly, as if resonating with the Holy Embryo Li Fan’s insights.

"Good," he murmured.

With a slight parting of his lips, the Black Sword soared into the air, entering his body and merging as his Foundation Establishment.

Chapter 1147: One Sword Suppresses The Demonic Sound

This time, when obtaining the Heavey Wonder, the Holy Embryo Li Fan had already received prior acknowledgment.

Therefore, the Wooden Sword did not obstruct him.

The mysterious aura contained within the Wooden Sword gradually faded away.

A subtle and profound fluctuation began to surface upon the Holy Embryo Li Fan’s body.

At this moment, the Xuanhuang Sword Dao completed its transference.

Amidst the countless rusted swords within the Sword Tomb bending slightly in salute, the Holy Embryo Li Fan slowly departed.

“Possessing the Heavey Wonder allows one to directly step into the Dao Integration Realm.”

“The Sword’s Foundation, the Sword’s Law, the Sword transforming into its own Cave Heaven. And beyond that...”

Li Fan’s true body, which had already reached the Soul Transformation Realm, instantly comprehended the path the Holy Embryo would take next.

“The life transformation of the Xuanhuang Sword Dao itself.”

“After that, once the Heavey Wonder completely fuses with one’s being, it will be equivalent to devouring and fully mastering the power of Heaven and Earth’s laws, thus attaining the level of Dao Integration Immortal Sage.”

“Compared to ordinary cultivators of the new-method era, it is indeed far easier. But the price...”

“...is that it almost completely severs the possibility of defying the natural order to attain Longevity. Just like Mr. Bai, who as the Great Celestial Venerable of Xuanhuang, rebelled against the very laws he governed. Even one as supremely gifted as he was, despite succeeding in defiance, could not escape the backlash of Heaven and Earth.”

“The day of enlightenment would also be the day of one’s fall.”

The Holy Embryo Li Fan’s thoughts surged in his mind, while his cultivation realm steadily ascended step by step.

When he left the Sword Tomb, he was merely at the early stage of the Foundation Establishment Realm.

By the time he stepped out of Tianyou Prefecture, the Holy Embryo Li Fan had advanced to the Golden Core Realm.

When he arrived before the white fog barrier concealing the Chizha Abyss, he had already entered the ranks of the Nascent Soul realm.

Without any deliberate effort, without any resistance, the spiritual energy of Heaven and Earth surged toward him on its own, flowing naturally into his body.

It became a steady ladder for his continual ascension.

“This is the benefit of being recognized by the Heavenly Dao.”

“And this is merely the path of the Sword. If it were the entire Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao itself, it would be unimaginable. No wonder Mr. Bai could ascend to the Soul Transformation Realm in a single step and instantly attain longevity...”

With the Heavenly Wonder upon him, his cultivation no longer required any external resources.

Moreover, with the momentum of Heaven and Earth actively assisting him, the Holy Embryo Li Fan’s cultivation speed could no longer be measured by conventional standards.

The fluctuations of his sword intent dispersed around him, forcing back the devouring white mist.

Transforming into a streak of sword light, the Holy Embryo Li Fan flew straight toward Broken Palm Immortal City.

When faint wailing sounds, resembling the cries of countless vengeful spirits, reached his ears, the Holy Embryo Li Fan’s cultivation had already reached the same level as his true body—the Soul Transformation Realm.

However, after breaking through to the Soul Transformation Realm, his comprehension of the evolution of his sword path remained incomplete.

As a result, his progress slowed down significantly.

“If I polish and refine it further in this lifetime, then should I begin anew in another, my advancement will be even faster.”

“Stop right there!” A furious voice suddenly interrupted the Holy Embryo Li Fan’s thoughts.

A figure appeared before him.

Clad in armor made of azure-scaled light, wielding a massive halberd, the man pointed the weapon straight at Li Fan.

“Who are you? How dare you trespass into the Eternal Immortal Fortress?” The tone of the armored cultivator was harsh.

Li Fan’s eyes narrowed slightly as he recognized that voice.

During the 114th cycle, he had been ordered to construct the Tianxuan Spirit-Locking Formation within the white mist outside the Eternal Immortal Fortress.

Later, when the Chizha Abyss suddenly stirred, his Jiaxu Outpost lost all contact with the Immortal City.

As their supplies ran low, Li Fan reversed his Dao Heart cultivation, journeying alone from the outpost back to the city in search of aid.

Although he encountered several obstacles and mistakenly wandered into the Maze of the Chaos Character, he eventually escaped unscathed.

In the end, just before his lifespan reached its limit, he barely managed to return to Broken Palm Immortal City.

He originally had decades of life remaining, but at the final moment, a thunderous voice shouting “Who dares approach!” blocked his path.

Li Fan had to stop and explain his purpose, wasting precious time.

By the time he finally reentered the Immortal City, his life force had nearly run dry, and he did not have much time left to live.

…

"Hmph!"

Holy Embryo Li Fan glanced faintly at the other party, offering no explanation.

With his finger as a sword, he lightly pointed forward.

As if parted by an ancient force, the white mist that had existed since time immemorial split instantly.

The wails from the Chizha Abyss were suppressed by the resonant hum of a sword.

A dazzling white sword light illuminated the heavens and earth.

The Eternal Immortal Fortress, crafted from the severed palm of the Ancient Heavenly Sovereign, and the terrifying abyss filled with malevolent energy...

At this moment, both were laid bare under Holy Embryo Li Fan's sword light, with no place to hide.

The majestic sword light passed through the figure standing before Li Fan, crossed the Immortal City of the Broken Palm, and pierced fiercely into the Chizha Abyss!

The world fell silent.

As if awed by this world-shaking sword, the wails from the abyss did not resound for a long time.

Only when the white mist slowly gathered, healing the torn wound, and the abyss was once again concealed beneath the mist, did the murmuring cries gradually resume.

But compared to their previous intensity, they were far diminished.

Perhaps they feared that a louder sound would provoke another of Li Fan's swords.

Snapping out of their shock, the Qingxuan Army within the Eternal Immortal Fortress fixed their gazes on Li Fan's position.

All were silent.

Even the thick white mist could not obscure the figure that seemed like a Sword Immortal.

No one understood the terror of those demonic sounds better than the Qingxuan Army, who guarded the abyss day and night.

Yet now, countless demonic sounds had been subdued by a single sword.

What level of cultivation was this?

The Qingxuan Army stood in a daze, their minds blank.

Holy Embryo Li Fan, hands clasped behind his back, calmly gazed ahead.

The azure-scaled armored cultivator before him had already turned to stone.

Having faced Li Fan's sword directly, though the sword light did not harm him, it had etched itself deeply into his mind.

From this day forward, his mind would hold nothing but this sword light.

Like a fool, he would no longer react to the outside world.

But conversely, if he could comprehend this sword and transform it into his own understanding, for someone at the Nascent Soul stage, this sword would be an immense fortune.

Even without resources to advance, this sword alone could allow him to slay enemies above his level.

For the current Li Fan, there was no need to settle every score with those who had offended him in the past.

If he happened to encounter them again, a lesson would suffice.

This sword from Holy Embryo Li Fan was primarily a demonstration, meant to force the Qingxuan Army's leader, Shang Shaojun, to appear.

After all, displaying his strength first would make the subsequent discussion smoother.

As expected, faced with Li Fan standing in the air within the white mist, not a single member of the Qingxuan Army dared to approach.

Moments later, the white mist rippled.

A strange, wavering figure, like ripples on water, appeared before Li Fan.

"Excellent swordsmanship, Fellow Daoist!"

"I've guarded the Chizha Abyss for years, yet I never imagined someone like you would emerge in this world!"

Shang Shaojun stared at Li Fan: "What brings you here, Fellow Daoist?"

Pointing to the Chizha Abyss not far below, Li Fan smiled and said, "The demonic sounds are grating and vexing. I intend to wield my sword and enter the abyss to silence them forever."

"Would Young Lord care to join me?"

Holy Embryo Li Fan's gaze was sincere, his words utterly earnest.

Chapter 1148: Single-Handedly Suppressing The Demonic Tide

"Would you be willing to join me?"

Li Fan's voice thundered, piercing through the white mist, momentarily overpowering the wails echoing from the Chizha Abyss, and reverberating endlessly within the Eternal Immortal Fortress.

Feeling the countless gazes of the Qingxuan Army upon him, Shang Shaojun's wavering figure steadied.

His true form emerged.

Clad in a flowing azure robe, his tall and slender frame stood upright. A sharp glint flashed in his eyes as Shang Shaojun stared at Li Fan: "Fellow Daoist, you seem overly confident. Your sword may be sharp, but it may not withstand the terrifying surge of the demonic sound tide. And you still dream of eternally suppressing the abyss…"

"How would we know without trying? I see, Shaojun, that years of guarding the abyss have worn down your courage!" Holy Embryo Li Fan retorted without yielding, meeting him head-on.

"Hmph, mere words."

In an instant, it seemed as though thousands of shadows emerged around Shang Shaojun. He appeared to meld into this shadowy tide, lurking within, ready to strike a fatal blow at any moment.

Meanwhile, Holy Embryo Li Fan felt as if he were surrounded by an army of thousands, even experiencing the illusion of facing the Chizha Abyss alone.

"Rumors within the Immortal Alliance suggest that the demonic sound of the abyss benefits Shang Shaojun's cultivation, which is why he willingly guards this place for centuries without leaving. It seems the rumors are true."

When Li Fan was still at the Foundation Establishment stage, he had experienced the terror of the abyss's demonic sound up close.

Now, Shang Shaojun seemed like a walking abyss, capable of unleashing a demonic sound tide at any moment!

Above Holy Embryo Li Fan's head, a plain, unadorned black longsword suddenly materialized.

Its tip pointed directly at Shang Shaojun.

Despite the onslaught of countless demonic sounds, the moment the sword locked onto him, a faint sense of crisis arose in Shang Shaojun's heart.

It was as if he saw, beyond the black sword, countless other swords raining down from the sky, all aimed at him!

Shang Shaojun's heart grew heavy.

The two faced off in the void, a battle on the verge of erupting.

At this critical moment, a strange fluctuation came from nearby.

At first, it was a faint rustling, like the sharp tearing of autumn winds.

Then, it surged like the rapid hoofbeats of galloping horses, erupting like a sudden storm.

It resembled the cries of ten thousand ghosts from an endless hell or the despairing wails echoing through a world on the brink of apocalypse.

The white mist churned, roiling ceaselessly.

The Broken Palm Immortal City, like a small boat on the sea, trembled violently, teetering on the edge of collapse!

"The demonic sound tide!"

Perhaps due to the suppression caused by Li Fan's sword light earlier, the abyss's power had been provoked into a rebound.

This time, the intensity and scale of the demonic sound tide far exceeded anything before.

The Eternal Immortal Fortress, directly facing the tide's impact, seemed like a leaf on a branch, liable to be blown away at any moment!

Shang Shaojun's expression changed drastically. No longer paying attention to Li Fan, his figure flashed as he returned to the Broken Palm Immortal City.

With Shang Shaojun holding the fort, the Azure Light shimmering ring swirled around, and only then did the Eternal Immortal Fortress gradually stabilize amid the Demonic Sound Tide.

Just as the Qingxuan Army soldiers were still reeling in shock, they suddenly discovered to their horror that Li Fan, whom they had thought had vanished into the Demonic Sound Tide, was still standing quietly in the void.

Even with the demonic sounds raging around him and the white mist churning.

He remained utterly unmoved.

And then, Li Fan did something that no one could have anticipated.

He strolled leisurely, as if taking a casual walk, to the outskirts of Broken Palm Immortal City.

Facing the endless Chizha Abyss, Li Fan sat down cross-legged.

A pitch-black longsword appeared precisely beneath him at that moment.

Li Fan crooked his right finger and lightly tapped the sword's body.

"Ding!"

The crisp sound rang out, standing out sharply even amid the frenzied tide of demonic sounds!

The sword's hum echoed as countless different sword shadows instantly materialized behind Li Fan.

The sword shadows stretched endlessly, like a great wall, blocking the way before Broken Palm Immortal City.

Ten thousand swords stood like a forest, all pointed toward the abyss!

"The demonic sounds pierce the mind, yet he is utterly unaffected! How on earth is he doing this?"

"He is sitting right in front of the city; what is he up to?"

This shocking scene left the million-strong Qingxuan Army utterly dazed.

Even Shang Shaojun abruptly rose to his feet, gazing from afar at Li Fan's back outside the city.

If the great tide of demonic sounds was an endless, surging, and violently raging ocean wave.

Then Li Fan at this moment was a massive boulder standing firm by the sea,

Unwavering and immovable in all directions.

No matter how the demonic sounds ravaged, they could not harm him in the slightest.

And Li Fan's display had only just begun.

"Go!"

Before the great tide, Li Fan uttered a soft command.

From the great wall formed by the myriad sword shadows behind him, one sword shot forth in an instant.

Just like the world-shaking sword light Li Fan had unleashed earlier, it charged straight into the Demonic Sound Great Tide with a whistle.

In the face of the surging Demonic Sound Tide, the sword light gradually dimmed.

But the Qingxuan Army soldiers inside Broken Palm Immortal City could clearly feel that the tide's power had weakened by a fraction because of that sword light!

"Could it be..."

A thought involuntarily surfaced in their minds.

The bodies of everyone in the immortal city trembled slightly.

Then, a scene they would never forget in their lifetimes unfolded.

Myriad flying swords charged forward one after another, charging selflessly toward the Chizha Abyss.

Countless sword lights flashed in succession, illuminating the Eternal Immortal Fortress completely.

Each strike weakened the Demonic Sound Tide by a fraction.

The terrifying wails that echoed in everyone's ears also gradually grew feeble and listless.

The vast immortal city fell utterly silent.

Everyone watched Li Fan's back in quiet reverence.

No room for any other thoughts.

Like a heavy rain finally letting up, clearing the skies after the storm.

Outside the immortal city, the demonic sounds ceased entirely.

Yet the sword lights behind Li Fan remained in abundance.

Boundless and inexhaustible, eternal and unending.

"That lord..."

"Perhaps what he said earlier about eternally suppressing the abyss is not impossible after all."

This idea surfaced simultaneously in the minds of the Qingxuan Army soldiers.

With his back to Broken Palm Immortal City, Li Fan did not rise.

The Qingxuan Army soldiers seemed frozen in place as well, not daring to move a muscle.

"The sword blocked the Demonic Sound Tide, and the Xuanhuang Sword Dao's recognition of me has deepened considerably."

The Holy Embryo clone of Li Fan was actually sensing the changes occurring within himself.

"As expected, the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao instinctively repels these [foreign objects]. If one aligns with the mandate of heaven, one will receive the Heavenly Dao's corresponding favor."

With these thoughts, Li Fan slowly stood and turned his head, looking at the countless cultivators inside the Eternal Immortal Fortress.

He smiled and asked: "Does the Lord Shang still think I am arrogant?"

The immortal city was deathly still.

A moment later, Shang Shaojun's figure reappeared before Li Fan.

He bowed slightly and said with sincere admiration: "I was wrong."

"Your talent, fellow Daoist, is unmatched in this world."

Just as he was about to say more, Li Fan waved his hand, cutting him off.

"So, Young Lord, are you willing to travel with me?"

Li Fan asked once more.

Seeing this, Shang Shaojun gritted his teeth and replied helplessly: "In that case, I shall risk my life to accompany this gentleman!"

With these words from the Shang Shaojun, an earth-shattering cheer erupted from within Broken Palm Immortal City.
